Description of worship of Krishna, rejecting all other worship, establishing Krsna as the most complete form, establishing Krishna’s eternal manifestations inside Vrindavana, establishing Krsna’s kaisora svarupa and description of bhakti are just some of the topic of this book.

# Kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśa \[BS\]

## Chapter 1 - Description of worship of Kṛṣṇa

*kalātta-māyālavakātta-mūrtiḥ*  
*kala-kvaṇad-veṇu-nināda-ramyaḥ \|*  
*śrito hṛdi vyākulayaṁs trilokīṁ*  
*śriye’stu gopī-jana-vallabho vaḥ \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May the lover of the gopīs, having a form endowed with beauty, enchantment and arts, who is attractive with the soft sounds of his flute, who is sheltered in the heart and agitates the three worlds, bestow auspiciousness to you.**

*guru-caraṇa-saroruha-dvayotthān*  
*mahita-rajaḥ-kanakān praṇamya mūrdhnā \|*  
*gaditam iha vivicya nāradādyair*  
*yajana-vidhiṁ kathayāmi śārṅgapāṇeḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects with my head to worshippable particles of dust arising from the lotus feet of guru. Deliberating on the words of Nārada and others, I will speak about the rules for worship of Kṛṣṇa.**

*kṣiti-sura-nṛpa-viṭ-turīya-jānāṁ*  
*muni-vana-vāsi-gṛhastha-varṇināṁ ca \|*  
*japa-huta-yajanādibhir manūnāṁ*  
*phalati hi kaścana kasyacit kathañcit \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Someone among brāhmaṇas, kṣatriyas, vaiśyas, śūdras, sages (sannyāsīs), vānaprasthas, householders and brahmacārīs may sometimes gain results by japa, yajña, worship, etc.**

*sarveṣu varṇeṣu tathāśrameṣu*  
*nārīṣu nānāhvaya-janmabheṣu \|*  
*dātā phalānām abhivāñchitānāṁ*  
*drāg eva gopālaka-mantra eṣaḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Gopāla-mantra quickly gives desired results for all varṇas and āśramas, women, and persons born under various birth constellations.**

*nūnam acyuta-kaṭākṣa-pātane*  
*kāraṇaṁ bhavati bhaktir añjasā \|*  
*tac catuṣṭaya-phalāptaye tato*  
*bhaktimān adhikṛto gurau harau \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti is the cause of the glance of the Lord. One who has bhakti for guru and the Lord is qualified for obtaining the four goals from the Lord.**

*snāto nirmala-dhauta-sūkṣma-vasano dhautāṅghri-pāṇy-ānanaḥ*  
*svācāntaḥ sapavitra-mudrita-karaḥ śvetordhva-puṇḍrojjvalaḥ \|*  
*prācī-dig-vadano nivadhya sudṛḍhaṁ padmāsanaṁ svastikaṁ*  
*vāsīnaḥ svagurūn gaṇādhipam atho vandeta baddhāñjaliḥ \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having bathed, dressing in pure, washed, fine cloth, washing the feet, hands and face, performing ācamana, holding kuśa in the hand, bright with white tilaka, facing east, sitting in padmāsana or svastikāsana, one should with folded hands offer respects to one’s gurus and Gaṇeśa.**

*athāstra-mantreṇa viśodhya pāṇī*  
*tritāla-dig-bandha-hutāśa-śālān \|*  
*vidhāya bhūtātmakam etad aṅgaṁ*  
*viśodhayec chuddha-matiḥ krameṇa \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Purifying the hands with the astra mantra, preparing a sacrificial arena by clapping the hands three times with dig-bandha1 , with pure mind, one should purify the limbs made of material elements.**

*iḍā-vaktre dhūmraṁ satata-gati-bījaṁ salavakaṁ*  
*smaret pūrvaṁ mantrī sakala-bhuvanocchoṣaṇa-karam \|*  
*svakaṁ dehaṁ tena pratata-vapuṣāpūrya sakalaṁ*  
*viśoṣya vyāmuñcet pavanam atha mārgeṇa khamaṇeḥ \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Remembering the vāyu-bīja with the bindu (yam?) of smoky color, which dries up the whole universe, while inhaling through the left nostril one should dry up one’s body which is filled with the pervading bīja and release the air on the path of the sun (right nostril). (Krama-dīpikā 1.1-8)**

*ity ādīni santi tatraiva jñātavyam \| kim etat saṅgraheṇa \| aho satyam etad uktaṁ, kintu tāny eva santi, taj-jñātāro na santi \| kecit śuṣka-tārkikā nyāya-vādinaḥ \| kecit sandigdha-manasaḥ \| kecit karma-mārgiṇaḥ \| kecid bauddha-pathāveśinaḥ \| kecin nānā-deva-parāyaṇāḥ \| yasya ye, te tasya guṇa-vādinaḥ \| kecit sarva-deva-māhātmya-sāmyaṁ vistārayanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**One should understand that such verses exist in Krama-dīpikā. What is the use of collecting these verses? That is truthfully stated. Though the statements exist but people who know them do not exist. Some are dry logicians, propounding Nyāya. Some are skeptics. Some follow the path of karma. Some are Buddhists. Some are dedicated to various devatās. Each praises the qualities of their philosophy. Some propagate that the glories of all devatās are equal.**

*tad eva—*  
*prācīnāny api tāni tāni bahuśaḥ śāstrāṇi vedyāni ca*  
*prāyas tatra samasta-deva-samatāṁ vakṣyanti te sūrayaḥ \|*  
*sarvātmā parameśvaro’khila-paraḥ kṛṣṇo na tair jñāyate*  
*tat tebhyo’tisudurlabhaṁ samanayaṁ kṣīrābdhi-pīyūṣavad \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**These learned men say that many ancient scriptures proclaim that all devatās are almost equal. They do not know that Kṛṣṇa is superior to them all, that he is the Supreme Lord. I have brought forward this extremely rare conclusion for them.**

*evaṁ teṣu teṣv api śāstrādiṣv apy asti \| tat tasya sarveśasya śrī-kṛṣṇasya*  
*tattva-viśeṣaḥ kaiścid api na jñāyate \| katham evam \| tasyaiveśvarasya*  
*māyayācchannās te nānātvaṁ paśyanti tad iti nānā-śāstrānusāreṇa*  
*yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**This fact is present even in their various scriptures. How is it that no one knows the excellent tattva of Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Lord?**  
**Covered by the Lord’s māyā, they see various other philosophies (in the scriptures). They interpret according to various scriptures.**

*śrutvā tac chruti-śāstra-tantra-nigamān dṛṣṭvā purāṇādikān*  
*tat saṁśritya satāṁ mataṁ ca satataṁ khyātaṁ nigūḍhaṁ param \|*  
*līlā-vigraha-dhāriṇo’pi paramānandasya kṛṣṇasya ca*  
*brahmāder api durlabhaṁ kila yaśaḥ saṅkīrtyate yatnataḥ \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the conclusions of śruti and tantras and seeing the Puraṇas and taking shelter of them, the conclusion of the devotees, always glorified, most confidential, the fame of Kṛṣṇa, full of the highest bliss, possessing a form for pastimes, rare for Brahmā and others, is glorified with attention.**

*atha kaiścid api tarka-vādibhiḥ purāṇoditaṁ nādriyate \|*  
*tatrāha bṛhannāradīye prathamādhyāye—*

**Перевод:**

**Some logicians do not respect what is presented in the Purāṇas. In reply, in Bṛhan-nāradīya Purāṇa it is said:**

*purāṇeṣv artha-vādatvaṁ ye vadanti narādhamāḥ \|*  
*tair arjitāni puṇyāni tadvad eva bhavanti vai \|\|11\|\|*

*samasta-karma-nirmūla-sādhanāni narādhamaḥ \|*  
*purāṇeṣv artha-vādena mṛto narakam aśnute \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The lowest of men who say that the Purāṇas make exaggerated statements, the puṇyas accrued by them become mere exaggerations and the very means of destroying all their karmas (i.e. yield no results). That person due to considering the Purāṇas as mere exaggerations, goes to hell on dying.**

**By accepting the Purāṇas as exaggeration, they destroy the results of all their karmas and go to hell on dying.**

*yāvad brahmā sṛjaty etaj jagat sthāvara-jaṅgamam \|*  
*tāvat sa pacyate pāpī narakāgniṣu santatam \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such a sinful person burns in the fire of hell continually for as long as Brahmā creates moving and non-moving beings.**

*yathā purāṇāni samastāni vedāṅgāni, tasmād vedānām anādareṇa evaṁ bhavaty eva, nānyathaiva;*

**Перевод:**

**Since all the Purāṇas are the Vedāṅgas, thus by disrespecting the Vedas they get such a result (of falling into hell) not otherwise.**

*yathā bṛhan-nāradīye—*

**Перевод:**

**Bṛhan-nāradīya Purāṇa says:**

*veda-vyāsas tu dharmātmā veda-śāstra-vibhāga-kṛt \|*  
*proktavān sarva-dharmāṇi purāṇeṣu mahīpate \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O King! Veda Vyāsa, who follows dharma, divided up the Vedas and spoke all dharmas in the Purāṇas.**

*tad evaṁ śruti-smṛty-ādi-samasta-dharma-śāstra-samuddiṣṭam*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇacaraṇa-bhajanam iti vidheyam \| ataḥ sarvopāsanam apāsya*  
*sarvopāsyaśrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇāravinda-śaraṇaṁ kartavyam iti śreyaḥ;*

**Перевод:**

**This is indicated in all dharma-śāstras such as the śrutis and smṛtis. They indicate that one should worship the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. Giving up all types of worship, one should surrender to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, the most worthy object of worship. This gives the highest benefit.**

*yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus it is said:**

*tasmād govinda-māhātmyam ānanda-rasa-sundaram \|*  
*śṛṇuyāt kīrtayen nityaṁ sa kṛtārtho na saṁśayaḥ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore the person who hears and glorifies constantly the greatness of Govinda, which is beautiful with the rasa of bliss, is successful without doubt.**

*tathā—*

**Перевод:**

**In the same way:**

*karmaṇā manasā vācā sarva-bhāvena cācyutam \|*  
*bhajanti parayā bhaktyā labhante padam avyayam \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who worship the Lord with pure devotion by actions, mind and words, with complete concentration, attain the indestructible abode.**

*śrī-bhāgavate parīkṣitaṁ prati śuka uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Śrīmad Bhāgavatam, Śukadeva Gosvāmī says to Parīkṣit:**

*tasmād bhārata sarvātmā bhagavān harir īśvaraḥ \|*  
*śrotavyaḥ kīrtitavyaś ca smartavyaś cecchatābhayam \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord who is Paramātmā, Bhagavān and the controller, should be heard about, glorified and remembered by those desiring freedom from fear. (SB** [**2.1.5**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/1.5)**)**

*tathā viṣṇu-dharmottare, bṛhan-nāradīye ca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Viṣṇu-dharmottara and Bṛhan-nāradīya Purāṇa it is said:**

*paramārtham aśeṣasya jagataḥ prabhavāpyayam \|*  
*śaraṇyaṁ śaraṇaṁ gacchan govindaḥ nāvasīdati \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Govinda is the supreme goal to be attained by all living entities, the creator and destroyer of the entire universe, and the real shelter of everyone. One who takes shelter of Lord Govinda never perishes but it always protected in all circumstances.**

*tathā brāhme—*

**Перевод:**

**In Brahmā Purāṇa, it is said:**

*kalpa-vṛkṣaṁ samāśritya phalāni svecchayā yathā \|*  
*gṛhṇāti puruṣo rājan tathā kṛṣṇān manorathān \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O King! Just as a person taking shelter of a desire tree attains all fruits (results) he desires, a person attains all his desires from Kṛṣṇa.**

*tathā brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**In Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.61**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.61)**), it is said:**

*dharmān anyān parityajya mām ekaṁ bhaja viśvasan \|*  
*yādṛśī yādṛśī śraddhā siddhir bhavati tādṛśī \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up all other practices, just worship me alone with faith. According to one’s faith, one attains perfection.**

*tathaikaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇābjaṁ bhajanīyam iti*  
*\| yathā atharvopaniṣadi gopāla-tāpanīye—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa alone are worshippable. This is explained in Gopāla-tāpanī Upaniṣad (**[**1.19-20**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/gopala-tapani/1.19)**) of the Atharva Veda:**

*eko vaśī sarvagaḥ kṛṣṇa īḍya*  
*eko’pi san bahudhā yo vibhāti \|*  
*taṁ pīṭha-sthaṁ ye nu bhajanti dhīrās*  
*teṣāṁ sukhaṁ śāśvataṁ netareṣām \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is supreme, all-pervading, and most worthy of praise. Though he is one, he appears as many. The happiness of the wise who worship him in the pīṭha is eternal but not of others.**

*nityo nityānāṁ cetanaś cetanānām*  
*eko bahūnāṁ yo vidadhāti kāmān \|*  
*taṁ pīṭhagaṁ ye’nubhajanti dhīrās*  
*teṣāṁ siddhiḥ śāśvatī netareṣām \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wise, who worship Kṛṣṇa in his pīṭha—Kṛṣna who is the principal being among many, who is the chief eternal among eternals and the chief knower among knowers, who satisfies their prema—their perfection is eternal but not of others.**

*iti jñātvā vilambo na kartavyaḥ \| yathā śrī-bhāgavate ekādaśa-skandhe*  
*navamādhyāye caturviṁśati-guru-prasaṅge brāhmaṇa uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Knowing this, one should not delay. Similarly in the Bhāgavatam, Eleventh Canto, ninth chapter concerning the twenty-four gurus, the brāhmaṇa says:**

*labdhvā su-durlabham idaṁ bahu-sambhavānte*  
*mānuṣyam artha-dam anityam apīha dhīraḥ \|*  
*tūrṇaṁ yateta na pated anu-mṛtyu yāvan*  
*niḥśreyasāya viṣayaḥ khalu sarvataḥ syāt \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having attained after many births the rare human body which is temporary but of great value, the intelligent person should immediately strive for spiritual life as long as the body lives, because the body dies after being born. Material enjoyment is available in all forms of life. (SB** [**11.9.29**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/9.29)**)**

*tathā, daitya-bālakān prati śrī-prahlāda-vacanam—*  
*Similarly, Prahlāda speaks to the young children of the demons:*  
*kaumāra ācaret prājño dharmān bhāgavatān iha \|*  
*durlabhaṁ mānuṣo dehas tad apy adhruvam arthadam \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wise person should practice bhakti-yoga in his life from youth. Human form is rare and transient but effective for bhakti. (SB** [**7.6.1**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-7/6.1)**)**

*yathā devakī-stutiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**As stated in Devakī’s prayers:**

*martyo mṛtyu-vyāla-bhītaḥ palāyan*  
*lokān sarvān nirbhayaṁ nādhyagacchat \|*  
*tvat-pādābjaṁ prāpya yadṛcchayādya*  
*susthaḥ śete mṛtyur asmād apaiti \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**No one in this material world, fearful of the snake of time, has become free from fear, even by fleeing to various planets. But now that you have appeared, death is fleeing from them, and the living entities, having obtained shelter at your lotus feet by your mercy, sleep peacefully. (SB** [**10.3.27**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/3.27)**)**

*mṛtyur iti sad-asat-karmaṇā yama-vaśo bhūtvā tat-tat-karma-phalaṁ*  
*bhuktvā punar jāyate iti garbha-vāsa-yātanā prāpyata iti mṛtyuḥ \| etad*  
*eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇa-parāyaṇānāṁ naivaṁ \| etad bhautikaṁ śarīraṁ te*  
*tyaktvā nitya-siddha-dehaṁ prāpya śrī-kṛṣṇa-dāsā bhavantīty amutreti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Controlled by Yamarāja due to one’s piety and sin, having experienced results of those karmas, one is born again. One suffers in the womb. This is the meaning of death in the verse. Those who surrender to Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet do not experience this. Giving up the material body, obtaining an eternal spiritual body they become servants of Kṛṣṇa in the spiritual world.**

*tasmāt śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇa-bhajanam eva kartavyam iti nānyat \| yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, the only duty is to worship the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa and nothing else:**

*yasyāvatāra-guṇa-karma-viḍambanāni*  
*nāmāni ye’su-vigame vivaśā gṛṇanti \|*  
*te’naika-janma-śamalaṁ sahasaiva hitvā*  
*saṁyānty apāvṛtāmṛtaṁ tam ajaṁ prapadye \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I surrender to the Lord without birth, whose names, invested with the powers of the Lord’s qualities and activities, chanted at the point of death even without attention, immediately destroy lifetimes of sins and allow men to achieve the uncovered, true form of the Lord. (SB** [**3.9.15**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/9.15)**)**

*yathā viṣṇu-purāṇe, nārasiṁhe ca yama uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Viṣṇu Purāṇa and Narasiṁha Purāṇa, Yama says:**

*aham amara-gaṇārcitena dhātrā*  
*yama iti loka-hitāya sanniyuktaḥ \|*  
*hari-guru-vimukhān praśāsya martyān*  
*hari-caraṇa-praṇatān namaskaromi \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am Yama, engaged by the Lord who is worshipped by the devatās, for the benefit of people. I punish those who are averse to the Lord and offer respects to those who respect the Lord’s feet.**

*tasmāt śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇa-bhajanaṁ kartavyam iti nānyat \| tathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, the only duty is to worship the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa and nothing else. Similarly it is said:**

*samāśritā ye pada-pallava-plavaṁ*  
*mahat-padaṁ puṇya-yaśo murāreḥ \|*  
*bhavāmbudhir vatsa-padaṁ paraṁ padaṁ*  
*padaṁ padaṁ yad vipadāṁ na teṣām \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For those who have accepted the boat of the lotus feet of the Lord, killer of Mura, the ocean of the material world is like the water contained in a calf’s hoof-print. Their goal is Vaikuṇṭha, the place of the eternal associates, not the place where there is danger at every step. (SB** [**10.14.58**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.58)**)**

*teṣāṁ paraṁ padaṁ bhavati tad iti śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇāravinda-*  
*paramānanda-rase paripūrṇo bhūtvā tiṣṭhati janma-mṛtyu-varjitaḥ \|*  
*yathā śrī-bhagavad-gītāsūpaniṣatsu arjunaṁ prati śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*kaunteya pratijānīhi na me bhaktaḥ praṇaśyati iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Attaining param padam means “becoming complete with the highest rasa at the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, they remain devoid of birth and death.” In Bhagavad Gītā, Kṛṣṇa says to Arjuna kaunteya pratijānīhi na me bhaktaḥ praṇaśyati: O Kaunteya! I promise that my devotee is never destroyed. (BG** [**9.31**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/9.31)**)**

*tathā kāśī-khaṇḍe śrī-dhruva-carite—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly, in Kāśī-khaṇḍa, in the story of Dhruva, it is said:**

*na cyavante hi mad-bhaktā mahatyāṁ pralayāpadi \|*  
*ato’cyuto’khile loke bhagavān parikīrtyate \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**My devotees do not fall into great danger. Thus Lord Acyuta is glorified in the whole world.**

*tathā daśame brahma-stutiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**In the Tenth Canto, in Brahmā’s prayers, it is said:**

*tathā na te mādhava tāvakāḥ kvacid*  
*bhraśyanti mārgāt tvayi baddha-sauhṛdāḥ \|*  
*tvayābhiguptā vicaranti nirbhayā*  
*vināyakānīkapa-mūrdhasu prabho \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Mādhava, Supreme Lord, Lord of the goddess of fortune, your devotees completely in love with you do not fall from the path of devotion, for you still protect them. Thus they fearlessly traverse the heads of their opponents and continue to progress in devotional service. (SB** [**10.2.33**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/2.33)**)**

*mahābhārate śrī-bhagavantaṁ praty uddhava uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava says to the Lord in Mahābhārata:**

*pratijñā tava govinda na me bhaktaḥ praṇaśyati \|*  
*iti saṁsmṛtya saṁsmṛtya prāṇān saṁdhārayāmy aham \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Continually remembering your promise that your devotee is never destroyed, I maintain my life. (Mahābhārata)**

*ity evaṁ bhagavad-bhaktānāṁ nāpada iti jñāpanīyam \|*  
*tataḥ sarvān anyān vihāya śrī-kṛṣṇa-candra-caraṇāravinda-bhajanam eva śreyaḥ ananya-bhāveneti \|*

**Перевод:**

**In this way it is stated that the devotees do not meet with danger. Thus giving up everything else, it is best to worship the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa with pure motivation.**

*etac chrī-hari-pāda-padma-bhajanoddeśojjvalaṁ nirmalaṁ*  
*vajraṁ ratnam idaṁ samasta-tanu-bhṛd-dhṛd-dhvānta-vidhvaṁsanam \|*  
*jñātvānena kuruṣva sādaratayā karṇāvataṁsaṁ sudhīr*  
*dhīrasyāpi nivedanaṁ śṛṇu mama śrī-rāghavasyāsakṛt \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This pure diamond shining with instructions to worship the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa destroys the darkness in the hearts of all embodied beings. The intelligent person should make this book his earring, after understanding it. Listen constantly to the proclamation of intelligent Rāghava.**

*iti śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśe śrī-kṛṣṇa-candra-caraṇa*  
*bhajanoddeśo nāma prathamaṁ ratnam*

**Перевод:**

**The first chapter called ‘Description of worship of Kṛṣṇa’ in Kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśa is completed.**

## Chapter 2 - Rejecting all other worship

*atha pravakṣye śrī-kṛṣṇa-pādāmbuja-niṣevaṇam \|*  
*samasta-śāstra-vihitaṁ sarveṣām uttamottamam \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will explain service to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa which is prescribed in all scriptures. That service is the highest.**

*atha ity ākarṇya kecid vadanti—aho evaṁ nānā-devatāḥ santi, nānā-tīrthāni santi, nānā-sat-karmādīni santi, sarveṣām uttamaṁ brahmopāsanam asti, eteṣām ekopāsanena śreyo bhavati \| kim anena?*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the word atha, some say, “There are many devatās, many tīrthas, and many sat-karmas. The best of these is worship of Brahman. One attains the best result by worship of one among these, what is the use of worshipping Kṛṣṇa?”**

*tatra śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇa-parāyaṇā vadanti—svarga-bhogino devā yad eva tat śrūyatāṁ te sarve naśvarāḥ kiṁ teṣām upāsanena ? yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Those surrendered to Kṛṣṇa say: Please hear about the devatās who enjoy in Svarga. They are all temporary. What is the use of worshipping them? As it is said:**

*tāvat pramodate svarge yāvat puṇyaṁ samāpyate \|*  
*kṣīṇa-puṇyaḥ pataty arvāg anicchan kāla-cālitaḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Until his pious results are used up, he enjoys life in Svarga. When the pious results are exhausted, however, he falls from Svarga against his will, by the force of time. (SB** [**11.10.26**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/10.26)**)**

*tathā śrī-gītāyāṁ śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*  
*In Bhagavad-gītā the Lord says:*  
*traividyā māṁ somapāḥ pūta-pāpā*  
*yajñair iṣṭvā svar-gatiṁ prārthayante \|*  
*te puṇyam āsādya surendra-lokam*  
*aśnanti divyān divi deva-bhogān \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some, knowing the three Vedas, worshipping me indirectly through sacrifice, drinking soma and becoming purified, pray for Svarga. Attaining puṇya in the form of the planet of Indra, they enjoy in heaven the pleasures of the devatās. (BG** [**9.20**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/9.20)**)**

*te taṁ bhuktvā svarga-lokaṁ viśālaṁ*  
*kṣīṇe puṇye martya-lokaṁ viśanti \|*  
*evaṁ trayī-dharmam anuprapannā*  
*gatāgataṁ kāma-kāmā labhante \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having enjoyed the vast pleasures of Svarga and exhausting their merit, they return to this planet. Following the rites of the three Vedas, desiring objects of enjoyment, they take repeated birth and death. (BG** [**9.21**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/9.21)**)**

*ato deva-sevanena kim ? tathā brahmādīnām apy evaṁ, kim anyeṣām ? yathā śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus what is the use of serving devatās? Such is the result of service even to Brahmā and others, what then to speak of service to others? The Lord says:**

*lokānāṁ loka-pālānāṁ mad-bhayaṁ kalpa-jīvinām \|*  
*brahmaṇo ’pi bhayaṁ matto dvi-parārdha-parāyuṣaḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The whole world and all of the great devatās who live for one thousand yuga cycles fear me. Even Brahmā, who possesses the supreme life span of 311,040,000,000,000 years, is afraid of me. (SB** [**11.10.30**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/10.30)**)**

*evaṁ nānā-tīrthāni, yathā bhāgavate śuka uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**This is so even for the various tīrthas. In Bhāgavatam, Śukadeva says:**

*vidyā-tapaḥ-prāṇa-nirodha-maitrī*  
*tīrthābhiṣeka-vrata-dāna-japyaiḥ \|*  
*nātyanta-śuddhiṁ labhate ’ntar-ātmā*  
*yathā hṛdi-sthe bhagavaty anante \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By one’s engaging in the processes of devatā worship, austerities, breath control, compassion, bathing in holy places, strict vows, charity and chanting of various mantras, one’s mind cannot attain the same absolute purification as that achieved by remembering the unlimited Lord in one’s heart. (SB** [**12.3.48**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-12/3.48)**)**

*tathā bṛhan-nāradīye—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus in Bṛhan-nāradīya Purāṇa it is said:**

*kiṁ vedaiḥ kim u vā śāstraiḥ kiṁ vā tīrthābhiṣecanaiḥ \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhakti-vihīnānāṁ kiṁ tapobhiḥ kim adhvaraiḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For persons devoid of kṛṣṇa-bhakti what is the use of the Vedas, other scriptures, bathing in tīrthas, austerities and yajñas?**

*atha nānā-dharma-kathā, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Regarding other dharmas, it is said:**

*dharmaḥ satya-dayopeto vidyā vā tapasānvitā \|*  
*mad-bhaktyāpetam ātmānaṁ na samyak prapunāti hi \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Neither dharma endowed with honesty and mercy nor knowledge obtained with great penance can completely purify one’s consciousness if they are bereft of loving service to me. (SB** [**11.14.22**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/14.22)**)**

*tathā śrī-śuka uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus Śukadeva says:**

*dāna-vrāta-tapo-homa-japa-svādhyāya-saṁyamaiḥ \|*  
*śreyobhir vividhaiś cānyaiḥ kṛṣṇe bhaktir hi sādhyate \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A taste for devotional service unto Kṛṣṇa is attained by charity, strict vows, austerities, fire sacrifices, japa, study of Vedic texts, controlling the senses and by the performance of many other auspicious practices. (SB** [**10.47.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/47.24)**)**

*tathā—*  
*dharmaḥ svanuṣṭhitaḥ puṁsāṁ viṣvaksena-kathāsu yaḥ \|*  
*notpādayed yadi ratiṁ śrama eva hi kevalam \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Varṇāśrama-dharma of the human being, which does not produce attraction for topics of the Lord, is only wasted effort. (SB** [**1.2.8**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/2.8)**)**

*ata eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇa-sevanaṁ vinā na kim api \| yathā brahmādayaḥ sarve naśvarāḥ, naśvaropāsanena naśvaro bhavatīti tad-uddeśena tapasyayā vā kim? svayaṁ naśvarā ye, te kiṁ śāśvatatvaṁ dāsyanti? nānā-dharma-karmaṇā vā kim? karma-bandhāya kalpate,*

**Перевод:**

**What is the use of anything without serving Kṛṣṇa’s feet? Just as Brahmā and others are temporary, one attains temporary results by worship of the temporary. Why perform austerities for them? Can those who are themselves temporary award eternal results? What is the use of various acts of dharma? They produce bondage of karma.**

*yathā mucukunda-stutiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**As stated in Mucukunda’s prayers:**

*na kāmaye ’nyaṁ tava pāda-sevanād*  
*akiñcana-prārthyatamād varaṁ vibho \|*  
*ārādhya kas tvāṁ hy apavarga-daṁ hare*  
*vṛṇīta āryo varam ātma-bandhanam \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O all-powerful one, I desire no boon other than service to your lotus feet, the boon most eagerly sought by those who reject liberation as insignificant. O Hari, what enlightened person who worships you, the giver of bhakti, would choose a boon that causes his own bondage? (SB** [**10.51.55**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/51.55)**)**

*tathaiva brahmopāsanena kim? brahmāpi śūnyam \| śūnyopāsanena śūnyatvaṁ prāpnoti \| yathā śrutiḥ—yādṛśī bhāvanā yasya siddhir bhavati tādṛśī iti śūnyopāsanena kim? nityākṣaya-paramānanda-sukha-svarūpa-śrī-kṛṣṇa-candra-caraṇāravinda-premāmṛta-madhu-pānena vañcitaḥ syāt \|*

**Перевод:**

**What is the use of worshipping Brahman? Brahman is also void. By worshipping the void, one becomes void. Śruti says:**

*yādṛśī bhāvanā yasya siddhir bhavati tādṛśī*

**Перевод:**

**According to the conception one attains the result. What is the use of worshipping the void? One is cheated of drinking the honey of prema from the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, whose svarūpa is the highest, eternal bliss.**

*atha mumukṣuṇoktam—naitat tadā kim ? yadi dehāder muktir bhavati, tadā kiṁ na bhūtam ? tat pratyuttaram eva, yathā—so’ham iti jñāna-niścayena nirvāṇa-muktir bhavati, tena kim ? muktiḥ kimiti ?*

**Перевод:**

**But those desiring liberation, object: What if it is not as you have described? If one becomes liberated from body, etc., then what will not have been attained (in fact one has attained everything)? The answer is this: by jñāna in which one identifies the self with Brahman, one attains nirvāṇa-mukti. What is the use of that liberation?**

*yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**As it is said:**

*bhaktir bhagavataḥ sevā muktis tat-pada-laṅghanam \|*  
*sa mūḍhaḥ sevakād anyo muktiṁ nirvāṇam icchati \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Serving the Lord is bhakti and liberation avoids this. The fool desires nirvāṇa-mukti rather than service. (Padyāvalī)**

*tathā vālmīkīye śrī-rāmacandraṁ prati hanūmatoktam—*

**Перевод:**

**Hanumān says to Rāmacandra in Vālmīkī-Rāmāyaṇa:**

*bhava-bandha-cchide tasyai spṛhayāmi na muktaye \|*  
*bhavān prabhur ahaṁ dāsa iti yatra vilupyate \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I do not desire liberation which cuts the bondage of material life, in which the concept that “You are the Lord and I am the servant” disappears.**

*tathā bhāvārtha-dīpikāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**In Bhāvārtha-dīpīkā it is said:**

*tvat-kathāmṛta-pāthodhau viharanto mahā-mudaḥ \|*  
*kurvanti kṛtinaḥ kecic catur-vargaṁ tṛṇopamam \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some clever people, most blissful, play in the sweet ocean of your topics and consider the puruṣārthas to be like grass. (Commentary to SB** [**10.87.21**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/87.21)**)**

*ata eva śrī-kṛṣṇe bhaktiḥ sādhyā, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti to Kṛṣṇa should be achieved. As it is said:**

*caturṣu puruṣārtheṣu gūḍho’yaṁ bhakti-saṁjñakaḥ \|*  
*dvijā eva hi jānanti munayo nāradādayaḥ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti is hidden in the four puruṣārthas. The brāhmaṇa sages like Nārada alone know this.**

*tathā mukter bhaktir garīyasī, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus bhakti is superior to liberation, as it is said:**

*animittā bhāgavatī bhaktiḥ siddher garīyasī \|*  
*jarayaty āśu yā kośaṁ nigīrṇam analo yathā \|\|16\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti to the Supreme Lord without material desires is superior to liberation. This bhakti quickly destroys the subtle body, just as the digestive fire consumes food. (SB** [**3.25.32-33**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/25.32)**)**

*tatra mukter bhaktiḥ sudurlabhā \| yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti is rarer than liberation, as it is said:**

*rājan patir gurur alaṁ bhavatāṁ yadūnāṁ*  
*daivaṁ priyaḥ kula-patiḥ kva ca kiṅkaro vaḥ \|*  
*asty evam aṅga bhagavān bhajatāṁ mukundo*  
*muktiṁ dadāti karhicit sma na bhakti-yogam \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O King Parīkṣit! Kṛṣṇa was the protector, instructor, object of worship, giver of affection, and the protector of both the Yadus and Pāṇḍavas. But he was sometimes your servant. The Lord gives liberation but not even bhāva-bhakti to those who worship him. (SB** [**5.6.18**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-5/6.18)**)**

*kintu sādhavo’pi muktiṁ na vāñchanti, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**But the devotees do not desire liberation:**

*na kiñcit sādhavo dhīrā bhaktā hy ekāntino mama \|*  
*vāñchanty api mayā dattaṁ kaivalyam apunar-bhavam \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**But intelligent devotees, completely dedicated to me alone, do not desire liberation even if I offer it to them. (SB** [**11.20.34**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.34)**)**

*athātra kecid ādhyātmikā vadanti—aho kṛṣṇaṁ yad vadasi \| sa eva śarīrī rūpavān paricchinnāvayavaś cākṣuṣyaḥ \| ataḥ sa eva bhautikaḥ \| bhautikatvāt sthūlaḥ \| sthūlatvān naśvaraḥ \| naśvaropāsanena kim iti \| ekaḥ kṛṣṇa eva upāsanīya iti yad uktaṁ tad atyantāsambhāvanīyopadeśaḥ \| eṣa vedānta-śāstrair anabhidheyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Some ādhyātmikas (those absorbed in ātmā) say: The Kṛṣṇa you speak of has a body. He has a form and has limited limbs and is visible to material eyes. Thus he is material. Since he is material, he is gross matter. Because he is gross matter, he is temporary. What is the use of worshipping that temporary form? Saying the Kṛṣṇa alone should be worshipped is an incomprehensible teaching. This is not mentioned in Vedānta scriptures.**

*yathā vāsiṣṭha-rāmāyaṇe śrī-rāmacandraṁ prati vasiṣṭhenoktam—*

**Перевод:**

**For instance, Vasiṣṭha speaks to Rāmacandra in VāsiṣṭhaRāmāyaṇa (Yoga-vāsiṣṭha):**

*yad idam dṛśyate sarvaṁ jagat sthāvara-jaṅgamam \|*  
*tat suṣuptāv iva svapnaḥ kalpānte pravinaśyati \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The world full of moving and non-moving beings which is seen, like a dream in sleep, is destroyed at the end of the kalpa.**

*tathoddhavaṁ prati śrī-bhagavān vāsudeva uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly Lord Vāsudeva said to Uddhava:**

*yad idaṁ manasā vācā cakṣurbhyāṁ śravaṇādibhiḥ \|*  
*naśvaraṁ gṛhyamāṇaṁ ca viddhi māyā-mano-mayam \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The material universe that you perceive through your mind, speech, eyes, ears and other senses is a temporary creation that is equivalent to what is made of māyā when you perceive in the waking state and to what is made of the mind when you perceive in the dream state. (SB** [**11.7.7**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/7.7)**)**

*ata eva sarvaṁ māyā-mayam iti matvā nityaṁ nirākāraṁ nirañjanaṁ*  
*nirlepaṁ ca brahmopāsyam iti tathaivoktaṁ vāsiṣṭhe vasiṣṭhena—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus understanding that everything is illusion, one should worship Brahman, which is eternal, without form, pure, and uncontaminated. This is stated by Vasiṣṭha in Vāsiṣṭha-Rāmāyaṇa:**

*āste’nastamito bhāsvān yo devo hi nirāmayaḥ \|*  
*sarvadā sarva-kṛt sarvaḥ paramātmā maheśvaraḥ \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Supreme ātmā, the Supreme Lord, is indestructible, selfmanifesting, pure, always accomplished.**

*tathā śruter vākya-vṛttau—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in the Vākya-vṛtti (11), it is said:**

*antaḥ-karaṇa-tad-vṛtti-sākṣī caitanya-vigrahaḥ \|*  
*ānanda-rūpaḥ satyaḥ san kiṁ nātmānaṁ prapadyase \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Being the form of conscious, the witness of functions of the antaḥkaraṇa, blissful and eternal, why do you not take pleasure in yourself?**

*tathāṣṭāvakra-saṁhitāyām—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly, in the Aṣṭāvakra-saṁhitā it is said:**

*aho nirañjanaḥ śānto bodho’yaṁ prakṛteḥ paraḥ \|*  
*etāvantam ahaṁ kālaṁ hā mohena viḍambitaḥ \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am pure, peaceful, conscious, beyond prakṛti. For some time, I am under illusion.**

*tatra kārṣṇā vadanti—ya eva rūpa-guṇa-varjitaḥ \| acalas tv akartā \| kaya-mano-vākyair agrāhyaḥ sa eva na kiñcit \| tatra brahma-jñānī vadati evaṁ neti \|*

**Перевод:**

**For this the devotees of Kṛṣṇa reply: that which is devoid of form and qualities, which is immovable and inactive, unperceived by body, mind and words is insignificant. For that the brahma-jñānī says: That is not so.**

*yathā śrutau—*

**Перевод:**

**As stated in the śrutis:**

*apāṇi-pādo javano grahītā*  
*paśyaty acakṣuḥ sa śṛṇoty akarṇaḥ \|*  
*sa vetti vedyaṁ na ca tasyāsti vettā*  
*tam āhur agryaṁ puruṣaṁ mahāntam \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Without hands and feet, he moves quickly. Without eyes he sees. Without ears he hears. He knows whatever is to be known. No one knows him. The sages say that he is the original cause, the mahāpuruṣa. (Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad)**

*tatra bhāgavatā vadanti—aho vaicitryam \| so’stīti paṇḍitā vadanti \| tasya vettā nāstīty api vadanti ca \| ata eva asti nāstīti sandehaḥ \| yatra sandehas tasyānveṣaṇena kim iti ?*

**Перевод:**

**In response, the devotees say: This is astonishing. The paṇḍitas say, ‘He exists.’ They also say, ‘No one can know him.’ Thus there is a doubt whether he exists or not. Thus, what is the use of trying to find him, with such doubt?**

*tato brahma-vādī vadati—aho asti nāstīti sandeho jñāna-rahitānām, astīti niḥsandehaḥ \| tat śrūyatāṁ yathā śrutau—*

**Перевод:**

**The brahma-vādī says: For those without jñāna, there is doubt whether he exists or not. He exists without doubt according to śruti:**

*ghṛtam iva payasi nigūḍhaṁ bhūte bhūte vasati ca vijñānam \|*  
*satataṁ manthayitavyaṁ manthana-bhūte prakāśate ātmā \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Like ghee hidden in milk, consciousness is present in every living entity. It should be churned constantly. When churned, the ātmā (consciousness) reveals. (Brahma-bindūpaniṣad)**

*dṛśyaś ca—*

**Перевод:**

**Brahman can be seen:**

*rāhur adṛśyo’pi yathā śaśi-bimba-sthaḥ prakāśate jagati \|*  
*tathā sarva-gato’pi ātmā buddhi-stho dṛśyatām eti \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though Rāhu is invisible, it becomes visible in the world, situated on the disc of the moon. Similarly, though ātmā is all-pervading, it becomes visible, situated in intelligence. (Paramārtha-sāra 18)**

*kartā ca—*

**Перевод:**

**Brahman is also the doer:**

*savitari udite yadvat karoti karmāṇi loko’yam \|*  
*na ca tāni karoti ravir na kārayati vā tadvad ātmā \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the sun rises people perform activities, but the sun does not do those activities nor causes others to do. The ātmā, is like the sun (i.e. it does not do the activities nor cause others to do). (Paramārtha-sāra 13)**

*tathā hastāmalake —*

**Перевод:**

**In Hastāmalaka-stotram it is said:**

*nimittaṁ manaś-cakṣur-ādi-pravṛttau*  
*nirastākhilopādhir ākāśa-kalpaḥ \|*  
*ravir loka-ceṣṭā-nimittaṁ yathā yaḥ*  
*sa nityopalabdhi-svarūpo’ham ātmā \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am the ātmā, the eternal svarūpa of awareness, devoid of all upādhis, like the sky and the cause of the functioning of mind, and senses, just as the sun is the cause of people’s activities.**

*tat prāptāv upāyo, yathā śrutau cit-prakāśe—*

**Перевод:**

**The method for that attainment is described in śruti:**

*agaman me mano’nyatra sāmprataṁ ca sthirī-kṛtam \|*  
*evaṁ yo vetti dhī-vṛttiṁ so’ham ity avadhārayet \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**My mind went elsewhere. Now it is fixed. One who understands the functions of intelligence can understand “I am Brahman.”**

*evam ātmā jñāna-dṛṣṭyā dṛśyate prāpyate ca vāsiṣṭhe—*

**Перевод:**

**In this way, the ātmā can be seen and attained by vision of jñāna. Vāsiṣṭha-rāmāyaṇa says:**

*mṛgair yathā mṛgāṇāṁ ca gajānāṁ ca gajair yathā \|*  
*pakṣiṇāṁ pakṣibhir yadvat jñeyaṁ jñānena gṛhyate \|\|30\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**As animals are understood by animals, elephants by elephants and birds by birds, one should understand what is to be known by knowledge.**

*tad evaṁ jñānena jñāyate, ajñānānāṁ na kim api \| athaitat śrutvā śrīkṛṣṇa-candrasya caraṇa-parāyaṇā vadanti— bhavatā yad uktaṁ tat kim ? śrī-kṛṣṇa-candrasya caraṇa-vaibhavaṁ ye na jānanti, ta eva evaṁ vadanti \| te’tikṣudrāḥ kṣudra-matayaḥ sūkṣmaṁ na paśyanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**In this way the ātmā is understood by knowledge. Those who are devoid of knowledge cannot realize it. Hearing this, the devotees of Kṛṣṇa say: What are you saying? Those who do not know the power of Kṛṣṇa’s feet alone speak in this way. Those who have meager intelligence cannot see the subtle.**

*tatra—*

**Перевод:**

**In this regard it is said:**

*ye kṛṣṇa-caraṇāmbhoja-makaranda-madhu-vratāḥ \|*  
*na bhavanti paraṁ kṣubdhās te nānā-patha-gāminaḥ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are not bees tasting the honey of Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet are disturbed and follow various paths.**

*yathā brahmādi-stutiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā and others pray:**

*tvam eka evāsya sataḥ prasūtis*  
*tvaṁ sannidhānaṁ tvam anugrahaś ca \|*  
*tvan-māyayā saṁvṛta-cetasas tvāṁ*  
*paśyanti nānā na vipaścito ye \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You are the one cause of this material existence. You are also its destruction and maintenance. The wise, uncovered by māyā, do not see varieties but only you. (SB** [**10.2.28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/2.28)**)**

*ataḥ sarvopari śrī-kṛṣṇa-candra eka eva jñānibhir jñāyate \| yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Only Kṛṣṇa, the supreme being, should be known by the jñānīs, as it is said;**

*kṛṣṇasyopari kaścid vā tulyo bhinno’sti yo vadet \|*  
*saḥ tasya māyayācchano nālapet taṁ kadācana \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who says that there is someone equal or superior to Kṛṣṇa, is covered by the Lord’s māyā, and one should never speak to him.**

*ity evaṁ yat kiñcit sarvaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-vaibhavam iti mantavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, one should consider that everything is a manifestation of Kṛṣṇa.**

*atha bhagavataḥ śrī-kṛṣṇasya bhautikaṁ prākṛtaṁ sa-guṇaṁ sthūlaṁ deham iti yad ajñānād uktaṁ tad iti śrūyatām \| yathā saṁmohana-tantre prathama-paṭale nāradaṁ prati sanaka uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Out of ignorance people say that Kṛṣṇa has a material gross body made of the guṇas and elements. Thus Sanaka speaks to Nārada in Sammohana-tantra, first chapter:**

*tad ānandamayī rādhā tad-ānanda-mayo hariḥ \|*  
*na bhautiko deha-bandhas tayor ānanda-rūpayoḥ \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The forms of bliss of Rādhā, full of bliss, and Kṛṣṇa, full of bliss, are not material, bound to material bodies.**

*yathā vatsa-haraṇe brahma-stutiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**In stealing the calves, Brahmā says:**

*asyāpi deva vapuṣo mad-anugrahasya*  
*svecchā-mayasya na tu bhūta-mayasya ko’pi \|*  
*neśe mahi tv avasituṁ manasāntareṇa*  
*sākṣāt tavaiva kim utātma-sukhānubhūteḥ \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even by controlling the mind, no one can understand the greatness of this form of the Lord of the universe, which manifested in many four-armed forms, but which is actually you, the one form of Svayam Bhagavān, who gave me mercy by your own will, who spread through the universe in many spiritual forms and possess bliss which can be understood only by yourself. (SB** [**10.14.2**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.2)**)**

*kintu śrī-kṛṣṇasya vigrahaṁ bhautikaṁ yo vadati, tatrāha bṛhad-vaiṣṇave—*

**Перевод:**

**But concerning those who say Kṛṣṇa has a material form, Bṛhad-viṣṇu Purāṇa says:**

*yo vetti bhautikaṁ dehaṁ kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*sa sarvasmād bahiṣkāryaḥ śrauta-smārta-vidhānataḥ \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who considers that Kṛṣṇa’s body is material should be excluded from all ceremonies which follow śruti and smṛti.**

*mukhaṁ tasyāvalokyātha sa celo jalam āviśet \|*  
*paśyet sūryaṁ spṛśed vāri ghṛtaṁ prāśyo viśuddhati \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If one sees his face one should enter water with clothing on. One should look at the sun, perform ācamāna and purify oneself by sipping ghee.**

*sthūlatvaṁ yad uktaṁ tad iti yathā mahā-kaurme—*

**Перевод:**

**Regarding calling the Lord’s body material, it is described as follows in Mahā-kūrma Purāṇa:**

*asthūlaś cānaṇuś caiva sthūlo’ṇuś caiva sarvataḥ \|*  
*avarṇaḥ sarvataḥ proktaḥ savarṇaś ca prakīrtitaḥ \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is not material, and not small. He is material in appearance and small in appearance. He has no color but is glorified as having color.**

*tathā śukoktiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva says:**

*amunī bhagavad-rūpe*  
*mayā te hy anuvarṇite*  
*ubhe api na gṛhṇanti*  
*māyā-sṛṣṭe vipaścitaḥ \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wise do not accept these two forms of the Lord described by me since they are composed of matter. (SB** [**2.10.35**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/10.35)**)**

*ataḥ sthūla-rūpaṁ yat tad bhagavan-māyayā sṛṣṭam \| etayoḥ paraṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ \| yathā govinda-vṛndāvane bhagavati śrī-kṛṣṇe balarāma-praśne—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, the material forms are created by the Lord’s māyā. Kṛṣṇa is different from these gross and subtle forms of matter. As stated in Govinda-vṛndāvana, when Balarāma questions Kṛṣṇa:**

*paramātmā paraṁ brahma sac-cid-ānanda-vigrahaḥ \|*  
*śabda-brahma-mayaḥ sākṣāt svayaṁ prakṛtir īśvaraḥ \|*  
*ādy-anta-rahitaḥ sūkṣma-sthūlātītaḥ parātparaḥ \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is Paramātma, Parabrahman, a form of eternity, consciousness and bliss, directly the Vedas. He is prakṛti and is the Lord. He is devoid of beginning and end, is beyond the subtle and gross and is greater than the greatest.**

*atha paraṁ brahmopāsanam iti yad uktaṁ tad brahma yat tat śrūyatām \| yathā varāha-saṁhitāyāṁ (2.53,55) śrī-bhagavān varāha uvāca (śrīkṛṣṇa-candrasya yathā rūpam)—*

**Перевод:**

**Some say that the Supreme Brahman should be worshipped.One should hear about that Brahman. In Varāha-saṁhitā, Lord Varāha says:**

*dhvaja-vajrāṅkuśāmbhoja-karāṅghri-tala-śobhitam \|*  
*nakhendu-kiraṇa-śreṇī pūrṇa-brahmaika-kāraṇam \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His hands and feet are decorated with the flag, thunderbolt, goad and lotus. Rays emanating from his toe nails are the cause of Brahman.**

*kecid vadanti tad-raśmi brahma cid-rūpam avyayam \|*  
*tad-aṁśāṁśaṁ mahā-viṣṇuṁ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some say that Brahman is the effulgence of his indestructible spiritual form. The wise say that Mahā-viṣṇu is an aṁśa of his aṁśa.**

*tathā śrī-kṛṣṇa-yāmale dvādaśādhika-śatatama-paṭale śrī-vāsudevaṁ prati tripurovāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Kṛṣṇa-yāmala, Chapter 112, Tripurā speaks to Vāsudeva:**

*sūcanāt sūtram ity āhuḥ kṛṣṇānubhava-sūcakam \|*  
*jyotir vṛndāvanātmakaṁ khyātaṁ brahmeti jagad ujjvalam \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because it gives an indication, it is called sūtra. Brahman gives an indication of Kṛṣṇa. The light which is composed of Vṛndāvana is known as Brahman. It illuminates the universe.**

*tad brahma kṛṣṇa-kiraṇam yato bhāti carācaram \|*  
*yasya bhāsā bhāti viśvaṁ yathārthaṁ śrutayo jaguḥ \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That Brahman is the effulgence of Kṛṣṇa by which all beings are illuminated. By his light the universe is illuminated. The śrutisglorify the Lord in this way.**

*ata eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-pādābja-lābhe’pi sarvaṁ prāptam \| kaścid avaśeṣo’sti naivam \| yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus one attains everything by attaining Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. There remains nothing else to be gained. As stated in:**

*vṛkṣa-lābhe na vṛkṣasya kiñcid bhavati durlabham \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-pādābja-lābhe’pi durlabhaṁ nāsti kiñcana \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On obtaining a tree, nothing of the tree remains unobtained. On attaining Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet, there is nothing left to be attained.**

*yā nirvṛtis tanu-bhṛtāṁ tava pāda-padma-*  
*dhyānād bhavaj-jana-kathā-śravaṇena vā syāt \|*  
*sā brahmaṇi sva-mahimany api nātha mā bhūt*  
*kiṁ tv antakāsi-lulitāt patatāṁ vimānāt \|\|46\|\| iti*

**Перевод:**

**The bliss for your servant available from meditating on your lotus feet or from hearing about your pastimes from the devotees is not available in your form of greatness known as Brahman, what to speak of the happiness for those who fall from the pleasures of Svarga. (SB** [**4.9.10**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/9.10)**)**

*ato yadi śrī-kṛṣṇa-padārāvindaṁ prāptaṁ, tadā sarvaṁ prāptam eva \| kintu śrī-kṛṣṇa-pādāmbujaṁ vinā nānyatra siddhiḥ, yathā—naiṣkarmyam apy acyuta-bhāva-varjitaṁ na śobhate jñānam alaṁ nirañjanam ity ādi \| tathā trailokya-saṁmohana-tantre brahma-vidyopāsanīyaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-pāda-padmaṁ, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**If one has attained Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet then one has attained everything. Without attaining his feet, nothing is accomplished as explained in naiṣkarmyam apy acyuta-bhāva-varjitaṁ na śobhate jñānam alaṁ nirañjanam: Even the stage of jñāna without the bondage of karma is not glorious if it is devoid of bhakti to the Supreme Lord. What is the use of having destroyed ignorance? (SB** [**1.5.12**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/5.12)**) Similarly in the Sammohana-tantra, it is said that Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet should be worshipped by brahma-vidyā:**

*brahma-vādī muniḥ kaścid jābālir iti viśrutaḥ \|*  
*so’dhyātma-nirato yogī vicaran pṛthivīm imām \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There was a brahma-vādī sage named Jābāli. Absorbed in adhyātmā, he wandered about the earth.**

*apaśyat tāpasīṁ kāñcic carantīṁ dāruṇaṁ tapaḥ \|*  
*tāruṇya-vayasā yuktāṁ rūpeṇāti-manoharām \|\|48\|\|*  
*candrāṁśu-sadṛśābhāsāṁ sarvāvayava-śobhanām \|*  
*kṛtvā kaṭi-taṭe caiva kṛṣṇājina-sukomalām \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He saw an extremely beautiful young female ascetic performing severe austerities. Her complexion was like moonlight and she was beautiful in all limbs. She wore a deer skin on her hips.**

*jñāna-mudrāṁ ca bibhrāṇām animiṣāyatekṣaṇām \|*  
*tyaktāhāra-vihāraṁ ca munir niścalatāṁ sthitām \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She was showing the jñāna-mūdra and had unblinking eyes. She had given up eating. She was silent and motionless.**

*jijñāsus tāṁ muni-varas tasthau tatra śataṁ samāḥ \|*  
*tatas tv evaṁ samutthāya muninā prārthitā ca sā \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Inquisitive about her the sage remained there for a hundred years. When she rose, he inquired from her.**

*ato’haṁ jñātum icchāmi tapasaḥ kāraṇaṁ tava \|*  
*yadi yogyaṁ bhavet tarhi kṛpayā vaktum arhasi \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I want to know the cause of your austerity. If it is proper, please mercifully tell me.**

*athābravīc chanair bālā tapasā tīvra-karśitā \|*  
*brahma-vidyāham atulā yā yogīndrair vimṛgyate \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The young woman, thin from austerities, spoke slowly. I am incomparable in brahma-vidyā, who am sought by the best of yogīs.**

*jitendriyā jitāhārā kāmyayā duścaraṁ tapaḥ \|*  
*carāmy ahaṁ vane ghore dhyāyantī puruṣottamam \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Controlling the senses, controlling eating, by my own desire, I have performed difficult austerities in this fearful forest, meditating on the Supreme Lord.**

*brahmānandena pūrṇāhaṁ jñāna-vijñāna-tṛpta-dhīḥ \|*  
*tathāpi śūnyam ātmānaṁ manye kṛṣṇa-ratiṁ vinā \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am filled with the bliss of Brahman and my intelligence is satisfied with jñāna and vijñāna. Still I consider myself empty without attraction to Kṛṣṇa.**

*idānīm atinirviṇṇā dehasyāsya visarjanam \|*  
*kartuṁ gacchāmi puṇyāyāṁ vāpikāyām ihaiva tu \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Now very depressed, I have come to give up my body at this pious lake.**

*tac chrutvā vacanaṁ tasyā munir atyanta-vismitaḥ \|*  
*patitvā caraṇe tasyāḥ kṛṣṇopāsā-vidhiṁ śubham \|\|57\|\|*  
*papraccha parama-prītas tyaktvādhyātma-vivecanam \|*  
*tayoktaṁ mantrām ādāya jagāma mānasaṁ saraḥ \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing her words, the sage was most astonished. Falling at her feet, he asked the auspicious method of worshipping Kṛṣṇa. Most delighted, he gave up deliberation on adhyātmā, accepted the mantra spoken by her and went to Mānasa-sarovara.**

*sa evaṁ bahu-deheṣu samupāsya jagat-patim \|*  
*nava-kalpāntare jātā gokule divya-rūpiṇī \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In many bodies he worshipped the Lord of the universe. He was born in Gokula with a spiritual form of a gopī in another kalpa.**

*ata eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-pāda-padma-sevanaṁ vinā brahmopāsanaṁ kim iti \| tathā brahmādi-stutiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, without serving the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, worship of Brahman is useless. Similarly Brahmā and others pray as follows:**

*ye’nye’ravindākṣa vimukta-māninas*  
*tvayy asta-bhāvād aviśuddha-buddhayaḥ \|*  
*āruhya kṛcchreṇa paraṁ padaṁ tataḥ*  
*patanty adho’nādṛta-yuṣmad-aṅghrayaḥ \|\|60\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O lotus-eyed Lord, although nondevotees, having impure intelligence because of not having devotion to you, think that they are liberated and become jīvanmuktas after severe austerities and penances, they fall down from their position of imagined superiority because they have no regard for the lotus feet of you and your devotees. (SB** [**10.2.32**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/2.32)**)**

*tathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly:**

*śreyaḥ-sṛtiṁ bhaktim udasya te vibho*  
*kliśyanti ye kevela-bodha-labdhaye \|*  
*teṣām asau kleśala eva śiṣyate*  
*nānyad yathā sthūla-tuṣāvaghātinām \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! If fools give up bhakti, the all-inclusive path, and suffer to attain realization of ātmā without bhakti, they simply attain suffering and nothing else. They are like fools who beat empty husks. (SB** [**10.14.4**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.4)**)**

*ataḥ sarvaṁ vihāya śrī-rādhā-kānta-caraṇa-sevanaṁ kartavyaṁ nānyat śāśvatam iti śeṣaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus giving up all else one must serve the feet of Kṛṣṇa, the lover of Rādhā. Nothing else is permanent.**

*athātra mumukṣavo vadanti—śrī-kṛṣṇa eva brahma paraḥ pūrṇānandasvarūpaḥ nityo nityaprakāśo līlā-vigrahaḥ iti yad uktaṁ tasya kiṁ rūpaṁ ? kiṁ pramāṇaṁ ? kiṁ vā guṇaḥ ? kim asya prabhāvaḥ ? kiṁ vā sthānaṁ ? tad ucyatām \| tataḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇa-parāyaṇā vadanti — aho ! ajñānād evaṁ vadatha \| asyānanta-mahimno rūpa-guṇādīn vaktuṁ ke samarthāḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Now persons desiring liberation say, “You have said that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Brahman, having a svarūpa of complete bliss, eternal, with eternal manifestations and a form for performing pastimes. What is that form? What is the proof? What are his qualities? What are his powers? What is his place? That should be explained.” The devotees of Kṛṣṇa say: You speak out of ignorance. Who can describe the form, qualities, etc., of Kṛṣṇa who has unlimited glories?**

*yathā brahma-stutiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**As stated by Brahmā in his prayers:**

*guṇātmanas te ’pi guṇān vimātuṁ*  
*hitāvatīrṇasya ka īśire ’sya \|*  
*kālena yair vā vimitāḥ sukalpair*  
*bhū-pāṁśavaḥ khe mihikā dyubhāsaḥ \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In time, skilled people might be able to count all the atoms of the earth, the particles of snow, or perhaps even the photons in the sky. But who could possibly count the unlimited transcendental qualities possessed by you, the vitalizer of all qualities, who have descended to display those qualities for the benefit of all living entities? (SB** [**10.14.7**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.7)**)**

*tathā ekādaśa-skandhe—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in the Eleventh Canto it is said:**

*yo vā anantasya guṇān anantān*  
*anukramiṣyan sa tu bāla-buddhiḥ \|*  
*rajāṁsi bhūmer gaṇayet kathañcit*  
*kālena naivākhila-śakti-dhāmnaḥ \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Any fool wanting to enumerate fully the unlimited qualities of the unlimited Supreme Lord may be able after some time to count the particles of dust on the earth, but can never count the qualities of the Lord who is the abode of all powers. (SB** [**11.4.2**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/4.2)**)**

*tathā—*  
*jānanta eva jānantu kiṁ bahūktyā na me prabho \|*  
*manaso vapuṣo vāco vaibhavaṁ tava gocaraḥ \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Let the people who think they know, think in that way! What more can be said, O Lord! Your powers are not accessible by my mind, body or words. (SB** [**10.14.38**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.38)**)**

*yatra brahmaivam, tatrānye ke varākāḥ ? tasminn aham api kiṁ kṣudrātikṣudraḥ ? tad eva—*

**Перевод:**

**Where Brahmā himself falls short, what can other insignificant creatures do? Am I not a most insignificant being compared in this regard?**

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-candra-caraṇābja-guṇa-pravāhaṁ*  
*vaktuṁ yathā druhiṇa-vidruta-buddhi-śaktiḥ \|*  
*tasmin mamābhilaṣitā matir alpakasya*  
*bālo yathā vidhum abhīpsati khelanārtham \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When Brahmā’s power of intelligence was confused to speak about the wave of qualities from the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, then my mind which desires to do this is like a small child desiring to play with the moon.**

*tad eva, yad avadhi mamāvagatā tad iti śāstrānusāreṇocyate—tatrādau athārvopaniṣadi gopāla-tāpanīye*

**Перевод:**

**Whatever I have understood is described according to the scriptures. In this regard, in Gopāla-tāpanī Upaniṣad it is said:**

*oṁ namo viśvarūpāya viśva-sthity-anta-hetave \|*  
*viśveśvarāya viśvāya govindāya namo namaḥ \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to Govinda the form of the universe, the cause of maintenance and destruction of the universe, the Lord of the universe, non-different from the universe.**

*namo vijñāna-rūpāya paramānanda-rūpiṇe \|*  
*kṛṣṇāya gopīnāthāya govindāya namo namaḥ \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to Govinda, Kṛṣṇa, master of the gopīs, the form of realization, the form of supreme bliss.**

*niṣkalāya vimohāya śuddhāyāśuddha-vairiṇe \|*  
*advitīyāya mahate śrī-kṛṣṇāya namo namaḥ \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to Kṛṣṇa, who is beyond māyā, who is devoid of illusion, who is pure, who is the killer of the demons, who is without equal, and who is therefore great.**

*tathā brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ brahma-stutiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly, in Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.33**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.33)**), Brahmā prays:**

*advaitam acyutam anādim ananta-rūpam*  
*ādyaṁ purāṇa-puruṣaṁ nava-yauvanaṁ ca \|*  
*vedeṣu durlabham adurlabham ātma-bhaktau*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda who has no equal, who is infallible in protecting his devotees, who is the source of all others but expands into infinite forms, who is topmost, who exists before all others but is eternally youthful, and who is rarely attained by those studying the Vedas but attained easily by his devotee.**

*tathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly:**

*idaṁ hi puṁsas tapasaḥ śrutasya vā*  
*sviṣṭasya sūktasya ca buddha-dattayoḥ \|*  
*avicyuto ’rthaḥ kavibhir nirūpito*  
*yad-uttamaśloka-guṇānuvarṇanam \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The unfailing goal of men’s austerity, study of the Vedas, performance of sacrifices, chanting of mantras, practicing jñānaand giving charity is defined by the wise as glorifying the qualities of the Lord. (SB** [**1.5.22**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/5.22)**)**

*ata eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-candra-caraṇa-bhajanam eva kartavyam iti nānyat \|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, one must worship the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. There is no other way**

*dṛṣṭvā śrutvāvagamyātha purāṇādau tu sarvataḥ \|*  
*paramānanda-sandoha-kṛṣṇa-pādāmbujaṁ bhaja \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having seen, heard and understood in Purāṇas and other scriptures, worship with all effort the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa endowed with the highest bliss.**

*śrīmat-kṛṣṇa-pādāravinda-yugale bhaktir vidheyā sadā*  
*nānopāsana-varjanābhidham idaṁ ratnaṁ paraṁ mauktikam*  
*kaṇṭhasyābharaṇaṁ kuruṣva satataṁ hy anyābhilāṣaṁ tyaja*  
*sāraṁ śrī-kavirāja-rāghava-vacaḥ sānandam ākarṇaya \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should always perform bhakti to the two lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. Accept this pearl gem (chapter) called “Rejecting all other worship,” as a neck ornament. Give up other desires at all times. Blissfully listen to the sweet words of Rāghava Kavirāja.**

*iti śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśe nānopāsana-varjanaṁ nāma dvitīyaṁ ratnam \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The second chapter called ‘Rejecting all other worship’ in Kṛṣṇabhakti-ratna-prakāśa is completed.**

## Chapter 3 - Establishing Kṛṣṇa as the most complete form

*pūrṇaṁ sarva-paraṁ vakṣye kṛṣṇam ānanda-vigraham \|*  
*nitya-vṛndāvanāsīnaṁ rādhikā-prāṇa-vallabham \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will speak of Kṛṣṇa, the complete being, the highest among all, the form of bliss, who resides eternally in Vṛndāvana and the beloved of Rādhā.**

*atha śrī-bhagavad-bhāvakā vadanti—aho yac chrī-kṛṣṇasya guṇānuvādaṁ śrotuṁ praśnaṁ kṛtavanto bhavantaḥ, tad eva sarvopari Nityānanda-maya-paramātma-svarūpaśrī-kṛṣṇa-candrasya līlā-vigrahasya rūpa-guṇa-prakāśaṁ nānā-śāstrānusāreṇāhaṁ vivṛṇomi \| tadeva sāvadhānaṁ śrūyatām \| samyag brahmādibhir na jñāyate yat \|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees of Kṛṣṇa speak: You were asking questions in order to hear about the qualities of Kṛṣṇa. According to various scriptures, I will describe the manifestation of the form and qualities of Kṛṣṇa endowed with playful form, the svarūpa of Paramātmā endowed with eternal bliss. It should be carefully listened to. This is not understood completely by Brahmā and others.**

*yathā brahma-saṁhitāyām—*

**Перевод:**

**As stated in Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.1**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.1)**):**

*īśvaraḥ paramaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ saccidānanda-vigrahaḥ \|*  
*anādir ādir govindaḥ sarva-kāraṇa-kāraṇam \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is the supreme controller. He is the enjoyer of innumerable consorts, and the possessor of an eternal form of consciousness and bliss. Though, he is without origin, he is the source of all other forms of God, and is the cause of all causes. He is Govinda, the boy who cares for the cows.**

*panthās tu koṭi-śata-vatsara-sampragamyo*  
*vāyor athāpi manaso muni-puṅgavānām \|*  
*so ’py asti yat prapada-sīmny avicintya-tattve*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda, whose toes alone, possessing inconceivable powers, are attained only after a billion years by multitudes of sages who discipline the mind and breath.Brahma-saṁhitā** [**5.34**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.34)**)**

*param—*

**Перевод:**

**It is also said elsewhere:**

*kṛṣṇo yaḥ kathyate vedaiḥ pūrṇaḥ sarveśvaraḥ pumān \|*  
*sa eva nikhilādhāro nirguṇaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa, who is described by the Vedas as the complete Supreme Lord, is the support of all beings, is devoid of guṇas, and is beyond prakṛti.**

*nirguṇo yathā viṣṇu-purāṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**Viṣṇu Purāṇa describes that he is devoid of guṇas:**

*sattvādayo na santīśe yatra ca prākṛtā guṇāḥ \|*  
*sa śuddhaḥ sarva-śuddhebhyaḥ pumān ādyaḥ prasīdatu \|\|5\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**In Viṣṇu, there are no material guṇas such as sattva. May the Lord, purer than all pure beings, be pleased with me.**

*tathā pādme—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Padma Purāṇa it is said:**

*yo’sau nirguṇa ity uktaḥ śāstreṣu jagad-īśvaraḥ \|*  
*prākṛtair heya-saṁyuktair guṇair hīnatvam ucyate \|\|6\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**The lord of the universe who is called nirguṇa in scriptures, is called so since he is devoid of material qualities which are inferior.**

*ataḥ prākṛta-guṇair varjito līlayā sa-guṇaḥ \|*  
*śrī-bhāgavate rāse —*

**Перевод:**

**Thus the Lord is devoid of material guṇās. He has qualities for his pastimes. In Bhāgavatam, concerning the rāsa dance, it is said:**

*avyayasyāprameyasya nirguṇasya guṇātmanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord is inexhaustible and immeasurable, he is untouched by the material modes (nirguṇa) and is full of qualities (guṇātmanaḥ). (SB** [**10.29.14**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/29.14)**)**

*atha —*  
*athāpi te deva padāmbuja-dvayaprasāda-leśānugṛhīta eva hi \|*  
*jānāti tattvaṁ bhagavan mahimno*  
*na cān ya eko’pi ciraṁ vicinvan \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, revealer of everything! O Lord endowed with all qualities! Such persons, receiving a particle of mercy from your lotus feet, realize to some degree your greatness. Others cannot understand even after reflecting for a long time. (SB** [**10.14.29**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.29)**)**

*viṣṇu-purāne bhagavān yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**In Viṣṇu Purāṇa, Bhagavān is described:**

*jñāna-śakti-balaiśvarya-vīrya-tejāṁsy aśeṣataḥ \|*  
*bhagavac-chabda-vācyāni vinā heyair guṇādibhiḥ \|\|8\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**The word bhagavān means that he is complete with power of mind (omniscience), power of senses, power of body, influence, control, and beauty, and is devoid of all inferior qualities.**

*atha yatra bhautika-dehas tatra māyā, yatra māyā tatra guṇāḥ, yatra guṇās tatra pralayo dhruva eva \| evaṁ bhautika-dehe deha-dehi-bhedo vartate \| ataḥ sa eva naśvaraḥ \| īśvarasya śrī-vṛndāvana-candrasya na bhautiko dehaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Where there is a material body there is māyā. Where there is māyāthere are material guṇas. Where there are material guṇas, there is certainly pralaya, destruction. In this way in material bodies there is a difference between the body and the possessor of the body. Thus, the body is temporary. Kṛṣṇa, the lord of Vṛndāvana, does not have a material body.**

*yathā saṁmohana-tantre sanaka uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**As Sanaka states in Saṁmohana-tantra:**

*tad ānanda-mayī rādhā tad ānanda-mayo hariḥ \|*  
*na bhautiko deha-bandhas tayor ānanda-svarūpayoḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā is full of bliss. Kṛṣṇa is full of bliss. The two forms of bliss are not bound to material bodies.**

*tathaiveśvarasya deha-dehi-bhedo nāsti, yathā kaurme—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly for the Supreme Lord there is no difference between his body and possessor of body, as stated in Kūrma Purāṇa:**

*deha-dehi-vibhedas tu neśvare vidyate kvacit \|*  
*ato līlā-mayo dehaḥ kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is no distinction of body and possessor of the body in the Lord. Kṛṣṇa, Paramātmā, has a body of spiritual pastimes.**

*tathā ādi-yāmale—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Ādi-yāmala it is said:**

*sarveṣāṁ naśvaro deho deha-dehi-vibhedataḥ \|*  
*sarvātmakānanda-maye pralayaḥ kim u jāyate \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All beings have temporary bodies because of difference between the body and the ātmā. How can destruction take place in the Lord, who is complete bliss and the ātmā of all beings?**

*tathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly:**

*sṛṣṭiṁ sthitiṁ ca pralayaṁ yaḥ karoti sa īśvaraḥ \|*  
*tasmin sarvāṇi līyante sa kutra parilīyate \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who creates, maintains and destroys the world is the Lord.Everything merges in him. In what does he merge?**

*atha śrī-kṛṣṇa-candrasya deho nityo na bhautikaḥ \|*  
*tasmin deha-dehibhedo nāsti, tat kim iti tad atra śrūyatām*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, Kṛṣṇa’s body is eternal, not material. In him, there is no difference between his body and himself. The following should be heard:**

*udayate bahir yo’sau sthūla-sūkṣma-paraḥ pumān \|*  
*līlayā sa-tanur bhāti nityānandaḥ sanātanaḥ \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord, beyond gross and subtle, who manifests externally shines with eternal bliss eternally, with a body, for pastimes.**

*aho yady evaṁ tadā kathaṁ prakṛti-saṅgaḥ? na tu tad eva, dvayor ekatvād eka eva, yathā śrī-kṛṣṇa-yāmale ūna-viṁśādhika-śatatama-paṭale strī-rūpam āśritya śrībhagavatā vāsudevena divya-vṛndāvane rādhayā śrī-kṛṣṇo dṛṣṭo’bhedena—*

**Перевод:**

**If that is so how is it that he associates with prakṛti (his śakti)? Not just that, since the two are one, they are actually one entity only. In Kṛṣṇa-yāmala, Chapter 119, it is described that Vāsudeva Kṛṣṇa taking shelter of a female form in spiritual Vṛndāvana, saw that Kṛṣṇa was non-different from Rādhā.**

*anyonyāśleṣitāṅgau tau rādhā-kṛṣṇau dadarśa sā \|*  
*rādhāṁ sphurad-rasāṁ kṛṣṇa-sarvāṅga-svāṅga-gopitām \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vāsudeva as the gopī saw Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa embracing. Rādhā, trembling with rasa, was covered on all her limbs by Kṛṣṇa’s limbs.**

*cumbantīṁ kṛṣṇa-candrasyādhara-divya-sudhāśrayām \|*  
*kṛṣṇo rādhāṅga-rāgeṇa kuṅkumī-kṛta-vigrahaḥ \|\|15\|\| ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**She was kissing, taking shelter of the divine nectar of Kṛṣṇa’s lips. Kṛṣṇa’s limbs turned red due to the color of Rādhā’s limbs.**

*tad iti viṣṇu-dharmottare—*

**Перевод:**

**In Viṣṇu-dharmottara it is said:**

*sac-cid-ānanda-sāndratvād dvayor evāviśeṣataḥ \|*  
*aupacārika evātra bhedo’yaṁ dehayor dvayoḥ \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Since the two cannot be distinguished, both being condensed eternity, consciousness and bliss, the difference between their two bodies is metaphorical.**

*evaṁ rādhāṅgajāṅgavat \| tadā kathaṁ dvidhā-rūpaḥ? tathā nārada-pañcarātre—*

**Перевод:**

**In this way, Rādhā manifests from his limbs, like other limbs. Why then are there two forms? Nārada-pañcarātra says:**

*svayaṁ hi bahavo bhūtvā ramaṇārthaṁ mahā-rasaḥ \|*  
*tayātirasayā reme priyayā caika-rūpayā \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, full of great rasa, becomes many for enjoyment. He enjoys with his beloved who has one form, who is filled with rasa.**

*priyayā rādhayā saha \| tathā govinda-vṛndāvane — ardhāṅgāt rādhā samutpannā ity agre vakṣyāmi \| tatra vyaktī-bhaviṣyati*

**Перевод:**

**Priyayā means with Rādhā. I will explain later that Rādhā arises as half his limbs according to Govinda-vṛndāvana. That will become clear in that statement.**

*atha kaiścid uktam—yadi sva-prakāśo līlā-rasa-mayaḥ paramātmasvarūpas tasmin kathaṁ śyāma-varṇatvaṁ sarvatra prasiddham ? yathā śrutau—rūpaṁ na vedyaṁ na ca bindu-nādaḥ ity ādi \| tad āha—*

**Перевод:**

**Some say that if the Lord is self-manifesting, filled with the rasa of pastimes, the svarūpa of Paramātmā, why is he famous everywhere as having a form having blue complexion?**

*śyāmābhatvaṁ vidhatte yat sarva-varṇo’tra līyate \|*  
*nityaṁ ca prabhavaty eva kālo’smin naiva vidyate \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He manifests a black complexion, in which all colors merge. This is eternally existing and is unaffected by time.**

*atha kaiścid uktam – nityatvaṁ katham uktam ? mahā-pralaye’pi sarvaṁ naśyaty eva, yathā— na ke’pi sthātāraḥ sura-giri-prabhṛtayaḥ iti vedāntapramāṇam \| tat pratyuttaram āha—*

**Перевод:**

**Someone says, “How can you say that this is eternally manifested? Even during mahā-pralaya, everything is destroyed. Vedānta says na ke’pi sthātāraḥ sura-giri-prabhṛtayaḥ: nothing remains, not devatās or mountains.” In answer to that, it is said:**

*bhūr-lokād ūrdhvataḥ sthānaṁ lakṣa-yojana-mānataḥ \|*  
*sūryasyaiva sudhāṁśoś ca tad ūrdhvaṁ lakṣa-yojanam \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Above earth at 100,000 yojanas is the sun and above that at 100,000 yojanas is the moon.**

*tad-vaśāt kāla-niyamo na tatra hi cakāsati \| yatra divya-vṛndāvanam, tatra candra-sūryayoḥ prakāśo nāsti \| yatra kālo nāsti tatra kathaṁ pralayaḥ ? yataḥ kāla-niyamāt pralayaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**By that place’s influence, the rules of time (due to movements of sun and moon) do not manifest there. In that place, spiritual Vrndāvana, the moon and sun do not manifest. How can there be pralaya where time does not exist? for pralaya occurs due to rules of time.**

*yathā goloka-saṁhitāyām—*

**Перевод:**

**As stated in Goloka-saṁhitā:**

*pāda-gamyaṁ tu yat kiṁcit sa bhūrloka iti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*ā-sūryaṁ tu bhuvar-loka ā-dhruvaṁ svarga ucyate \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Wherever one can go on foot is called Bhūrloka. Bhuvar-loka extends to the sun. Above that till Dhruva-loka is called Svarga.**

*mahar-lokaḥ kṣiter urdhvam eka-koṭis tu mānataḥ \|*  
*koṭi-dvayena vikhyāto jano lokas tu yojane \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Maharloka is ten million yojanas above earth. Janaloka is twenty million yojanas above earth.**

*catuṣkoṭi-pramāṇas tu tapo lokas tu bhūtalāt \|*  
*upariṣṭāt tataḥ satyaṁ koṭir aṣṭau pramāṇataḥ \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Tapoloka is forty million yojanas above earth. Satyaloka is eighty million yojanas above earth.**

*satyād upari vaikuṇṭhaḥ koṭir aṣṭau pramāṇataḥ \|*  
*tasyopariṣṭāt kaumāra umā-lokas tataḥ paraḥ \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Eighty million yojanas above Satya-loka is Vaikuṇṭha. Above it is the planet of the Kumāras and above that the planet of Umā.**

*śiva-lokas tad-upari golokas tad-upari smṛtaḥ \|*  
*jyotirmayaṁ tatra brahma tatra vṛndāvanaṁ mahat \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Above that is Śiva-loka and above that is Goloka. There is resplendent Brahman. Within this place is great Vṛndāvana.**

*tatraiva rādhikā devī sarva-śakti-namaskṛtā \|*  
*tatraiva bhagavān kṛṣṇaḥ sarva-deva-śiromaṇiḥ \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There, Rādhā is offered respects by all śaktis. There, Bhagavān Kṛṣṇa is the crest jewel of all devatās.**

*tatra śrī-bhagavān, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Bhagavān is described as follows:**

*āyatiṁ niyatiṁ caiva bhūtānām agatiṁ gatim \|*  
*vetti vidyām avidyāṁ ca sa vācyo bhagavān iti \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He who knows the future and karma, the coming and going of all beings, knowledge and ignorance is called Bhagavān. (Viṣṇu Purāṇa)**

*ata eva bhagavataḥ kathaṁ pralayaḥ? tac ca śrī-bhagavad-aṅga-jyotiṣā sarvam uddīpitam \| tasmin kathaṁ kālaḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**Thus how can Bhagavān be destroyed by pralaya? Everything is illuminated by the light from his limbs. How can time exist in him?**

*atha vādino vadanti—yadi candra-sūrya-gati-vaśān na kāla-niyamo’bhūt \| tat kiṁ nimeṣādibhiḥ kālo mantavyaḥ ? tena pralaya-mahā-pralayādir vidhātavyaḥ \| yathā nimeṣādir ayaṁkālas*

**Перевод:**

**Philosophers say: If the control of time due to movements of sun and moon does not exist, then do you accept time with measurements of moments? Pralaya and mahā-pralaya are calculated by those divisions of time. Time is made of moments.**

*tad eva—*

**Перевод:**

**It is said:**

*aṣṭādaśa-nimeṣās tu kāṣṭhās triṁśat tu tāḥ kalāḥ \|*  
*tās tu triṁśat kṣaṇas te tu muhūrto dvādaśa ’striyām \|\|27\|\| iti*

**Перевод:**

**Eighteen nimeṣas make one kāṣṭha. Thirty kāṣṭhas make one kalā. Thirty kalās make a kṣaṇa. Twelve kṣaṇas make a muhūrta.(Amara-kośa)**

*tatra vihasya bhāgavatā vadanti—udīritārtho’pi bhavadbhir na jñāyate \| tad eva yatra ca na bhautiko dehaḥ \| tatra kathaṁ nimeṣo vartate ? nimeṣādir iti vāyoḥ svabhāvaḥ \| ata eva bhautike dehe nimeṣādiḥ \| yathā— pṛthivy-āpas tathā tejo vāyur ākāśa eva ca iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Laughing, the devotees say: You do not even understand the meaning of what was said. If the Lord does not have a material body, what is the question of moments of time existing for him? Nimeṣa and other measurements of time are the nature of vāyu.Nimeṣa, etc., exist in a material body, like earth, water, fire, air and either.**

*vāyur yathā gorakṣa-saṁhitāyāṁ yoga-vāsiṣṭhe ca—*

**Перевод:**

**Vāyu is explained in Gorakṣa-saṁhitā and Yoga-vāsiṣṭha:**

*prāṇo’pānaḥ samānaś ca udāno vyāna eva ca \|*  
*nāgaḥ kūrmo’tha kṛkāro devadatto dhanañjayaḥ \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The airs are prāṇa, apāna, samāna, udāna, vyāna, nāga, kūrma, kṛkāra, devadatta and dhanañjaya.**

*tatra kūrma-vāyor nimeṣonmeṣādi-guṇaḥ \| tathā tatraiva— nāgo gṛhṇāti caitanyaṁ kūrmaś caiva nimīlati; vāsiṣṭhe—nimīlanādi kūrmasya kṣut tṛṣṇā kṛkarasya ca ity evaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candre kālo nāstīti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Among them, closing and opening the eyes are the qualities of kūrma air. Nāga supports consciousness and kūrma causes eyes to blink. According to Vāsiṣṭha, kūrma causes eyes to blink and kṛkara causes hunger and thirst. Thus, time does not exist in Kṛṣṇa.**

*yathā govinda-vṛndāvane balarāmaṁ prati śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**As stated in Govinda-vṛndāvana, Kṛṣṇa says to Balarāma:**

*premānanda-mayaḥ śuddhaḥ sarvadā nava-yauvanaḥ \|*  
*kālaḥ kāla-svarūpo’haṁ kālātmā kāla-gocaraḥ \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I, full of the bliss of prema, pure, always a fresh youth, am also time, the svarūpa of time, the essence of time and am perceivable in time.**

*samasta-kāla-rahitaḥ sarva-kāraṇa-kāraṇam \|*  
*cit-svarūpo jñāna-rūpo’dvitīyaḥ sama-dṛk paraḥ \|*  
*evaṁ rūpaḥ sadaivāhaṁ tiṣṭhāmy atraiva sarvadā \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am devoid of all time. I am the cause of all causes. I am the svarūpa of consciousness, the form of knowledge, without a second, seeing all equally. I always stay in this form in Vṛndāvana.**

*ata eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-candro nityo mahā-rasa-mayaḥ samasta-kāla-rahita iti jñātavyam \| tiṣṭhāmy atraiva iti vṛndāvane ity uktam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus it should be understood that Kṛṣṇa is eternal, composed of great rasa, devoid of all time. Tiṣṭhāmy atraiva means ‘I remain in Vṛndāvana’.**

*atha yathā śrī-bhagavān tathā vṛndāvanam eva, yathā padma-purāṇe nirvāṇa-khaṇḍe rahasyādhyāye śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Just as Kṛṣṇa is eternal, so is Vṛndāvana. The Lord says in Padma Purāṇa, Nirvāṇa-khaṇḍa, Rahasyādhyāya:**

*nityāṁ me mathurāṁ viddhi vanaṁ vṛndāvanaṁ tathā \|*  
*mamāvatāro nityo’yam atra mā saṁśayaṁ kṛthāḥ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Know that my Mathurā and the forest of Vṛndāvana are eternal. My avatāra is eternal there. Have no doubt about this.**

*tad atra vṛndāvanaṁ, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndāvana is described as follows:**

*vṛndāvanaṁ ca dvividhaṁ nityaṁ divyam itīritam \|*  
*nityaṁ bhuvi tathā divyaṁ sarvopari virājate \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are two types of Vṛndāvana: nitya and divya. The nitya exists on earth and the divya exists above everything.**

*tad eva nitya-vṛndāvanaṁ yathā śrī-kṛṣṇa-yāmale trayodaśādhika-śatatama-paṭale—*

**Перевод:**

**Nitya-vṛndāvana is described in Kṛṣṇa-yāmala, Chapter 130:**

*nityā vedaiḥ pragīyante siddhidāḥ siddhi-kāṅkṣibhiḥ \|*  
*nitya-vṛndāvanaṁ sthānaṁ pūrṇāti-pūrṇam ucyate \|\|33\|\|*

*līlāḥ pūrṇātipūrṇāś ca turīyās tatra kīrtitāḥ \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The eternal objects are glorified by the Vedas just as givers of siddhis are glorified by those who desire siddhis. Nitya-vṛndāvana is a place which is most complete. The pastimes there are glorified as most complete and spiritual.**

*divya-vṛndāvanaṁ, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Divya-vṛndāvana is described:**

*yena vilāsayaty eva śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ divya-nāyakam \|*  
*divyaṁ ca yad yathā devyā rādhāyā aṅga-saubhagam \|\|35\|\|*

*krīḍanīyaṁ pareśasya divyaṁ tenaiva kathyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Divya-vṛndāvana delights Kṛṣṇa, the divya hero, just as the attractive (divya) beauty of Rādhā’s limbs delight him. Because it is the place of playing of the Lord, it is called divya.**

*turīyādi-trayaṁ sthānaṁ divya-vṛndāvanaṁ varam \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The place of the three states beginning with turīyā, is Divya-vṛndāvanam the best.**

*pūrṇātipūrṇa-pūrṇaṁ yat svarūpaṁ rādhikā-pateḥ \|*  
*yasyāṁśāṁśa-praṇihitā līlās teṣu pratiṣṭhitāḥ \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The svarūpa of Kṛṣṇa is most complete. The pastimes of the aṁśas of his aṁśas take place in these places.**

*divya-vṛndāvane kṛṣṇaḥ śrī-rādhā-vallabhas tathā \|*  
*gopī-jana-vallabhas tu nitya-vṛndāvane sadā \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is the beloved of Rādhā in Divya-vṛndāvana. But the lover of the gopīs exists eternally in Nitya-vṛndāvana.**

*vṛndāvanam iti śrī-bhagavad-aṅga-viśeṣaḥ \| ataḥ tad eva kālādi-rahitam \| atha yatra śrī-kṛṣṇa-candras tad-aṅga-jyotiṣā vṛndāvanādi-samastaṁ pradīptam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndāvana is a special limb of the Lord. Thus it also is devoid of time. Kṛṣṇa, by the effulgence of his limbs, illuminates all of Vrndāvana.**

*yathā goloka-saṁhitāyāṁ balabhadraṁ prati śrī-bhagavān āha—*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord speaks to Balarāma in Goloka-saṁhitā:**

*eko’neka-svarūpo’haṁ sarva-śakti-mayaḥ pumān \|*  
*mad-dehān nirgataṁ jyotiḥ sarva-bhūta-mayaṁ param \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am one form and many forms. I am endowed with all śaktis.From my body emanates light which consists of all beings and is supreme.**

*tathaiva govinda-vṛndāvane balarāma-praśne—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Govinda-vṛndāvana, Balarāma inquires:**

*anyat tu sūrya-candrādi-prakāśa-sadṛśaṁ tava \|*  
*tanu-pāda-nakhāj jyotiḥ kim idaṁ tad vadasva me \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Please explain to me the light from your toenails, a different aspect, which is similar to the light of the sun and moon.**

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said:**

*jyotir brahma-mayaṁ tejo mac-charīrād vinirgatam \|*  
*mamānena na bhedo’sti brahma-jyotir ahaṁ param \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This spiritual light, made of Brahman, emanates from my body. This brahma-jyoti is not different from me. I am the Supreme Brahman.**

*pṛthivy-āpo-vahni-rūpair vāyu-rūpais tathaiva ca \|*  
*ākāśa-rūpaiḥ sadā paśya jala-bhāṇḍe yathā raviḥ \|*  
*durlabhaṁ durgamaṁ jyotir durdarśaṁ sarvagaṁ śuci \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**See this light always present as earth, water, fire, air and ether, just as the sun is reflected in a water pot. That light is hard to attain, hard to approach, hard to see, all pervading and pure.**

*sukhadaṁ mokṣadaṁ mahyaṁ pādāṅguṣṭhād vinirgatam \|*  
*etad dhyātvā yogino’pi yānti nirvāṇam uttamam \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That light bestows happiness and liberation and emanates from my toe. Meditating on that light, the yogīs go to the supreme nirvāṇa.**

*tathā atharvopaniṣadi gopāla-tāpanīye brahmāṇaṁ prati śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Gopāla-tāpanī Upaniṣad, the Lord speaks to Brahmā:**

*cit-svarūpaṁ paraṁ jyotiḥ-svarūpaṁ rūpa-varjitaṁ \|*  
*hṛdā māṁ saṁsmaran brahman tat-padaṁ yāti niścitam \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Brahmā! He who in his heart remembers me, the svarūpa of consciousness, the supreme svarūpa of Brahma-jyoti, devoid of material form, certainly attains my abode.**

*atha varāha-saṁhitāyāṁ śrī-varāha uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Varāha-saṁhitā, Lord Varāha says:**

*tac-chyāma-deha-kiraṇaiḥ parānanda-rasāmṛtaiḥ \|*  
*tad-aṁśu-koṭi-koṭy-aṁśā jīvās tat-kiraṇātmakāḥ \|\|45\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**The jīvas, a billionth part of the Lord’s aṁśa, are rays of that light which emanate from his dark body and are filled with the sweetness of the superior bliss.**

*evaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candra-tanu-pāda-nakha-jyotiṣā samujjvalaṁ vṛndāvanādi-samasta-sthalam iti mantavyam \| tatra kālādi-praveśo naiva, yatra śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ sva-prakāśo nitya-kiśoraḥ samasta-kālādi-rahitaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**One should consider that all the places such as Vṛndāvana glow from the light from Kṛṣṇa’s toenails. In that place, there is no entrance of time, etc. There, Kṛṣṇa resides self-manifested, endowed with eternal kaiśora age, devoid of all aspects of time.**

*tathā hi, govinda-vṛndāvane śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ prati balarāma uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly, in Govinda-vṛndāvana, Balarāma speaks to Kṛṣṇa:**

*rādhā-kānta jagannātha śrīmad-gokula-nāgara \|*  
*śyāmasundara gopīśa gokulānanda-candramaḥ \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**
