**O lover of Rādhā! O lord of the universe! O hero of Gokula! O Śyamasundara! O lord of the gopīs! O blissful moon of Gokula!**

*vṛndāvana-sukhānanda pītavāsaḥ priya prabho \|*  
*pādāmbuja nakha-jyotir āpta-loka-traya prabho \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O bliss of Vṛndāvana! O wearer of yellow cloth! O dearest! O master! O lotus-eyed, with light emanating from your toenails! O possessor of the three worlds! O master!**

*śabda-brahma-mayī-vaṁśī-priya padma-dalekṣaṇa \|*  
*prema-bhakti-puṣpa-mayī-vana-mālā priyottama \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Kṛṣṇa, fond of the flute composed of śabda-brahman! O Lord with lotus petal eyes! O wearer of a forest garland made of flowers of prema! O dearest!**

*govinda go-gaṇārtighna go-pate go-gaṇārcita \|*  
*yat tvayā kathitaṁ tattvam ātmanas tu samāsataḥ \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Govinda! O destroyer of the pain of the cows! O Lord of the cows! O Lord worshipped by the cows! You have told me the truth about you in brief.**

*kiṁ-svarūpo’si bhagavan kim-īhaḥ kiṁ-svarūpakaḥ \|*  
*vistareṇa punas tasmai śrotum icchāmi tadvat \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What is your svarūpa? What are your activities? What is your nature? I desire to hear this in detail.**

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said:**

*aham ātmā paraṁ brahma sac-cid-ānanda-vigrahaḥ \|*  
*śabda-brahma-mayaḥ sākṣāt svayaṁ prakṛtir īśvaraḥ \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am the ātmā, Para-brahman, the form of eternity, knowledge and bliss, composed of śabda-brahman directly. I am directly prakṛti and its controller.**

*ādy-anta-rahitaḥ sthūlātītaḥ parātparaḥ \|*  
*svayaṁ-jyotiḥ svayaṁ-kartā svayaṁ-hartā svayaṁ-prabhuḥ \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am devoid of beginning and end. I am beyond gross matter and superior to the best. I am self-manifesting, the doer, the destroyer and the master.**

*kaṭākṣa-mātra-brahmāṇḍa-koṭi-sṛṣṭi-vināśa-kṛt \|*  
*sadāśiva-mahāviṣṇu-rudra-brahmādi-kārakaḥ \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I create and destroy millions of universes just by my glance. I am the cause of Sadāśiva, Mahā-viṣṇu, Rudra, Brahmā and others.**

*narākṛti-nitya-rūpī vaṁśī-vādya-priyaḥ sadā \|*  
*indranīla-maṇi-śyāmas tribhaṅgī madhurākṛtiḥ \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I have an eternal human-like form and am always fond of playing the flute. I am dark like a sapphire, have three bends and have a sweet form.**

*pūrṇendu-koṭi-sadṛśo nānā-lāvaṇya-vāridhiḥ \|*  
*puṇḍarīka-dalākāra-nayanaḥ prema-sāgaraḥ \|*  
*jita-kāma-dhanur-divya-bhrū-latā-lalitotsavaḥ \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am like a million full-moons, an ocean of various types of beauty. I have lotus-shaped eyes. I am an ocean of prema. I am joyful with graceful eyebrows which defeat Cupid’s bow.**

*tribhaṅga-lalita-śrīmat-tīryag-grīvātisundaraḥ \|*  
*śabda-brahma-mayī-vaṁśī-vādanotsava-sāgaraḥ \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am beautiful, with a tilted neck and graceful form with three bends. I am a joyful ocean of music emanating from my flute made of śabda-brahman.**

*vana-mālī pītavāsāḥ sukuñcita-śiroruhaḥ \|*  
*barhi-barha-kṛtottaṁsaḥ pārijātāvataṁsakaḥ \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I wear a forest garland and yellow cloth. My hair is finely curled. I wear a peacock feather head ornament and pārijāta ear ornaments.**

*premānanda-mayaḥ śuddhaḥ sarvadā nava-yauvanaḥ \|*  
*kālaḥ kāla-svarūpo’haṁ kālātmā kāla-gocaraḥ \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I, full of the bliss of prema, pure, always a fresh youth, am also time, the svarūpa of time, the essence of time and am perceivable in time.**

*samasta-kāla-rahitaḥ sarva-kāraṇa-kāraṇam \|*  
*cit-svarūpo jñāna-rūpo’dvitīyaḥ sama-dṛk-paraḥ \|*  
*evaṁ rūpaḥ sadaivāhaṁ tiṣṭhāmy atraiva sarvadā \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am devoid of time and the cause of all causes. I am the svarūpaof consciousness and the form of jñāna. I am without a second and see equally. I remain in Vṛndāvana eternally with this form.**

*tathā brahma-saṁhitāyām—*

**Перевод:**

**In Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.33**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.33)**), it is said:**

*advaitam acyutam anādim ananta-rūpam*  
*ādyaṁ purāṇa-puruṣaṁ nava-yauvanaṁ ca \|*  
*vedeṣu durlabham adurlabham ātma-bhaktau*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|60\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda who has no equal, who is infallible in protecting his devotees, who is the source of all others but expands into infinite forms, who is the topmost, who exists previous to all others but is eternally youthful, and who is rarely attained by those studying the Vedas but attained easily by his devotees.**

*atha sarveśvaratvam \| śrī-bhagavan-nirūpaṇaṁ, yathā brahma-saṁhitāyām śrī-bhagavantam ālokya brahmā āṣṭā-daśākṣara-mantraṁ prāpa \| tad anu śrī-bhagavantaṁ stauti \| yathā śrī-bhagavataḥ samudbhūya brahmā sarvatrāndhakāraṁ dṛṣṭvā bhagavantam stauti \| tato brahmāṇaṁ prati aṣṭā-daśākṣaram adāt \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is defined as the controller of all. In Brahmā-saṁhitā, Brahmā sees the Lord and obtained the eighteen-syllable mantra. After that, he praised the Lord, just as (according to Bhāgavatam) when, born from the Lord, seeing the darkness everything, he offered prayers. Then the Lord gave Brahmā the eighteen-syllable mantra.**

*atha tepe sa suciraṁ prīṇan govindam avyayam \|*  
*śvetadvīpa-patiṁ kṛṣṇaṁ goloka-sthaṁ parātparam \|\|61\|\|*

*prakṛtyā guṇa-rūpiṇyā rūpiṇyā paryupāsitam \|*  
*sahasra-dala-sampanne koṭi-kiñjalka-bṛṁhite \|\|62\|\|*

*bhūmiś cintāmaṇis tatra karṇikāre mahāsane \|*  
*samāsīnaṁ cid-ānandaṁ jyoti-rūpaṁ sanātanam \|\|63\|\|*

*śabda-brahma-mayaṁ veṇuṁ vādayantaṁ mukhāmbuje \|*  
*vilāsinī-gaṇa-vṛtaṁ svaiḥ svair aṁśair abhiṣṭutam \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā underwent meditation for a long time and pleased the highest form of God, the indestructible Govinda, Kṛṣṇa, Lord of Śvetadvīpa, situated in Goloka, whose land is made of cintāmaṇi. Govinda is worshipped by prakṛti in the form of its guṇas from a distance. He has an eternal form of consciousness and bliss which is self-revealing. He is seated on a large throne in the pericarp of a lotus with millions of stamens and thousands of petals, playing a flute with his lotus mouth, and is surrounded by the gopīs and praised by other associates.**

*atha veṇu-ninādasya trayī mūrti-mayī gatiḥ \|*  
*sphurantī praviveśāśu mukhābjāni svayambhuvaḥ \|\|65\|\|*

*gāyatrīṁ gāyatas tasmād adhigatya sarojajaḥ \|*  
*saṁskṛtaś cādi-guruṇā dvijatām agamat tataḥ \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then the embodiment of the Vedas, the Gāyatrī-mantra emanating from the sound of Kṛṣṇa’s flute (after entering his ears) appeared in the four mouths of Brahmā. Uttering the Gāyatrī-mantra, Brahmā, being purified, attained second birth from the original guru Kṛṣṇa. (Brahma-saṁhitā** [**5.25-28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.25)**)**

*trayyā prabuddho ’tha vidhir vījñāta-tattva-sāgaraḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭāva veda-sāreṇa stotreṇānena keśavam \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Becoming enlightened by this Gāyatrī, Brahmā became thoroughly knowledgeable of the highest truth. He satisfied Keśava by the following verses of praise following the statements of the Vedas.**

*cintāmaṇi-prakara-sadmasu ity ādi \| tathā nārada-pañcarātre nāradānanta-saṁvāde bhakti-rahasye—*

**Перевод:**

**He then recited his prayers starting with cintāmaṇi prakara sadmasu. Similarly in Nārada-pañcarātra, in a conversation between Nārada and Ananta, in bhakti-rahasya, it is said:**

*cid-ānanda-svarūpaṁ ca nirguṇaṁ prakṛteḥ param \|*  
*sudhā-toyaṁ latā-kalpa-latā-cintāmaṇi-sthalī \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The place is of the nature of consciousness and bliss, is devoid of guṇas, and beyond prakṛti. It has pure water; the creepers are desire creepers and the places are made of cintāmaṇi gems.**

*brahma-jyotiḥ priyā lakṣmīr astraṁ veṇuḥ pumān hariḥ \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The light is Brahman, the consort is Lakṣmī, the weapon is the flute, and the Lord is Kṛṣṇa.**

*kathā gānaṁ gatir nāṭyaṁ parikhā kṣīra-sāgaraḥ \|*  
*tal-loka-vāsināṁ devaṁ vṛndāvana-purandaram \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The speaking is song, the movements are dances, the water bodies are milk oceans. The lord of the inhabitants is the king of Vṛndāvana.**

*divyātidivyaṁ śrī-dehaṁ kāla-māyādy-agocaram \|*  
*dvibhujaṁ megha-śyāmāṅgaṁ kiśoraṁ vanamālinam \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His body is most attractive, untouched by time and māyā. He has two arms. His complexion is that of a dark cloud. He is of kaiśoraage and wears a forest garland.**

*divyābharaṇa-bhūṣāṅgaṁ gopa-kanyā-samāvṛtam \|*  
*dayitaṁ prema-bhaktānām advaitaṁ brahma-vādinām \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is surrounded by gopīs decorated with attractive ornaments.He is the lover for devotees in prema and is advaita (non-dual) to the brahma-vādīs.**

*mīna-kūrmādayo yasya aṁśāṁśāḥ sarva-devatāḥ \|*  
*yasya smaraṇa-mātreṇa naro nārī bhavaty api \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Matsya, Kūrma and and all devatās are aṁśas of his aṁśas. Just by remembering him, a male becomes a gopī.**

*paurāṇikā yajanty evaṁ vaitānair brahma-vādinaḥ \|*  
*bhakti-tantra-vidhānena trikāṇḍenaiva sādhavaḥ \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The followers of the Purāṇas worship him in this way. The brahma-vādīs worship by yajñas. The devotees worship him through rules of Pañcarātra in three divisions.**

*tathā saṁmohana-tantre dvitīya-paṭale nāradaṁ prati sanaka uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Saṁmohana-tantra, Chapter 2, Sanaka speaks to Nārada:**

*dhyāyet kṛṣṇaṁ ca su-śyāmaṁ pūrṇānanda-kalevaram \|*  
*koṭi-sūrya-prabhaṁ caiva yoginām api durlabham \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should meditate on Kṛṣṇa, dark in complexion, whose body is full of bliss, effulgent like a million suns and who is rare for yogīs.**

*sarva-saundarya-nilayaṁ rādhāliṅgita-vigraham \|*  
*pūrṇānanda-svarūpaṁ taṁ na tu bhūta-mayaṁ hi tat \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is the abode of all beauty, a form embraced by Rādhā, the svarūpa of full bliss, but not composed of anything material.**

*yādṛśī veśa-bhūṣā ca manasaḥ prīti-dāyinī \|*  
*tādṛśī ca harer jñeyā bhaktānugrāhako hariḥ \|\|77\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa’s clothing and ornaments are to be understood according to how they are pleasing to the mind. He is merciful to the devotees.**

*atha śrutibhir avalokito yathā bṛhad-vāmana-purāṇe vṛndāvana-māhātmye śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ prati śrutaya ūcuḥ, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**This is also observed by the śrutis. In Bṛhad-vāmana Purāṇa, in Vṛndāvana-māhātmya, the śrutis speak to Kṛṣṇa:**

*prākṛte pralaye prāpte’vyakte vyaktiṁ gate purā \|*  
*śliṣṭe brahmaṇi cin-mātre kāla-māyātige’kṣare \|\|*  
*brahmānanda-mayo loko vyāpī vaikuṇṭha-saṁjñakaḥ \|\|78\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When prakṛti becomes unmanifest at pralaya from a previously manifested state, merged in Brahman which is pure cit, beyond time and māyā and indestructible, the place called Vaikuṇṭha, composed of the bliss of Brahman, pervades everywhere.**

*nirguṇo’nādy-anantaś ca vartate kevale’kṣare \|*  
*akṣaraṁ paramaṁ brahma vedānāṁ sthānam uttamam \|\|79\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The place is beyond the guṇas, without beginning and end, within the pure indestructible. It is indestructible, the Supreme Brahman, the highest abode of the Vedas.**

*tal-loka-vāsi-tatra-sthaiḥ stuto vedaiḥ parātparaḥ \|*  
*ciraṁ stutvā tatas tuṣṭaḥ parokṣaḥ prāha tān girā \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord is praised by the Vedas who are situated there as residents. After they praised the Lord for a long time, the Supreme Lord, unseen by them, was satisfied and spoke to them.**

*śrutīḥ prati śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord spoke to the śrutis:**

*tuṣṭo’smi brūta bhoḥ prājñā varaṁ yan manasīpsitam \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am satisfied. O wise ones! Tell me the benediction you desire.**

*śrutaya ūcuḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**The śrutis said:**

*nārāyaṇādi-rūpāṇi jñātāny asmābhir acyuta \|*  
*sa-guṇaṁ brahma sarvedaṁ vastu-buddhir na teṣu naḥ \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Acyuta! We know the forms of Nārāyaṇa, etc. We do not consider that the material world with its guṇas is the ultimate truth.**

*brahmeti procyate’smābhir yad-rūpaṁ nirguṇaṁ param \|*  
*vāṅ-mano-gocarātītaṁ tato na jñāyate hi tat \|\|83\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What is called Brahman is without material guṇas, beyond words and mind. Thus it cannot be properly understood by us.**

*ānanda-mātram iti yad vadantīha purāvidaḥ \|*  
*tad-rūpaṁ darśayāsmākaṁ yadi deyo varo hi naḥ \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ancient knowers say that it is pure bliss. If you should give us a boon, show us that form.**

*śrutvaitad darśayāmāsa svaṁ lokaṁ prakṛteḥ param \|*  
*kevalānubhavānanda-mātram akṣaram avyayam \|\|85\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing that, the Lord showed them his planet beyond prakṛti, which was a pure experience of bliss, indestructible and imperishable.**

*yatra vṛndāvanaṁ nāma vanaṁ kāma-dughair drumaiḥ \|*  
*manorama-nikuñjāḍhyaṁ sarvartu-sukha-saṁyutam \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In that place, there was the forest called Vṛndāvana filled with desire trees and attractive bowers, endowed with the happiness of all seasons.**

*yatra govardhano nāma sunirjhara-darī-yutaḥ \|*  
*ratna-dhātu-mayaḥ śrīmān supakṣi-gaṇa-saṅkulaḥ \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In that place was beautiful Govardhana, with caves and waterfalls, filled with gems and minerals, crowded with attractive birds.**

*yatra nirjhara-pānīyā kālindī saritāṁ varā \|*  
*ratna-baddhobhaya-taṭī haṁsa-padmādi-saṅkulā \|\|88\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In that place Yamunā, best of rivers flowed with cascades of water, crowded with lotuses and swans, both its banks laden with jewels.**

*nānā-rāsa-rasonmattaṁ yatra gopī-kadambakam \|*  
*tat-kadambaka-madhyasthaḥ kiśorākṛtir acyutaḥ \|\|89\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In that place there were groups of gopīs intoxicated with various rasas of the rāsa dance. Kṛṣṇa, with kaiśora form, was situated in the middle of the group.**

*darśayitveti ca prāha brūta kiṁ karavāṇi vaḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭo madīya-loko’yaṁ yato nāsti paraṁ varam \|\|90\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having shown them this, the Lord said, “Tell me what can I do for you? You have seen my planet. There is nothing better than this.”**

*ataḥ kṛṣṇa-candropari ko’pi nāsti \| sarvopari śrī-kṛṣṇa-candro yathā śrī-kṛṣṇa-yāmale pañcāśītitama-paṭale śrī-bhagavantaṁ vāsudevaṁ prati śrī-rukmiṇy uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus there is no one superior to Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa is superior to all. In Kṛṣṇa-yāmala, Chapter 85, Rukmiṇī speaks to Vāsudeva:**

*tvad-ṛte nāsti yat kiñcij jagat sthāvara-jaṅgamam \|*  
*sarveṣām ātma-bhūto’si paramātmeti śabdyate \|\|91\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Moving and non-moving beings in this world do not exist without you. You are the ātmā of all beings. Thus, you are called Paramātmā.**

*sa tvaṁ kiṁ dhyāyasi śrīmān kāmināṁ sarva-kāmadaḥ \|*  
*kiṁ vā japasi tattvena etat tvaṁ vaktum arhasi \|\|92\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What do you meditate on? You are full of beauty and bestow all desires to persons who have desires. What do you chant? You should explain this.**

*niśamya vācaṁ kamalekṣaṇāyā*  
*yadūttamo yat priya-kṛt priyāyāḥ \|*  
*tām aṅkam āropya sukhaṁ ca sādaraṁ*  
*muhur muhuḥ prāhur mudā mudāntahṛt \|\|93\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing her words, the best of the Yadus, pleased with his beloved lotus eyed wife, happily and respectfully placed her on his lap and spoke constantly in joy, with joyful heart.**

*śrī-bhagavān vāsudeva uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Vāsudeva said:**

*sārāt sārataraṁ kānte yan māṁ pṛcchasi sāmpratam \|*  
*rahasyānāṁ rahasyaṁ tu tathāpi varṇayāmi te \|\|94\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved what you ask me now is the essence of all essences, the secret of all secrets. But I will still describe it to you.**

*tvaṁ me prāṇeśvarī kāntā yataḥ parama-śobhanā \|*  
*ato vaktavyam etat syāt tvayi nāsty api me rahaḥ \|\|95\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because you are my very life, my beloved, most beautiful, I must tell you. I keep no secrets from you.**

*premārāmaṁ lalita-vapuṣaṁ yat kalākrāntam etad*  
*yaṁ vedādyair vihita-munayo nāradādyā munīndrāḥ \|*  
*prāhuḥ satyaṁ parama-puruṣaṁ rādhikā-prāṇa-rūpaṁ*  
*taṁ gopīnāṁ nayana-kumuda-prekṣaṇīyendum īhe \|\|96\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I meditate upon the garden of prema, with charming body, expert at all arts, whom the sages such as Nārada ordained by the Vedas say is the truth, the supreme person, the life of Rādhā, the moon viewed by lotus eyes of the gopīs.**

*tasmād udita pro(?)yasya viśvādyam ādyam \|*  
*yad bhāgādaḥ sakala-jagatāṁ kāraṇaṁ dhīmahi sma \|\|97\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I meditate on the cause of the whole universe, who is full of auspiciousness, who is first within the universe.**

*jyotir yasya prathama-vapuṣo viśva-mūrtir virājaṁ*  
*brahmāṇḍānāṁ nicaya-racanā roma-kūpeṣu yasya \|*  
*āvṛtāste rucibhir amala-prajñayā durvigāhyā*  
*sā rādhā yaṁ praṇaya-viṣayaṁ sevate taṁ smarāmi \|\|98\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I meditate on he from whose first body effulgence radiates, who is the form of the universe, who is the virāja, in whose hair holes groups of universes are created, whom Rādhā, who is covered by his beauty, who cannot be understood by pure intelligence, serves as the object of her love.**

*brahmaṇḍānāṁ pracaya-racanā kukkuṭakīva mūrdhni*  
*yat śaṁ kālāvayava-rahitaṁ divya-vṛndāvanākhyam \|*  
*sthānaṁ saṁsthāvaraṇa-lalitaṁ yat turīyāt paraṁ tat*  
*premānanda-prathitam aniśaṁ dhīmahi dhyāna-harṣāt \|\|99\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I meditate with joy constantly on attractive, auspicious Vṛndāvana, devoid of the limbs of time, with delightfully shaped walls, superior to pure consciousness, which is situated on the head of the created universes like a feather.**

*vaṁśī yasya priya-sahacarī cid-rasajñā sadaiṣā*  
*kṛṣṇasyārthe yad udabhavanā nāda uccaiḥ svaro yat \|*  
*śabdā varṇā śrutaya uditā bodhayantī sma viśvaṁ*  
*viśvānandaṁ tam aham aniśaṁ prema-dhāma prapadye \|\|100\|*

**Перевод:**

**I constantly surrender to the abode of prema. The bliss of the universe, the universe, whose flute, always his dear companion, a knower of spiritual rasa, making loud notes for Kṛṣṇa’s pleasure, awakens the universe with its words, syllables and notes.**

*yasya krīḍā-kalita-vapuṣaḥ sthāna-saṁsthāna-saṁsthā*  
*dhyānāśaktyāvayava-sahitā nānyad icchā-mayeṣu \|*  
*ātmānanda-prathita-jagatāṁ nākakaṁ nāntam īśaṁ*  
*taṁ gopīnāṁ nayana-kumuda-prekṣaṇīyendum īhe \|\|101\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I meditate upon the moon seen by the lotus eyes of the gopīs, the infinite Lord, the sky for the world manifesting its bliss, whose playful body, which manifests various places, is not the subject of meditation for persons filled with material desires.**

*ity evaṁ rādhā-premānanda-mayaṁ paripūrṇa-prema-svarūpaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candram ahaṁ cintaye \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this way, I meditate upon Kṛṣṇa, the svarūpa of complete prema, filled with the bliss of Rādhā’s prema.**

*paraṁ yathā padma-purāṇe nirvāṇa-khaṇḍe nāradaṁ prati sanatkumāreṇa yat kathitaṁ tad vyāso’mbarīṣaṁ rājānaṁ praty āha—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly, in Padma Purāṇa, Nirvāṇa-khaṇḍa, Vyāsa explains to Ambarīṣa what Sanatkumāṛa spoke to Nārada:**

*atigopyaṁ tvayā pṛṣṭaṁ yan mayā na śukaṁ prati \|*  
*gaditaṁ sva-sutaṁ kintu tvām vakṣyāmi hari-priyam \|\|102\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What you ask is most secret. I did not speak this to Śukadeva, my own son. But I speak it to you, who are dear to the Lord.**

*mayā kṛtaṁ tapaḥ pūrvaṁ bahu-varṣa-sahasrakam \|*  
*phala-mūla-dalāśāmbu-vāyv-āhāra-niṣeviṇā \|\|103\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Previously, I performed austerities for many thousands of years, eating only fruit, roots, leaves, water, and air.**

*tato mām āha bhagavān svādhyāya-nirataṁ hariḥ \|*  
*kasminn arthe cikīrṣā te vivitsā vā mahā-mate \|\|104\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then the Lord spoke to me, while I was absorbed in study. O great minded one! What do you desire to do or know?**

*prasanno’smi vṛṇu mune varaṁ tvaṁ varada-rṣabhāt \|*  
*mad-darśanāntaḥ saṁsāra iti satyaṁ bravīmi te \|\|105\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am satisfied. O sage! Accept a boon, since I am the best giver of boons. By seeing me, one ends saṁsāra. I speak the truth to you.**

*ato ’ham abruvaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ pulakotphulla-vigrahaḥ \|*  
*tvām ahaṁ draṣṭum icchāmi cakṣurbhyāṁ madhusūdana \|\|106\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With hairs standing on end, I spoke to Kṛṣṇa. O Madhusūdana! I want to see you with my eyes.**

*yat tat satyaṁ paraṁ brahma jagaj-yoniṁ jagad-gatim \|*  
*vadanti veda-śirasaś cākṣuṣaṁ nātha me’stu tat \|\|107\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The best of the Vedas say that you are the truth, the Supreme Brahman, the source of the universe, the shelter of the universe.O Lord! May I see you.**

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said:**

*mām eke prakṛtaṁ prāhuḥ puruṣaṁ ca tatheśvaram \|*  
*dharmam eke varaṁ caike mokṣam eke’kutobhayam \|\|108\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some say I am material person, or the Lord. Others say that I am dharma. Others say I am benedictions. Others say I am liberation, which makes one fearless.**

*śūnyam eke ’bhāvam eke paramāṇum athāpare \|*  
*daivam eke devam eke graham eke manaḥ pare \|*  
*buddhim eke kālam eke śivam eke sadā-śivam \|\|109\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some say I am a vacant state. Some say I am non-existent. Some say I am an atom. Some say I am karma. Others say I am a devatā.Others say I am a planet. Others say I am the mind. Others say I am intelligence. Others say I am time. Others say I am Śiva. And others say I am Sadā-śiva.**

*apare veda-śirasi sthitam ekaṁ sanātanam \|*  
*yad-bhāva-vikriyā-hīnaṁ sac-cid-ānanda-vigraham \|\|110\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Others say that I am the one eternal entity situated in the Upaniṣads, which is devoid of change and conditions, which is a form of eternity, consciousness and bliss.**

*ko’pi veda pumān loke mad-anugraha-bhājanam \|*  
*paśya tvaṁ darśayiṣyāmi svarūpaṁ veda-gopitam \|\|111\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Someone in this world who is the recipient of my mercy knows. Look. I will show you my form hidden in the Vedas.**

*veda-vyāsa uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Vyāsa said:**

*tato’paśyam ahaṁ bhūpa tataḥ kālāmbuda-prabham \|*  
*gopa-kanyāvṛtaṁ gopaṁ hasantaṁ gopa-bālakaiḥ \|\|112\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O King! Then I saw a cowherd with the complexion of a black cloud, surrounded by young gopīs, laughing with cowherd boys.**

*kadamba-mūlam āsīnaṁ pīta-vāsasam adbhutam \|*  
*vanaṁ vṛndāvanaṁ nāma nava-pallava-maṇḍitam \|*  
*kokila-bhramarārāvaṁ manobhava-manoharam \|\|113\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He was sitting under a kadamba tree. He was astonishing, wearing yellow cloth. The forest called Vṛndāvana was decorated with fresh leaves, resounded with the humming of bees and cuckoos, and was attractive to Cupid.**

*bālam iti yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**The word bāla is described as follows:**

*kaumāraṁ pañcamābdāntaṁ paugaṇḍaṁ daśamāvadhi \|*  
*kaiśoram āpañcadaśa yauvanaṁ tu tataḥ param \|*  
*bālyaṁ yauvanaṁ vārdhakyam iti vayo’vasthā-trayam \|\|114\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kaumāra age lasts till the fifth year. Paugaṇḍa age extends to ten years. Kaiśora age extends to fifteen years. Yauvana is after that.There are three ages: bālya, yauvana and vārdhakya (old age).**

*atha kaiśorāvadhi bālyam \| evaṁ bāle’py ukte kiśora-vayaḥ-padena kaiśora-vayaḥ sūcitam iti boddhavyam \| kim abhiprāyas tad āha—gopa-kanyā vṛtam iti padena kaiśora-vayaḥ sūcitam iti \| tathā bālye gopa-kanyā vṛtatvena ujjvala-rasa ity ayogyatvāt, tathā śrī-kṛṣṇa-yāmale ūnaviṁśādhika-śata-paṭale dvārakānāthaḥ śrī-vāsudevo’pi rādhā-kṛṣṇaṁ draṣṭuṁ śrī-tripurā-sahāyena sa-tripuro divya-vṛndāvane rādhā-kṛṣṇāntikaṁ viveśa \| śrī-kṛṣṇājñāyā rādhā-kuṇḍe snātvā strī-rūpo bhūtvā śyāmā iti nāma dhṛtvā paripūrṇaṁ prema-mayaṁ rādhā-kṛṣṇaṁ sā śyāmā dadarśa \|*

**Перевод:**

**Bālya lasts till kaiśora period. Though the word bāla is used it means kaiśora period since a younger age would be inappropriate for mādhurya-rasa, with a small child surrounded by gopīs.The intention is to indicate kaisora since it is stated that he is surrounded by gopīs. Thus in Kṛṣṇa-yāmala, Chapter 119, even the lord of Dvārakā, Vāsudeva, approached Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in Divya-vṛndāvana along with Tripurā in order to see them. Bathing in Rādha-kuṇḍa on Kṛsṇa’s order, he took a female form and took the name Śyāmā. Śyāmā then saw Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, full of prema.**

*tad yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**As stated in the following:**

*madhu-priyā nāma sakhī rādhā-kṛṣṇāti-vallabhā \|*  
*sā śyāmāṁ ca kare dhṛtvā rādhāyāḥ sammukhe’nayat \|\|115\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A sakhī named Madhupriyā, very dear to Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, took Śyāmā by the hand and brought her before Rādhā.**

*sāpaśyad rādhikāṁ kṛṣṇa-vakṣaḥ-sthala-samāśritām \|*  
*anaupamya-rūpa-līlā-pratyaṅga-rabhasojjvalām \|\|116\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She saw Rādhā taking shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s chest. Each of Rādhā’s limbs was bright, with incomparable beauty and grace.**

*anyonya-śleṣitāṅgau tau rādhā-kṛṣṇau dadarśa sā \|*  
*rādhāṁ sphurad-rasāṁ kṛṣṇa-sarvāṅga-svāṅga-gopitām \|\|117\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She saw Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa embracing. Rādhā was trembling with rasa and was hidden on limbs by the limbs of Kṛṣṇa.**

*cumbantīṁ kṛṣṇa-candrasyādhara-divya-sudhāśrayām \|*  
*kṛṣṇo rādhāṅga-rāgeṇa kuṅkumī-kṛta-vigrahaḥ \|\|118\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She was kissing the sweet, attractive shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s lips. Kṛṣṇa’s limbs turned red due to the color of Rādhā’s limbs.**

*ubhayor antaraṁ taṁ ca āsvādyāsvādanai rasaiḥ \|*  
*anyonya-bhāva-sambhārair anyonyāśliṣṭa-cetasoḥ \|\|119\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Embracing each other’s heart, they tasted each other with tasty rasas, filled with love for each other.**

*ity evaṁ nityānanda-svarūpo nitya-vigrahaḥ śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇa iti mantavyam \| yathā varāha-saṁhitāyāṁ pṛthivīṁ prati śrī-bhagavān varāha uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, one should consider that Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are an eternal form, a svarūpa of eternal bliss. In Varāha-saṁhitā, Lord Varāha speaks to the earth:**

*tad-aṁśu-koṭi-koṭy-aṁśās tasya kandarpa-vigrahāḥ \|*  
*jagan-mohaṁ prakurvanti tad-aṇḍāntara-saṁsthitāḥ \|\|120\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The forms of Cupid situated within the universe which bewilder the universe are a billionth part of a ray of his splendor.**

*tat-prakāśasya koṭy-aṁśā raśmayaḥ sūrya-vigrahāḥ \|*  
*tad-deha-vilasat-kānti-koṭi-koṭy-aṁśa-candramāḥ \|*  
*tac-chyāma-deha-kiraṇaiḥ parānanda-rasāmṛtaiḥ \|\|121\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the suns with their rays are a millionth part of his effulgence.All the moons are billionth part of the beauty emanating from the Lord’s body endowed with dark bodily rays, sweet with rasa of the highest bliss.**

*parātma-nitya-cid-rūpā nirguṇasyaika-kāraṇam \|*  
*tad-aṁśu-koṭi-koṭy-aṁśā jīvās tat-kiraṇātmakāḥ \|\|122\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The eternal, cognizant forms of the supreme ātmā are the sole cause beyond the guṇas. The jīvas are a billionth part of the Lord’s rays.**

*tad-aṅghri-paṅkaja-śrīman-nakha-candra-maṇi-prabham \|*  
*tad-aṁśu-pūrṇa-brahmaiva kāraṇaṁ veda durgamam \|\|123\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The complete Brahman, the cause, difficult to understand in the Vedas, is but his rays, the effulgence from the toenails of his lotus feet.**

*tad-aṅga-saurabhānanta-koṭy-aṁśā viśva-mohanāḥ \|*  
*tat-sparśa-puṣpa-gandhādi-nānā-saurabha-santamam \|\|124\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A millionth portion of the unlimited fragrance of his limbs bewilders the universe. Various fragrances of flowers are produced by their touch.**

*tat-priyā-prakṛtis tv ādyā rādhikā tasya vallabhā \|*  
*tat-kalā-koṭi-koṭy-aṁśo durgādyā tri-guṇātmikā \|*  
*tasyāṅghri-rajasaḥ sparśāt koṭi-viṣṇuḥ prajāyate \|\|125\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The original Rādhā, his dear prakṛti, is his beloved. Durgā and others, made of the guṇas, are billionth part of her portion.Millions of Viṣṇus arise from the touch of his foot dust.**

*tatra śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇasyāvaraṇa-svarūpo viṣṇuś ca \| śrī-varāha-saṁhitāyāṁ śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇasya saptamāvaraṇam āha—*

**Перевод:**

**Viṣṇu is an āvaraṇa deity for Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. In Varāha-saṁhitā,he is described as the seventh āvaraṇa (surrounding wall).**

*tad-bāhye tu pravālādi-prācīraiḥ sumanoharaiḥ \|*  
*puṣpodyānaṁ ca nānābhiś caturdikṣu samujjvalaiḥ \|\|126\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Outside in all directions there is a flower garden with attractive walls of bud with flowers.**

*śuklaṁ caturbhujaṁ viṣṇuṁ paścime dvāra-pālakam \|*  
*śaṅkha-cakra-gadā-padma-kirīṭādi-vibhūṣitam \|\|127\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the west Viṣṇu with four arms, white in complexion, decorated with conch, cakra, club, lotus and crown, is the door keeper.**

*raktaṁ caturbhujaṁ viṣṇuṁ śaṅkha-cakra-gadā-dharam \|*  
*kirīṭa-kuṇḍaloddīpitam dvārapalakam uttare \|\|128\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the north, Viṣṇu with four arms, red in complexion holding conch, cakra, and club shining with crown and earrings is the door keeper.**

*gauraṁ caturbhujaṁ viṣṇuṁ śaṅkha-cakrāmbujāyudham \|*  
*kirīṭa-kuṇḍalādyaiś ca śobhitaṁ vanamālinam \|\|129\|\|*

*pūrva-dvāre dvāra-pālaṁ gauraṁ viṣṇuṁ prakīrtitam \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-varṇaṁ caturbāhuṁ śaṅkha-cakrādi-bhūṣitam \|*  
*dakṣiṇa-dvāra-pālaṁ ca śrī-viṣṇuṁ kṛṣṇa-varṇakam \|\|130\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At the east door, Viṣṇu with four arms, golden in complexion, holding conch, cakra and lotus, beautiful with crown and earrings, wearing a forest garland, is the door keeper. Visṇu who is black in complexion, with four arms, decorated with conch, cakra and other weapons, is the southern door keeper.**

*atha durgādyā yās tad eva śrūyatām \| nārada-pañcarātre śrutiṁ vijayovāca—*

**Перевод:**

**One should hear about Durgā and others. In Narada-pañcarātra, Vijayā speaks to the śrutis:**

*ato durgā mama matā prakṛtiḥ paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*prema-kautukajautkaṇṭhā raktāṅgī vyaktatāṁ gatā \|\|131\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to me, Durgā is prakṛti of Paramātmā. She manifests from longing for the joys of prema, with red complexion in her limbs.**

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-yāmale dvādaśādhika-śatatama-paṭale śrī-vāsudevaṁ prati śrī-bhagavatī tripurovāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Kṛṣṇa-yāmala, Chapter 112, Tripurā speaks to Vāsudeva:**

*rādhā-rasa-pravacanaiḥ kṛṣṇasyāpi tathā-vidhaiḥ \|*  
*praṇayāti-rasāviṣṭau rādhā-kṛṣṇau nirantarau \|\|132\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are constantly overwhelmed with great rasa of affection due to Kṛṣṇa’s talks about Rādhā’s rasa and her talks about his rasa.**

*ubhayor eva sampattiḥ paśyatām eva yad bhavet \|*  
*premānubhūti-vibhavo mahānanda-priyaṁvadaḥ \|\|133\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For those who can see it, the wealth of the couple consists of their wealth of experience of prema and their blissful conversations.**

*tato ’nyatra ca jānāmi kvacid vastv asti kiñcana \|*  
*ato dāsītvam anayoḥ svīkṛtyāhaṁ sadānugā \|\|134\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I do not know anything else except them. I accept to be their servant, always following them.**

*etaj jagat kāraṇam apy anādi*  
*brahma prayatnaṁ parito’nvavaihi \|*  
*vedair aśeṣair upadiṣṭam etat*  
*kṛṣṇasya dāsyaṁ rahasi praśasyam \|\|135\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Know for certain that Kṛṣṇa, is the cause of the universe and is infinite Brahman. All the Vedas teach dāsya to Kṛṣṇa, which is excellent and secret.**

*trailoya-nāthādi nṛṇāṁ yathāvat \|*  
*te māṁ prapadyābhimataṁ prayāntī \|\|136\|\| ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**The lords of the three worlds and humans surrender to me and attain their desires.**

*eko nitya-kiśora evam aniśaṁ vṛndāvanābhyantare*  
*rādhāśleṣa-parāyaṇo nava-ghana-śyāmaḥ sa vaṁśī-mukhaḥ \|*  
*nityaṁ ca dvibhujas tad-aṅghri-bhajanaṁ ratnaṁ sunīlābhidhaṁ*  
*bhālordhvaṁ kuru bhūṣaṇaṁ suvinayaṁ yad rāghaveṇoditam \|\|137\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The one eternal kaiśora Kṛṣṇa, dark like a fresh cloud, with flute in his mouth, absorbed in embracing Rādhā, eternally endowed with two arms resides constantly in Vṛndāvana. Worship his feet and make that a sapphire jewel ornament. Humbly, Rāghava states this.**

*iti śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśe śrī-kṛṣṇa-parama-pūrṇatva-*  
*nirūpaṇaṁ nāma tṛtīyaṁ ratnam \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The third chapter called ‘Establishing Kṛṣṇa as the most complete form’ in Kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśa is completed.**

## Chapter 4 - Establishing Kṛṣṇa’s eternal manifestation inside Vṛndāvana

*tad atra nityo nirantara-sva-prakāśo nitya-vibhavaḥ*  
*paripūrṇānanda-mayaḥ samasta-kālātīta ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is eternal, continually self-manifested, endowed with eternal powers, full of complete bliss, beyond the influence of all time.**

*atha pravakṣye sarveśaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ nanda-nandanam \|*  
*paramānanda-sandohaṁ vṛndāvana-vinodanam \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will describe Kṛṣṇa, the lord of all beings, the son of Nanda, full of the highest bliss, who performs pastimes in Vṛndāvana.**

*atha paripūrṇānando nitya-prakāśaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candro yadīritas tad eva*  
*jñātam \| śrīman-nanda-nandanaḥ ka eṣa iti sandehaḥ \| yathā ādi-yāmale—*

**Перевод:**

**It was understood that Kṛṣṇa is full of bliss and is eternally manifested. Who is the son of Nanda? This is the doubt. In Ādi-yāmala it is said:**

*kṛṣṇo’nyo yadu-sambhūto yaḥ pūrṇaḥ so’sty ataḥ paraḥ \|*  
*vṛndāvanaṁ parityajya sa kvacin naiva gacchati \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa who is born in the Yadu dynasty is different. He who is complete (Kṛṣṇa in Vṛndāvana) is superior to him (Vāsudeva Kṛṣṇa). He never gives up Vṛndavana to go elsewhere.**

*sarvadā dvibhujaḥ so’pi na kadācic caturbhujaḥ \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At all times Kṛṣṇa has two arms and never four arms.**

*ity evaṁ yadu-vaṁśa-sambhūtaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ ka ity ucyatām \| tasmin bhāgavatā vadanti— yaḥ pūrnaḥ prasiddhaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ, sa eva nanda-nandana ity asandehaḥ \| yathā brahmāha—*

**Перевод:**

**If that is so, who is the Kṛṣṇa who was born in the Yadu dynasty? The devotees reply: He who is famous as complete is Kṛṣṇa. He is the son of Nanda. There is no doubt about this. Brahmā says:**

*vasudeva-gṛhe sākṣād bhagavān puruṣaḥ paraḥ \|*  
*janiṣyate tat-priyārthaṁ sambhavantu sura-striyaḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa, who has full potency, will personally appear as the son of Vasudeva. Therefore all the wives of the devatās should also appear in order to satisfy him.**

*tathā rāse—*

**Перевод:**

**Concerning the rāsa dance, it is said:**

*nṝṇāṁ niḥśreyasārthāya vyaktir bhagavato nṛpa \|*  
*avyayasyāprameyasya nirguṇasya guṇātmanaḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O King, the Supreme Lord is inexhaustible and immeasurable, and he is untouched by the material modes because he is their controller. His personal appearance in this world is meant for bestowing the highest benefit on humanity. (SB** [**10.29.14**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/29.14)**)**

*tathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly:**

*anugrahāya bhaktānāṁ mānuṣaṁ deham āśritaḥ \|*  
*bhajate tādṛśīḥ krīḍā yāḥ śrutvā tat paro bhavet \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the Lord assumes a human-like body to show mercy to his devotees, he engages in such pastimes. Hearing about those pastimes, a person becomes dedicated to him. (SB** [**10.33.36**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/33.36)**)**

*ato yaḥ pūrṇaḥ pūrṇatamaḥ sa eva nanda-kiśora ity asandehaḥ \| yathā śrī-bhāgavate—*

**Перевод:**

**(In verse 2) ‘He who is perfect (pūrṇa)’ means ‘he who is most perfect (pūrṇatama)’, the son of Nanda. There is no doubt. In Bhāgavatam, it is said:**

*ete cāṁśa-kalāḥ puṁsaḥ kṛṣṇas tu bhagavān svayam \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is Svayaṁ Bhagavān. All these other forms are aṁśas and kalās of Kṛṣṇa. (SB** [**1.3.28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/3.28)**)**

*tathā brahma-saṁhitāyām—*

**Перевод:**

**In Brahma-saṁhitā, it is said:**

*kṛṣṇaḥ svayaṁ samabhavat paramaḥ pumān yo*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda who, though appearing in the worlds in eternal forms such as Rāma and Nṛsiṁha with their characteristic powers, also appears personally in the supreme form of Kṛṣṇa with the most attractive pastimes.**

*tathā brahma-vaivarte dvitīyādhyāye nāradaṁ prati brahmovāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Brahma-vaivarta Purāṇa, second chapter, Brahmā says to Nārada:**

*śṛṇu putra pravakṣyāmi caritaṁ paramādbhutam \|*  
*yogeśvarasya kṛṣṇasya bhaktānugraha-kāriṇaḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O son! Listen! I will describe the most astonishing activities of Kṛṣṇa, the master of yoga, who gives mercy to the devotees.**

*satāṁ niḥśreyasārthāya duṣṭānāṁ nigrahāya ca \|*  
*vyaktir bhagavatas tasya nityasya paramātmanaḥ \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The eternal Lord, the supreme ātmā, appears in this world to punish the wicked and give benefit to the devotees.**

*vasudevasya bhāryāyāṁ devakyāṁ sa janiṣyati \|\|11\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**He will be born to Devakī, wife of Vasudeva.**

*athaitat śrutvā vādino vadanti—aho mahad āścaryam, ya eva nitya-vṛndāvana-sthaḥ sva-prakāśo nityānando nitya-pūrṇo nirīhas tasya janma iti kim ? tatra bhāgavatā vadanti— sva-prakāśasya janmāvatārāsambhava iti yad uktam, tat satyam \| kintu laukika-vyavahāratvāt janma vācyam, na tu satyam, sva-prakāśatvāt \| yathā nandaṁ yaśodāṁ ca prati mad-uddhava-vākyam*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, the philosophers say, “Oh! This is most astonishing.He who is situated eternally in Vṛndāvana, who is self-manifest, filled with eternal bliss, who is eternally perfect, devoid of action, takes birth?”**  
**The devotees reply: It is true that it is impossible for a self-manifesting being to take birth on earth. But due to worldly convention birth is spoken of, but this is not the truth since he is actually self manifesting. Uddhava says this to Nanda and Yaśodā:**

*na mātā na pitā tasya na bhāryā na sutādayaḥ \|*  
*nātmīyo na paraś cāpi na deho janma eva ca \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He has no mother, no father, no wife, children or other relatives.No one is related to him, and yet no one is a stranger to him. He has no material body and no birth. (SB** [**10.46.38**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/46.38)**)**

*na cāsya karma vā loke sad-asan-miśra-yoniṣu \|*  
*krīḍārthaṁ so ’pi sādhūnāṁ paritrāṇāya kalpate \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He has no karma. Yet to enjoy his pastimes and deliver his saintly devotees, he manifests himself in different species. (SB** [**10.46.39**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/46.39)**)**

*sattvaṁ rajas tama iti bhajate nirguṇo guṇān \|*  
*krīḍann atīto ’pi guṇaiḥ sṛjaty avati hanty ajaḥ \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Although beyond the three modes of material nature—goodness, passion and ignorance—the Lord accepts association with them as his play. Thus the unborn Supreme Lord utilizes the material modes to create, maintain and destroy. (SB** [**10.46.40**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/46.40)**)**

*athāvatāra-kāraṇam ucyate \| yathā bṛhad-vāmana-purāṇe vṛndāvana-rajo-māhātmye tad eva śruti-prārthitam avadhārya tāsāṁ stuti-vaśo bhūtvā sadayas tābhyaḥ sandarśanaṁ dadau, tat śrutaya ūcuḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**Now, the cause of the avatāra is explained. In Bṛhad-vāmana Purāṇa, in the glorification of the dust of Vṛndāvana, hearing the prayers of the śrutis, controlled by their prayers, the Lord showed himself to them. The śrutis then spoke.**

*kandarpa-koṭi-lāvaṇye tvayi dṛṣṭe manāṁsi naḥ \|*  
*kāminī-bhāvam āsādya smara-kṣubdhāny asaṁśayaḥ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When we see your form more beautiful than a million Cupids, we develop the mood of lovers and our minds become agitated by Cupid without doubt.**

*yathā tval-loka-vāsinyaḥ kāma-tattvena gopikāḥ \|*  
*bhajanti ramaṇaṁ matvā cikīrṣā janinas tathā \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**We have developed the desire to be like the gopīs who, living on your planet worship you as a lover, with amorous love.**

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said:**

*durlabho durghaṭaś caiva yuṣmākaṁ sumanorathaḥ \|*  
*mayānumoditaḥ samyak satyo bhavitum arhati \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Your desire is very rare and hard to achieve. Since it is approved by me, it will come true.**

*āgāmini viriñcau tu yāte sṛṣṭy-artham udyate \|*  
*kalpaṁ sārasvataṁ prāpya vraje gopyo bhaviṣyatha \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the next Brahmā appears in order to create, during the Sārasvata kalpa, you will become gopīs in Vraja.**

*pṛthivyāṁ bhārate kṣetre māthure mama maṇḍale \|*  
*vṛndāvane bhaviṣyāmi preyān vo rāsa-maṇḍale \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will appear in Vṛndāvana, in Mathurā, within Bhārata, on earth. I will be your lover in the rāsa dance.**

*jāra-dharmeṇa susnehaṁ sudṛḍhaṁ sarvato ’dhikam \|*  
*mayi samprāpya sarve ’pi kṛta-kṛtyā bhaviṣyatha \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Attaining great, strong affection for me, superior to that of all others, with the mood of a paramour, you all will become successful.**

*evaṁ śrutīnām abhimata-siddhy-arthaṁ vṛndāvane sva-prakāśas tad atra pramāṇam adhigamyatām—*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord appeared in Vṛndāvana to fulfill the desire of the śrutis.The proof should be understood in the following:**

*tad-darśanāhlāda-vidhūta-hṛd-rujo*  
*manorathāntaṁ śrutayo yathā yayuḥ \| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Their heartache vanquished by the joy of seeing Kṛṣṇa, the gopīshad their desires completely fulfilled, just as the śrutis had their desires fulfilled. (SB** [**10.32.13**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/32.13)**)**

*tad eva śrutayo gopyo bhūtvā śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraṁ prāpuḥ \| tatra gopyo yathā aṅga-jā nityāḥ śruti-rūpā muni-rūpa deva-kanyā iti pañcadhā \| ataḥ sa eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ sva-prakāśaḥ, na tu garbha-vāsaḥ \| tatra vādino vadanti yadi garbha-sambhavo naiva, tadā katham—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus the śrutis became gopīs and attained Kṛṣṇa. There are five types of gopīs: arising from his limbs, those who are eternal, the śrutis, sages and daughters of devatās. Thus Kṛṣṇa is self-manifesting and is not born from a womb. The philosophers say, “How is it possible that he does not appear from a womb? It is said:**

*vasudeva-gṛhe sākṣād bhagavān puruṣaḥ paraḥ \|*  
*janiṣyate tat-priyārthaṁ sambhavantu sura-striyaḥ \|\|21\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa, who has full potency, will personally appear as the son of Vasudeva. Therefore all the wives of the devatās should also appear in order to satisfy him. (SB** [**10.1.23**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/1.23)**)**

*vasudevasya bhāryāyāṁ devakyāṁ sa janiṣyati \|\| iti brahma-vaivarte pāṭhaḥ \| ataḥ sandehaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Brahma-vaivarta Purāṇa says: he will be born in Devakī, wife of Vasudeva. Thus the doubt.”**

*tad atra śrūyatām — svāyambhuva-manau pṛṣṇi-sutapā-dampatībhyāṁ tapasā śrī-bhagavān ārādhitaḥ \| putratve varo yācitaḥ \| śrī-bhagavatā caivaṁ svīkṛtaḥ \| tatas tad-vara-siddhaye svāṁśa-viṣṇo rūpaṁ darśitam \| yathā—tam adbhutaṁ bālakam ambujekṣaṇaṁ; catur-bhujaṁ śaṅkha-gadādy-udāyudham ity ādi \| tathā pitarau evaṁ rūpaṁ darśayitvā śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**One should hear the following. During Svāyambhuva’s reign, Pṛṣṇi and Sutapā worshipped the Lord by performing austerities. They requested the Lord to become their son. The Lord accepted the request. To fulfill their request, he showed the form of his svāṁśa Viṣṇu. As stated in:**

*tam adbhutaṁ bālakam ambujekṣaṇaṁ*  
*catur-bhujaṁ śaṅkha-gadādy-udāyudham*

**Перевод:**

**He was an astonishing child with lotus eyes, four arms with conch and club as his weapons. (SB** [**10.3.9**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/3.9)**)**  
**After showing the form to his parents, the Lord said:**

*etad vāṁ darśitaṁ rūpaṁ prāg-janma-smaraṇāya me \|*  
*nānyathā mad-bhavaṁ jñānaṁ martya-liṅgena jāyate \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I have shown you this form so that you will remember my previous appearances before you. You would not understand my birth if I appeared in my human-like form. (SB** [**10.3.44**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/3.44)**)**

*iti tad eva svāṁśa-viṣṇo rūpaṁ darśitam, na tu svarūpam eveti jñātavyam \| yathā sarvadā dvibhujaḥ so’pi na kadācic caturbhujaḥ iti yāmala-pramāṇam \| tathaiva \[BhP 10.90.48\] jayati jananivāso devakī-janma-vādaḥ iti janma-vāda-mātram, na tu śrī-bhagavato janma ity eva jñātavyam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The form shown was his svāmśa Viṣṇu, not his svarūpa. As the Yāmala says, sarvadā dvibhujaḥ so’pi na kadācic caturbhujaḥ: he is always with two arms and never with four arms. Similarly Bhāgavatam also says jayati jananivāso devakī-janma-vādaḥ (SB** [**10.90.48**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/90.48)**). It should be understood that he is said to have taken birth (janma-vāsdaḥ), not that he actually took birth.**

*atha kecid vādino vadanti \| viṣṇu-purāṇe brahmaṇā prārthitaḥ śrī-bhagavān kṣīrodaśāyī viṣṇur brahmaṇe sva-keśau dattavān, tau rāma-kṛṣṇau babhuvatur iti —*

**Перевод:**

**Some philosophers say that in Viṣṇu Purāṇa, Kṣirodakaśāyī Viṣṇu, requested by Brahmā, gave two of his hairs to Brahmā and they became Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma.**

*evaṁ saṁstūyamānas tu bhagavān parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*ujjahārātmanaḥ keśau sita-kṛṣṇau mahā-mune \|\|23\|\|*

*uvāca ca surān etau mat-keśau vastudhā-tale \|*  
*avatīrya bhuvo bhāraṁ kleśa-hāniṁ kariṣyataḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O great sage! While being praised, the Lord, took a white and black hair from himself. He spoke to the devatās. “These two hairs of mine will appear on earth, and will destroy the suffering and burden of the earth.”**

*ity atrāpi sandehaḥ \| tatra kārṣṇā vadanti—etad eva sāmānya-vacanam \| viśeṣo’tra śrūyatām \| evaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ prati noktam \| yataḥ sa eva sarva-bīja-svarūpaḥ svaprakāśaḥ, tasyāṁśāḥ sarve, sa kasyāṁśaḥ? ata etan naivam \|*

**Перевод:**

**There is a doubt. The followers of Kṛṣṇa say: This is a general statement. The details should be heard. This description is not stated in relation to Kṛṣṇa, for he is the original svarūpa of all forms, and is self-manifesting. Everyone is his aṁśa. Whose aṁśais he? Thus it is not so.**

*tad-aṁśau viṣṇu-balabhadrau bhaviṣyata ity uktau yathā mat-keśau bhuvo bhāra-kleśa-hāniṁ kariṣyataḥ \| etenaitad vyaktīkṛtam—yathā sthiti-kārako viṣṇur asurādīn hatvā pṛthivī-bhāra-haraṇaṁ kṛtavān \| śrī-kṛṣṇa-candrasya naiṣa prabhāvaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**It is stated that Viṣṇu and Balarāma, his aṁśas, will appear. “My two hairs will remove the suffering of the earth.” It is clear from this statement that Viṣṇu who is in charge of maintenance kills the demons and removes the burden of the earth. This is not the power of Kṛṣṇa.**

*yathā—na tasya kaścid dayitaḥ suhṛttamo na cāpriyo dveṣya upekṣya eva vā ity ādi \| yataḥ sa eva kṛṣṇa-candro nirguṇaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ \| kevalaparamānanda-svarūpaḥ \| tatra mat-keśau dvi-vacanād viṣṇur balabhadraś ca \| balarāmo yathā—rāmeti loka-ramaṇād balabhadraṁ balocchrayād, bala iti nāśaka-svabhāvāt tamo-guṇaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**It is said:**

*na tasya kaścid dayitaḥ suhṛttamo*  
*na cāpriyo dveṣya upekṣya eva vā*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord does not consider a person dear or despised.(SB** [**10.38.22**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/38.22)**) This is because Kṛṣṇa is devoid of guṇas, beyond prakṛti. He has a svarūpa of pure, ultimate bliss. The words mat-keśau in the dual number indicates Viṣṇu and Balarāma. Balarāma is described with the following words;**

*rāmeti loka-ramaṇād balabhadraṁ balocchrayād*

**Перевод:**

**He will be called Rāma because of his ability to please all the inhabitants of Gokula, and he will be known as Balabhadra because of his extensive strength. (SB** [**10.2.13**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/2.13)**) Bala indicates tamoguṇa due to his destructive nature.**

*yathā govinda-vṛndāvane, bhagavantaṁ prati balarāma uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Govinda-vṛndāvana (2.7), Balarāma speaks to the Lord:**

*ahaṁ tamo-guṇa-mayaś coditas tava māyayā \|*  
*na jāne tava tattvaṁ hi kīdṛśaṁ ca jagat-prabho \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I deal often with tamoguṇa, instigated by your māyā. O Lord of the universe! I do not know the nature of your tattva.**

*ata eva balocchrayatvād anantāṁśaḥ \| balabhadras tu duṣṭa-nigrahārtham avatīrṇaḥ \| ato dvau keśau dattau \| kintu kṛṣṇa-candraḥ sva-prakāśaḥ parama-puruṣaḥ, yathā brahmovāca—vasudeva-gṛhe sākṣād bhagavān puruṣaḥ paraḥ ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Because he has extreme strength, Ananta is his aṁśa. Balabhadra appears in order to punish the wicked. Thus two hairs were given. But Kṛṣṇa is self-manifesting, the Supreme Lord. Brahmā says:**

*vasudeva-gṛhe sākṣād bhagavān puruṣaḥ paraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord, Bhagavān, directly appeared in Vasudeva’s house. (SB** [**10.1.23**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/1.23)**)**

*vasudeva-gṛhe ity aupacāratvāt purā proktam \| tatra bhagavatā kṣīrodaśāyinā śiroruha-vyājeneti sūcitam \| pṛthivyāṁ parama-puruṣo mama śiromaṇi-svarūpo divya-vṛndāvaneśvaraḥ śrī-kṛṣṇaḥ sva-prakāśo bhaviṣyatīti \| tad-aṁśau viṣṇu-balarāmau jātau \|*

**Перевод:**

**In this statement, vasudeva-gṛhe (in Vasudeva’s house) is metaphorical, since it was already stated by Kṣīrodakaśāyī, using the pretext of hairs from his head. “The Supreme Lord, my crest jewel svarūpa, the lord of Divya-vṛndāvana, Kṛṣṇa, will appear on his own.” Thus his two aṁśas, Viṣṇu and Balarāma, appeared.**

*yathoktaṁ—avatīrṇo hi bhagavān aṁśena jagadīśvaraḥ iti \| kintu, sa bhagavān eka eva, yathā govinda-vṛndāvane—svayaṁ jyotiḥ svayaṁ kartā svayaṁ hartā svayaṁ prabhuḥ \| ato’ṁśena katham avatīrṇo’pi kāryārthena \|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus it is said avatīrṇo hi bhagavān aṁśena jagadīśvaraḥ: the lord of the universe, Bhagavān, appeared with his aṁśa. (SB** [**10.33.26**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/33.26)**) But that Bhagavān is only one person. In Govinda-vṛndāvana it is said svayaṁ jyotiḥ svayaṁ kartā svayaṁ hartā svayaṁ prabhuḥ:he is the creator, self-manifesting, the destroyer, the master. But why does Kṛṣṇa have to appear with his aṁśa? Surely for some purpose.**

*yathā saṁsthāpanāya dharmasya praśamāyetarasya ca iti \| ato viṣṇu-balarāma-svarūpāṁśeneti \| athāṁśenaika-vacanam \| dvau katham uktau? tad evam aṁśe prokte—eko dvau bahava iti mantavyam \| aṁśa-jāti-svabhāvatvāt \| tathā śrī-kṛṣṇa-yāmale—evaṁ catuḥ-ṣaṣṭhi-bhāgair avatārāḥ parātmanaḥ iti \|*  
*saṁsthāpanāya dharmasya praśamāyetarasya ca*  
*avatīrṇo hi bhagavān aṁśena jagad-īśvaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O brāhmaṇa, though full of all powers, the lord of the universe, he has descended to this earth along with Balarāma to destroy irreligion and reestablish religious principles. (SB** [**10.33.26**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/33.26)**) Thus he descended with Viṣṇu and Balarāma, the aṁśas of his svarūpa. “In the above verse aṁśa (aṁśena) is in the singular.Why do you say that two aṁśas appeared?” The word aṁśa can refer to one, two or many forms since aṁśa merely indicates a type. Thus Kṛṣṇa-yāmala says**

*evaṁ catuḥ-ṣaṣṭhi-bhāgair avatārāḥ parātmanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The avatāras of the Lord appear with sixty-four types.**

*kenacid uktaṁ—yadi janma aupacāratvāt \| tadā kathaṁ bālyādi-līlā prakaṭitā? tad eva bhaktānurodhena, yathā devakī-vasudeva-nanda-yaśodādīnām anugrahāyavātsalya-premāmṛta-pānārtham \| paraṁ ca saṁmohana-tantre—*

**Перевод:**

**Some say, “If the birth is metaphorical, why does he manifest infancy and other pastimes?” This is by the request of the devotees. It was mercy to Devakī, Vasudeva, Nanda and Yaśodā so they could drink the sweet prema of vātsalya. Furthermore, in Saṁmohana-tantra, it is said:**

*muniḥ śuciśravā nāma surarcā nāma cāparaḥ \|*  
*kuśadhvajasya brahmarṣeḥ putrau tau veda-pāragau \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kuśadhvaja had two sons the sage Śuciśravā and Surarcā. They were both learned in the Vedas.**

*ūrdhva-pādau tapo ghoraṁ ceratus try-akṣaraṁ manum \|*  
*om? haṁsa iti kṛtvaivaṁ japantau yata-mānasau \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They undertook severe austerity, standing on their heads, uttering the three-syllable mantra om? haṁsa with controlled minds.**

*dhyāyantau gokule kṛṣṇaṁ bālakaṁ daśa-vārṣikam \|*  
*kandarpa-sama-rūpeṇa tāruṇya-taruṇena ca \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They meditated on the boy Kṛṣṇa ten years old in Gokula, with a form like Cupid, very youthful.**

*paśyantīr vraja-bimbauṣṭhīr mohanatam anāratam \|*  
*tau kalpānte tanuṁ tyaktvā labdhavantau janiṁ vraje \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He enchanted the red lipped women of Vraja who constantly gazed at him. At the end of the kalpa giving up their bodies, the two attain birth in Vraja.**

*sudhīra-nāmno gopasya sute parama-śobhane \|*  
*yayor haste ca dṛśyete śārikā-śuka-vādinī \|\|30\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**They were the very beautiful daughters of a cowherd named Sudhīra. In their hands were seen a śārika and parrot who spoke.**

*evaṁ bhakta-bhāvānurodhena bālya-rūpaṁ darśitam \| yamalārjunayor*  
*mokṣaṇārthaṁ sva-sevaka-nārada-vacana-pratipālanāya \| yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus by the request of the devotees, he shows his infant form. To protect the words of his servant Nārada, to liberate the Yamalārjuna trees, he appeared as a child.**

*ṛṣer bhāgavata-mukhyasya satyaṁ kartuṁ vaco hariḥ \|*  
*jagāma śanakais tatra yatrāstāṁ yamalārjunau \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa, to fulfill the truthfulness of the words of the greatest devotee, Nārada, slowly went to that spot where the twin arjuna trees were standing.**

*devarṣir me priyatamo yad imau dhanadātmajau \|*  
*tat tathā sādhayiṣyāmi yad gītaṁ tan mahātmanā \|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Because Nārada is my dear devotee, I will do just as he has predicted concerning the two sons of Kuvera. (SB** [**10.10.24-5**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/10.24)**)**

*atha sevakānurodhena bālya-kaumāra-paugaṇḍādi-vayasā parikrīḍamānaḥ \| anyac ca nanda-yaśodayor vātsalya-bhāva-pūraṇārtham \| yathā—aho bhāgyavatī devī yaśodā nanda-gehinī ity ādi-kāraṇena bālyaṁ \| nandas tu sarveṣāṁ vraja-vāsināṁ nāyakaḥ śreṣṭhaś ca \| tasya priya-tanayo bhūtvā go-rakṣanādikaṁ kṛtam \| kiṁ tasya kiṅkarā na santi? tadā katham evaṁ kṛtam ? tad eva bhajate tādṛśīḥ krīḍā yāḥ śrutvā tat-paro bhaved iti \| etat kāraṇam iti mantavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**At the request of his servants Kṛṣṇa plays in the ages of bālya,kaumāra and paugaṇḍa. Otherwise to fulfill the vātsalya-bhāva of Nanda and Yaśodā he did so. Thus it is said that Yaśodā, wife of Nanda was most fortunate. He manifested infancy for her. Nanda is the great leader of all the people of Vraja. Kṛṣṇa became his son in order to herd his cows. “But were there not his servants? Why would he do this?”**

*bhajate tādṛśīḥ krīḍā yāḥ śrutvā tat-paro bhavet*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing about those pastimes, a person becomes dedicated to him. (SB** [**10.33.36**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/33.36)**) This is the reason for his doing this.**

*atha kenacid uktam—aye ! yadi śrī-bhagavān kṛṣṇa-candraḥ sva-prakāśaḥ sarvātmā nirīhaḥ—na tasya kaścid dayitaḥ suhṛttamo na cāpriyo dveṣya upekṣya eva vā ity ādi, tadā kathaṁ pūtanā-śakaṭa-tṛṇāvartāgha-bakādīn jaghāna ? kim etat ?*

**Перевод:**

**Someone says, “If Kṛṣṇa is self-manifesting, the ātmā of all beings, and without desire according to SB** [**10.38.22**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/38.22)**, then why did he kill Pūtanā, Śakaṭa, Tṛṇāvarta, Agha and Baka. What is this?”**

*athaitat kāraṇaṁ mattaḥ śrūyatām \| bhagavān kṛṣṇa-candraḥ sarvatra sama-darśanaḥ \| nirīhaḥ parama-rasa-mayaḥ, keṣāṁcid vadhāya saṅgata iti naiva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hear the reason from me. Bhagavān Kṛṣṇa sees equally everywhere. He is without desire or indifferent, full of the highest rasa. It is thus not proper that he kills anyone.**

*yathā śrī-śuka uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva says:**

*kāmaṁ krodhaṁ bhayaṁ sneham aikyaṁ sauhṛdam eva ca \|*  
*nityaṁ harau vidadhato yānti tan-mayatāṁ hi te \|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Persons who constantly direct their lust, anger, fear, protective affection, and feelings of impersonal oneness or friendship toward the Lord are sure to attain realization of him. (SB** [**10.29.15**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/29.15)**)**

*āgatya yena etādṛg-bhāvena śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ spṛṣṭas tam evātma-sātkarotīti \| na tu keṣāṁcit māraṇāya samudyataḥ sa eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**He who comes and touches him with that mood, only them does he make like himself. He is never enthusiastic to kill anyone.**

*asura-vadhāya susajjaḥ sa eva tad-aṁśo viṣṇuḥ pṛthivī-pālanāya? pratyuttaram—aho ! etat satyam \| kintu tvaritam āgatya viṣode kālīya-phaṇi-damanaṁ kṛtaṁ katham iti ?*

**Перевод:**

**“His aṁśa Viṣṇu for protecting the earth, is ready to kill the demon?” The answer is: That is true. “But why did he rush and subdue Kālīya in the poisonous water?**

*tatrāha—svāṁśo viṣṇus tasya sevakaḥ garuḍaḥ \| kālīyas tu nirantaraṁ garuḍasya bhayād garuḍa-mayaṁ samastaṁ dadarśa, tato garuḍo vaiṣṇavaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**The answer is this: His aṁśa is Visṇu. His servant is Garuḍa. Kālīya, always in fear of Garuḍa, saw everything as Garuḍa. Garuḍa is a devotee.**

*tad eva—*  
*śrīmat-paṅkaja-tārkṣya-phālguna-śuka-prahlāda-bhīṣmoddhava-*  
*vyāsākrūra-parāśara-dhruva-mukhān vande mukunda-priyān \|*  
*yais tīrthair iva pāvitaṁ tribhuvanaṁ ratnair ivālaṅkṛtaṁ*  
*sad-vaidyair iva rakṣitaṁ sukha-karaiś candrair ivāpyāyitam \|\|34\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to Brahmā, Garuḍa, Arjuna, Śukadeva, Prahlāda, Bhīṣma, Uddhava, Vyāsa, Akrūra, Parāśara, Dhruva, all dear to the Lord, who purify the three worlds, ornamenting the world like jewels, protecting it like good doctors, pleasing it like pleasant rays of the moon.**

*ato garuḍo viṣṇu-ratho vaiṣṇavaḥ \| vaiṣṇavo viṣṇur yathā—vaiṣṇavāl labhate bhaktiṁ bhaktyā māṁ labhate naraḥ \| tasmād vai vaiṣṇavo viṣṇuḥ ity ādi \| viṣṇur api śrī-kṛṣṇasya svāṁśaḥ \| tan-mayatvāt kālīyāyānugrahaḥ kṛtaḥ \| tenābhayaṁ dadau saḥ \| na taṁ nihatavān iti \| ata eva bālya-līlayā yat kṛtaṁ tat paropakārāya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, Garuḍa, carrier of Viṣṇu, is a devotee. The devotee is (like) Viṣṇu.**

*vaiṣṇavāl labhate bhaktiṁ bhaktyā māṁ labhate naraḥ \| tasmād vai vaiṣṇavo viṣṇuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One attains bhakti through the devotee. By bhakti, a person attains me. Therefore, the devotee is verily Viṣṇu. Viṣṇu is the svāṁśa of Kṛṣṇa. Because he is non-different, he gave mercy to Kālīya. He made Kālīya fearless. He did not kill him. What he performs in his bālya pastimes is for the benefit of others.**

*yathā—nṛṇāṁ niḥśreyasārthāya vyaktir bhagavato nṛpa ity ādi \| paraṁ tu bhakta-prema-vaśo bhūtvā tad-anurodhena ca, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, it is said nṛṇāṁ niḥśreyasārthāya vyaktir bhagavato nṛpa: His personal appearance in this world is meant for bestowing the highest benefit on humanity. (SB** [**10.29.14**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/29.14)**) Moreover becoming dependent on the devotee’s prema, he responds to the devotee’s request, as stated in:**

*na cāntar na bahir yasya na pūrvaṁ nāpi cāparam \|*  
*pūrvāparaṁ bahiś cāntar jagato yo jagac ca yaḥ \|\|35\|\|*

*taṁ matvātmajam avyaktaṁ martya-liṅgam adhokṣajam \|*  
*gopikolūkhale dāmnā babandha prākṛtaṁ yathā \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking of the invisible Lord—who has no inside or outside, has nothing previous or after him, who existed in the past and will exist in the future, inside and outside of the universe, who is the universe—as her son, appearing with a human form though he was beyond material senses—she tied him to the mortar like a normal child with rope. (SB** [**10.9.13-14**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/9.13)**)**

*evaṁ bhakta-bhakti-vaśena bandhanam api svīkṛtam \| tad eva śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In this way the Lord agrees to be bound up, controlled by the devotee’s bhakti. The Lord has said:**

*nitya-mukto’pi baddho’haṁ bhaktasya sneha-rajjubhiḥ \|*  
*ajito’pi jito’haṁ tair avaśyo’pi vaśīkṛtaḥ \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though I am eternally liberated, I am bound by the ropes of the devotee’s affection. Though unconquered, I am conquered by the devotees. Though uncontrolled, I am controlled by the devotees.**

*ata eva ca bālya-līlayā yad yat kṛtaṁ tat sarvaṁ māyayā vihitam \| śrī-bhagavatā bālya-rūpaṁ yat prakaṭitam, tat sarvaṁ māyikam na svabhāvena \| yataḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candrasya kaiśora-vayaḥ svabhāvaḥ \| kaiśora-vayo vinā yad yad rūpaṁ tad eva māyikam iti vedādi-sarva-śāstra-sammatam \| kecidbālya-rūpaṁ svabhāvaṁ matvā tad eva praśaṁsanti—bālya-rūpaṁ praśaṁsanti śrī-kṛṣṇasya kvacit kvacit iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**What he enacts in his bālya pastimes is arranged by his māyā.The bālya form manifested by the Lord is all māyā7. It is not his natural form. Kṛṣṇa’s kaiśora age is his natural form. Except of the kaiśora age, the other forms are all māyā. This is approved by all scriptures such as the Vedas. Thinking that the bālya form is his natural form, some praise that form. Bālya-rūpaṁ praśaṁsanti śrī-kṛṣṇasya kvacit kvacit: sometimes they praise the bālya form of Kṛṣṇa.**

*tad eva saṁmohana-tantre—*

**Перевод:**

**In Saṁmohana-tantra, it is said:**

*santi tasya mahābhāgā avatārāḥ sahasraśaḥ \|*  
*teṣāṁ madhye’vatārāṇāṁ bālatvam atidurlabham \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa has thousands of greatly auspicious avatāra forms. Among them, the bāla form is most rare.**

*tad atra śāstrāntare kaiśora-svabhāvaḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**In another scripture, the kaiśora form is described as his nature:**

*bālyaṁ māyāmayaṁ rūpaṁ sarva-śāstre pratiṣṭhitam \|*  
*tasmād vṛndāvanāntaḥsthaṁ kaiśoraṁ ca sudurlabham \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is established in all scriptures that the bālya form is made of māyā. Therefore, the kaiśora form situated in Vṛndāvana is most rare.**

*tathā rudra-yāmale—*

**Перевод:**

**In Rudra-yāmala it is said:**

*kuca-kalasa-pibantaṁ māyinaṁ kṛṣṇam īḍe*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the form of māyā who drinks from Yaśodā’s pot-like breast.**

*tathaiva vatsa-haraṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**In stealing the calves, it is said:**

*ambhojanma-janis tad-antara-gato māyārbhakasyeśitur*  
*draṣṭuṁ mañju mahitvam anyad api tad-vatsān ito vatsapān \|*  
*nītvānyatra kurūdvahāntaradadhāt khe ’vasthito yaḥ purā*  
*dṛṣṭvāghāsura-mokṣaṇaṁ prabhavataḥ prāptaḥ paraṁ vismayam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Mahārāja Parīkṣit! Brahmā, who was born from Viṣṇu’s lotus navel, who resides in the sky, and who had become astonished on seeing Kṛṣṇa liberate Aghāsura, came to Vṛndāvana. Taking the boys and calves to another place in order to see some other power of the Lord, who was a boy with bewildering power, Brahmā then disappeared. (SB** [**10.13.15**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/13.15)**)**

*ato bālya-vayo māyā-mayam \| sarvopari kaiśora-vayaḥ svabhāvaḥ \| tathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus the bālya form is māyā. The kaiśora form is the best and is his natural form. Similarly it is said:**

*kṛṣṇa-candra-vihārasya sthānaṁ bahutaraṁ smṛtam \|*  
*tatraiva gokulaṁ vṛndāvanaṁ parama-durlabham \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa has many places for pastimes. Among them Gokula Vṛndāvana is the rarest.**

*sarveṣām upari-sthānaṁ vṛndāvanam itīritam \|*  
*yatra kaiśora-rūpeṇa sva-prakāśaḥ svayaṁ hariḥ \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The best place is Vṛndāvana, where Kṛṣṇa himself manifests in his kaiśora form.**

*gokule bālya-bhāvas tu vṛndāraṇye kiśorakaḥ \|*  
*nānā-rūpa-dharo’nyatra sarva-śāstra-mataṁ yathā \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In Gokula, the Lord is of bālya age. In Vṛndāvana, he is of kaiśoraage. Elsewhere he has various forms. This is approved in all scriptures.**

*sarvasmād gokulaṁ śreṣṭhaṁ tasmād vṛndāvanaṁ varam \|*  
*vṛndāvanāt paraṁ sthānaṁ na kṛṣṇasya priyaṁ kvacit \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gokula is the best of all. Vṛndāvana is even greater. There is no place superior to Vṛndāvana which is dear to Kṛṣṇa.**

*vṛndāvane ca kaiśora-rūpaḥ svābhāvikaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*go-gopa-gopī-vaṁśībhir yatra krīḍati sarvadā \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In Vṛndāvana, the kaiśora form is natural. There he always plays with the cows, cowherds, gopīs and his flute.**

*tathā varāha-saṁhitāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Varāha-saṁhitā it is said:**

*vṛndāvana-vihāreṣu kṛṣṇaḥ kaiśora-vigrahaḥ \|*  
*anyāraṇyeṣu sthāneṣu bālya-paugaṇḍa-yauvanam \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is of kaiśora age in his Vṛndāvana pastimes. In other places he has bālya, paugaṇḍa and yauvana ages.**

*tathā saṁmohana-tantre—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Saṁmohana-tantra, it is said:**

*deheṣu yauvanaṁ ramyaṁ kaiśoraṁ tatra durlabham \|*  
*kiśoraṁ yatnataḥ kṛṣṇaṁ dhyāyed ānanda-vigraham \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Among his bodies, the yauvana form is charming and the kaiśoraform is rare. One should meditate carefully on the kaiśora form of Kṛṣṇa, a form of bliss.**

*tathā varāha-saṁhitāyāṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-svarūpa-nirūpaṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Varāha-saṁhitā, in describing Kṛṣṇa’s svarūpa, it is said:**

*vrajendra-niyataiśvaryo vraja-prāṇaika-vallabhaḥ \|*  
*yauvanodbhinna-kaiśora-vayaḥ svākṛti-vigrahaḥ \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The dear life of Vraja, manifesting constant powers as king of Vraja, Kṛṣṇa has a pleasing form of kaiśora age, manifesting yauvana.**

*yathā padyāvalyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**In Padyāvalī (82), it is said:**

*śyāmam eva paraṁ rūpaṁ purī madhu-purī varā \|*  
*vayaḥ kaiśorakaṁ dhyeyam ādya eva paro rasaḥ \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Among all forms, Kṛṣṇa, with complexion of a cloud, is best. Among cities Mathurā is the best. Kaiśora age should be constantly worshipped. Mādhurya-rasa is the best among spiritual rasas.**

*tathā bṛhad-vāmana-purāṇe parokṣe śrī-bhagavantaṁ saṁstutya śrutya ūcuḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**In Bṛhad-vāmana-Purāṇa, the śrutis speak to the Lord, unseen:**

*ānanda-mātram iti yad vadantīha purā-vidaḥ \|*  
*tad-rūpaṁ darśayāsmākaṁ yadi deyo varo hi naḥ \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ancient wise sages say that your form is pure bliss. Show your form to us, if you wish to give us a benediction.**

*tataḥ śruty-abhimatam avadhārya svarūpaṁ darśayati—*

**Перевод:**

**Understanding the desire of the śrutis, the Lord showed his svarūpa:**

*nānā-rāsa-rasonmatto yatra gopī-kadambakam \|*  
*tat-kadambaka-madhyasthaḥ kiśorākṛtir acyutaḥ \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This form was of kaiśora age, under a kadamba tree, intoxicated with various rasas, with groups of gopīs.**

*darśayitveti ca prāha brūta kiṁ karavāṇi vaḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭo madīyo loko’yaṁ yato nāsti paraṁ padam \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Showing that form, he said, “Say what can I do for you? You have seen my planet, to which nothing is superior.”**

*tato varāha-saṁhitāyāṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-svarūpa-nirūpaṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**In Varāha-saṁhitā, in describing Kṛṣṇa’s svarūpa, it is said:**

*sarva-devasya mantrāṇāṁ viṣṇu-mantras tu jīvanam \|*  
*śrī-viṣṇoḥ sarva-mantrāṇāṁ kṛṣṇa-mantras tu kāraṇam \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Viṣṇu mantra is the life of the mantras of all devatās. The Kṛṣṇa mantra is the cause of all Viṣṇu mantras.**

*sarveṣāṁ kṛṣṇa-mantrāṇāṁ kaiśoram atihaitukam \|*  
*kaiśoraṁ sarva-mantrāṇāṁ hetuś cūḍāmaṇir manuḥ \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All Kṛṣṇa mantras are dependent on kaiśora mantras. The kaiśora mantras are the crest jewels of all mantras.**

*ato vṛndāvanaṁ nityam \| śrī-kṛṣṇaḥ kaiśora-vigraho nitya iti jñāpanīyam \| athaitat sarvaṁ nityam ity asandehaḥ \| kaiśora-vigraho nityam iti kim abhiprāya iti tad āha—śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ paripūrṇānanda-rasa-mayo līlayā rūpavān sa-prakṛtir iti ādi-rasa-vistāraṇāya,*

**Перевод:**

**Thus Vṛndāvana is eternal. The kaiśora form of Kṛṣṇa is eternal. Without doubt, they are all eternal. What is the intention of saying that his kaiśora form is eternal? Kṛṣṇa, full with the rasaof complete bliss, accompanied by this consort, has a natural form due to his pastimes for spreading mādhurya-rasa.**

*yathā nārada-pañcarātre—tayātirasayā reme priyayā caika-rūpayā iti \|*  
*ādi-rasaḥ pradhānam eva \| ādi-rasopabhoge bālya-vayo na sambhāvyam*  
*\| yauvane tu rasādhika-kṣaṇa-pramāṇena rasasya nyūnatvam \| ataḥ*  
*kaiśora-vaya iti pūrṇam ujjvala-rase praśastam \| yataḥ kṣaṇe kṣaṇe*  
*rasasya vardhiṣṇutā bhavati \| ata ādi-rase kaiśora-vayaḥ pūrṇa-rasamayaṁ vardamānam iti jñātavyam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In Nārada-pancarātra, it is said tayātirasayā reme priyayā caika-rūpayā: he enjoys with his beloved who is filled with rasa. Ādirasa or mādhurya-rasa is the chief rasa. When he is bālya age, it is not possible for him to enjoy mādhurya-rasa. In yauvana, due to more time for experiencing rasa, the quality of rasa is less. Thus, kaiśora age is best for mādhurya-rasa since at every moment the rasa increases. It should be understood that kaiśora age, full of rasa, increases mādhurya-rasa.**

*atha kenacid uktam—aho ! yadi vṛndāvane nitya-kiśora-vayāḥ śrī-kṛṣṇas tadā katham anyatra mathurādiṣu gatavān sthitir vaibhavaś ca prakaṭitaḥ ? vṛndāvanaṁ parityajya sa kvacin naiva gacchati iti yāmala-pramāṇam \| tad atra sandehaḥ \| tatra bhāgavatā vandanti—aho ! vṛndāvane kaiśoravayasā śrī-kṛṣṇo nityam astīti satyam \| nātra sandehaḥ \| tad iti—*

**Перевод:**

**Someone may say that if Kṛṣṇa is eternally kaiśora age in Vṛndāvana, why does he go to Mathurā and other places, manifesting places and powers? Yāmala says that he never leaves Vṛndāvana. There is a doubt here. The devotees say that Kṛṣṇa of kaiśora age in Vṛndāvana stays there eternally. That is true. There is no doubt.**

*vṛndāvanād yadi gato bhagavān mukundo*  
*guñjā-pravāla-śikhi-śikhaṇḍa-kiśora-nīpāḥ \|*  
*vaṁśī-vara-vraja-vadhū-jana-dhenu-saṅghā*  
*eṣāṁ na ko’py anugato vada ko’tra hetuḥ \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When Kṛṣṇa left Vṛnāvana his guñja garland, the leaves, peacock feathers, young kadamba trees, his fine flute, the gopīs, and cows did not follow him. Please explain the cause of this.**

*ata eva śrī-rādhā-kānto’ṁśenānyatra gatavān, svarūpeṇa vṛndāvane’vasthitaḥ \| atha kecid vadanti—athaitair veśa-bhūṣābhir na gatavān, tena kiṁ sa eva gatavān ? yathā saṁmohana-tantre—dhyānasya saṁsthitir nāsti harer icchānurūpataḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus Kṛṣṇa goes elsewhere in his aṁśa form. In his svarūpa, he remains in Vṛndāvana. Some say that he did not go with his clothing and ornaments. Then did he go alone? In Saṁmohanatantra it is said dhyānasya saṁsthitir nāsti harer icchānurūpataḥ: There is no fixed rule for meditation for it is according to the Lord’s desire.**

*tatra pratyuttaram—tad eva vṛndāvanāntar vinānyatra boddhavyam \| vṛndāvane’py etair veśa-bhūṣādibhis tiṣṭhann ity asandehaḥ \| kintu ye yad-rūpaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ dhyāyanti \| teṣu tad-rūpaṁ darśayati,*

**Перевод:**

**The answer is this.**  
**This (verse of Saṁmohana tantra) should be understood to refer to other places except Vṛndāvana. He remains in Vṛdnāvana with his clothing and ornaments without doubt. But whatever form of Kṛṣṇa one meditates on, the Lord shows that form.**

*yathā bhagavad-gītāsūpaniṣatsu śrī-bhagavān uvāca—ye yathā māṁ prapadyante tāṁs tathaiva bhajāmy aham iti \| tatra sva-prakāśo vaṁśī mayūra-puccha-guñjā-pītāṁśukādibhir bhūṣita eva yair yair vṛndāvanacandra ārādhya dṛṣṭaḥ \| tais tair etair veśa-bhūṣādibhir eva :*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord says in Bhagavad-gītā, ye yathā māṁ prapadyante tāṁs tathaiva bhajāmy aham: as they surrender to me I reward accordingly. (BG** [**4.11**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/4.11)**) Those who worship Kṛṣṇa, self-manifesting, decorated with his flute,peacock feather, guñjagarland and yellow cloth, see that form with that cloth and those ornaments.**

*yathā nārada-pañcarātre vijayaṁ prati durgovāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Nāradā-pancarātra, Durgā speaks to Vijaya:**

*koṭi-candra-mukhaṁ koṭi-manmathādbhuta-vigraham \|*  
*koṭi-sūrya-pratīkāśaṁ śyāmaṁ parama-sundaram \|\|56\|\|*

*vṛndāvana-mahā-hema-maṇi-mandira-madhya-gam \|*  
*vaṁśī-vinodinaṁ gopa-sundarī-prāṇa-nāyakam \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should meditate upon Kṛṣṇa, situated in a huge golden pavilion studded with gems in Vṛndāvana, playing the flute, the hero who is the life the beautiful gopīs.**

*nānā-rasa-sudhodgāra-prema-raṅga-taraṅgiṇam \|*  
*gūḍha-marma-rasānanda-mahāmbhodhi-mahā-vidhum \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is a joyous wave of prema which spouts various sweet rasas.He is the great moon in the great ocean of bliss arising from deep heartfelt rasas.**

*rādhikā-hṛdayākūta-rasa-lāmpaṭya-vibhramam \|*  
*sudhā-taraṅgiṇī-līlā-lohitāmbuja-locanam \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His bewildering passion produces overwhelming rasa in Rādhā’s heart. His red lotus eyes play in waves of nectar.**

*dvibhujaṁ kaustubhānandi-vana-mālā-vibhūṣitam \|*  
*pītāmbara-mahā-ratna-hārābharaṇa-bhūṣitam \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is decorated with forest garland which gives joy to the Kaustubha. He has two arms, and is ornamented with huge jewel necklace and yellow cloth.**

*ratna-kuṇḍala-bhādīpyan nāsāgra-maṇi-mauktikam \|*  
*bimba-māṇikya-bandhūka-sundara-dvija-sundaram \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He shines from the glitter of his jewel earrings, and wears a pearl at the tip of his nose. He has beautiful teeth and his lips are like bimba fruit, rubies and the bandhūka flower.**

*catuḥ-sama-mahā-gandha-mohitāneka-mānasam \|*  
*barhāpīḍa-mahā-kānta-rasa-prema-mukhāmbujam \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His catuḥsama fragrance enchants the mind of many. His lotus face decorated with a peacock feather is filled with mādhurya-prema.**

*kandarpa-loka-kandarpaṁ ramaṇī-prema-vallabham \|*  
*kiṅkiṇī-svana-mañjīra-maṇi-lipta-padāmbujam \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is Cupid for the planet of Cupid, and is the dear lover of the gopīs. His lotus feet are smeared with the jingling of his jeweled ankle bells.**

*bhāvayed ātma-bhāvena paramātmānam acyutam \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-mantra-japenaiva kṛṣṇa-prema labhen naraḥ \|\|64\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**One should meditate upon Acyuta, the supreme ātmā, wholeheartedly. A person will attain Kṛṣṇa prema by chanting the Krṣṇa mantra as japa.**

*atha brahma-vaivarte pūrva-janmani nanda-yaśodayos tapo-vaśo bhūtvā śrī-bhagavān āvirbabhūva \| tadā bhagavantaṁ (vasur) dadarśa, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**In Brahma-vaivarta Purāṇa, controlled by the austerities of Nanda and Yaśodā in previous birth, the Lord appeared. Then Vasu saw the Lord.**

*tato vasur hṛṣṭa-manā dṛṣṭvā taṁ pīta-vāsasam \|*  
*mahā-marakata-śyāmaṁ śikhaṇḍābaddha-kuntalam \|\|65\|\|*

*kiśoraṁ hāra-mañjīra-valayāṅgada-bhūṣaṇam \|*  
*jita-candra-mukhaṁ devaṁ sundaraṁ subhru-nāsikam \|\|66\|\|*

*bimbādhara-puṭa-dvandva-śobhi-dantāvali-dvayam \|*  
*smitāvalokitaṁ dhīraṁ dvibhujaṁ sarva-sundaram \|\|67\|\|*

*nipatya daṇḍavad bhūmau sa nanāma janārdanam \|*  
*hṛṣyat-tanu-ruho bhaktyā kṛṣṇaṁ prati vadiṣyati \|\|68\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Falling on the ground, he offered respects to Janardana, of kaiśoraage, ornamented with necklace, ankle bells, bracelets and arm bands, whose face defeated the moon, whose nose and brows were beautiful, whose rows of teeth shone within his bimba lips, who had a smiling glance, who was grave, with two arms, and was graceful in all limbs. His hairs standing on end, he spoke to Kṛṣṇa with devotion.**

*tathā brahma-saṁhitāyām—*

**Перевод:**

**In Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.30**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.30)**), it is said:**

*veṇuṁ kvaṇantam aravinda-dalāyatākṣam*  
*barhāvataṁsam asitāmbuda-sundarāṅgam \|*  
*kandarpa-koṭi-kamanīya-viśeṣa-śobhaṁ*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda who plays a flute, whose eyes are like lotus petals, who wears a peacock feather on his head, whose bodily hue is attractive like a dark cloud, and whose beauty is more spectacular than that of millions of Cupid.**

*tathā govinda-vṛndāvane balarāmaṁ prati śrī-kṛṣṇa uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Govinda-vṛndāvana, Kṛṣṇa speaks to Balarāma:**

*śabda-brahma-mayī-vaṁśī-vadano rasa-sāgaraḥ \|*  
*vanamālī pītavāsaḥ sukuñcita-śiroruhaḥ \|\|70\|\|*

*barhibarha-kṛtottaṁsaḥ pārijātāvataṁsakaḥ \|*  
*premānanda-mayaḥ śuddhaḥ sarvadā nava-yauvanaḥ \|*  
*evaṁ rūpaḥ sadaivāhaṁ tiṣṭhāmy atraiva sarvathā \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Playing a flute which is non-different from the Vedas, an ocean of rasa, wearing a forest garland and yellow cloth, with curly hair, with peacock feather on my head, with pārijāta ornaments, filled with the bliss of prema, pure, endowed with new youth, I remain in this form always in this place only.**

*atra kenacid uktam—evaṁ-rūpeṇa vṛndāvane nityaṁ tiṣṭhatīti tadā kathaṁ mathurāṁ gata iti sarvair dṛśyate \| anantaraṁ vṛndāvane kathaṁ dṛg-gocaro naiveti sandehaḥ \| aho etat-kāraṇaṁ mattaḥ śrūyatām \| āvirbhāva-tirobhāvāv īśvarasya bodhavyau \| tad eva bhaktābhakta-rūpeṇa \| yathā brahmāṇḍa-purāṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**Some say: If this form stays eternally in Vṛndāvana, how did he go to Mathurā? Everyone saw this. Afterwards, why was he not seen in Vṛndāvana? This is a doubt. Hear the cause from me. One should understand the appearance and disappearance of the Lord. He appears to the devotee and disappears for the nondevotee. In Brahmānanda Purāṇa it is said:**

*anādeyam aheyaṁ ca rūpaṁ bhagavato hareḥ \|*  
*āvirbhāva-tirobhāvāv asyokte graha-mocane \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord does not accept and reject his form. His appearance and disappearance appear to be accepting and rejecting bodies.**

*tathā govinda-vṛndāvane tṛtīya-paṭale nārada-praśne śrī-kṛṣṇa uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Govinda-vṛndāvana, Chapter 3, Kṛṣṇa answers Nārada’s question:**

*idaṁ vṛndāvanaṁ ramyaṁ mama dhāmaiva kevalam \|*  
*atra me paśavaḥ pakṣi-mṛgāḥ kīṭā narāmarāḥ \|\|73\|\|*

*atra devāś ca bhūtāni vartante sūkṣma-rūpataḥ \|*  
*sarva-deva-mayaś cāhaṁ na tyajāmi vanaṁ kvacit \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This attractive Vṛndāvana is my only abode. My cows, birds, deer, bees and humans are all eternal. The devatās and elements exist there in subtle form. Embodying all devatās, I never leave this forest.**

*āvirbhāvas tirobhāvo bhaven me’tra yuge yuge \|*  
*tejo-mayam idaṁ ramyam adṛśyaṁ carma-cakṣuṣā \|*  
*brahmādīnāṁ surāṇāṁ ca na bhaved dṛṣṭi-gocaraḥ \|\|75\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I will appear and disappear here yuga after yuga. This effulgent, attractive place is invisible to material eyes. Brahmā and other devatās cannot see it.**

*atha yadi keṣāṁcid dṛg-gocaro naiva, tadā katham udāra-līlayā samasta-loka-gocaro bhūtvā gopa-gopībhir nānā-krīḍā-raso vistāritaḥ? atha tad eva śrūyatām—grāmya-lokena saha yat kṛtaṁ tat sva-māyayācchādito bhūtvā, kintu vṛndāvane rāsa-krīḍādi yat kṛtaṁ, tad-aṅgajā-nityā-śrutimunijā-devakanyādi gopyas tāsāṁ gocaro bhūtvā rāsa-krīḍādikaṁ kṛtavān \|*

**Перевод:**

**If Kṛṣṇa is not visible to some, how does he become visible to all with generous pastimes and expand various rasas in pastimes with the cowherds and gopīs? Please listen. What he enacted with materialistic people, he did so being covered by his māyā,but when he performed the rāsa dance in Vṛndāvana, he become visible to the gopīs born from his limbs (aṅgajā), the nitya-gopīs, śrutis, sages and devatāwomen who became gopīs, and he performed the rāsa dance.**

*sva-māyayā sva-prakāśaḥ svayam eva nānyeṣāṁ gocaraḥ katham abhūt \| tadaiva aṅgajā rādhā aṅgavan \| nityā nityaṁ santi īśvaravat \| śrutir vedās ta eva bhagavad-aṅgam eva \| eka-saptati-sahasra-munīnāṁ śata-kalpāvadhi agni-śāyyāgnibhuk-kaṭhora-tapasā vaśo bhūtvā teṣām abhimata-siddhyai tān evātmasāt-kṛtavān \| deva-kanyā brahmājñayā gopyo babhūvuḥ \| etāsāṁ nitya-gocaraḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candro nitya-vṛndāvana-stha ity asandehaḥ \| tatra pramāṇam adhigamyatām—yad eva bhagavān śrī-kṛṣṇa-candro mathurāgamana-samaye gopīḥ praty avadat tad eva—*

**Перевод:**

**Being self-manifesting by his māyā, how was he not visible to others? (He was.) Aṅgajā refers to Rādhā who is like his limbs. Nityā refers to gopīs who are eternal like the Lord himself. The śrutis are Vedas, the limbs of the Lord. Controlled by the severe austerities of the seventy thousand sages lying in fire and enduring fire for a hundred kalpas, he made them like himself to fulfill their desires.
