The devatā women became gopīs on the order of Brahmā. Kṛṣṇa, situated eternally in Vṛndāvana, is eternally visible to them without doubt. One should understand the proof. When Kṛṣṇa was going to Mathurā he spoke to the gopīs:**

*tās tathā tapyatīr vīkṣya sva-prasthāne yadūttamaḥ \|*  
*sāntvayāmāsa sa-premair āyāsya iti dautyakaiḥ \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As he departed, that best of the Yadus saw how the gopīs were lamenting, and thus he consoled them by sending messengers with this loving promise: “I will return.” (SB** [**10.39.35**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/39.35)**)**

*tathā brahma-vaivarte—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in the Brahma-vaivarta Purāṇa it is said:**

*mādhiṁ kurudhvaṁ subhagāṁ sameṣye*  
*tūrṇaṁ vilambo na mameti kṛṣṇaḥ \|*  
*itthaṁ samāśvāsya janaṁ samutsukaṁ*  
*cacāla tūrṇaṁ saha gopa-vṛndaiḥ \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Do not worry, gopīs! I will return soon. I will not delay. In this way, Kṛṣṇa consoled the worried gopīs and quickly left with the cowherds.**

*ity evaṁ sva-nigamaḥ—āyāsyāmīti \| tad eva kathaṁ vyaktaṁ nābhūt \| tad āha śrī-bhagavān kutra vā gacchati ? kutra vā āgacchati ? yathādiyāmale—vṛndāvanaṁparityajya sa kvacin naiva gacchati iti \| āyāysāmīty uktam aupacārikatvāt, vāstavaṁ naiva \| dautyakair iti vacanād etad vyaktī-kṛtam \| ity evaṁ nitya-vṛndāvana-sthaḥ śrī-rādhā-kānto’ṁśena śrī-viṣṇu-svarūpa-vāsudevenaiva gatavān syāt \| yadi vṛndāvana-tyāgo bhavet tadā punar āgamanaṁ ca na bhaviṣyaty evety ādi jñātavyam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He instructed. “I will return.” Why was this not evident? Where did the Lord go? Where did he come back? Ādi-yāmala says, “He never leaves Vṛndāvana.” Thus, when he says “I will return,” it is a metaphorical statement, not actual. This is clear in the statements of the messengers as well. Thus, Kṛṣṇa stays eternally in Vṛndāvana and goes in his aṁśa form, Vāsudeva, the svarūpaof Viṣṇu. It is understood that if he really left Vṛndāvana, he would never come back.**

*atha kecid vādino vadanti—etāsāṁ cen nitya-gocaraḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candras tadā kathaṁ uddhavaṁ prasthāpya gopīnāṁ viraha-nivāraṇaṁ kṛtavān iti \| yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Some philosophers say that if Kṛṣṇa is eternally visible to the devotees, why did he send Uddhava to remove the feelings of separation of the gopīs as in the following:**

*gacchoddhava vrajaṁ saumya pitror nau prītim āvaha \|*  
*gopīnāṁ mad-viyogādhiṁ mat-sandeśair vimocaya \|\|78\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Dear gentle Uddhava, go to Vraja and give pleasure to our parents. And also relieve the gopīs, suffering in separation from me, by giving them my message. (SB** [**10.46.3**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/46.3)**)**

*athāṅgajā-nityā-śruti-muni-rūpāḥ prati naivam \| deva-kanyā prati jñātavyam \| katham evam ? tā eva brahmājñayā deva-kanyā bhuvi samāgatya gopyo bhūtvā bhagavat-prītiṁ cakruḥ \| na tu prema-bhaktyā tapasā vā ārādhitaḥ prabhur naivaṁ brahmājñayāpi, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**It should be understood that this message was not for Rādhā, nitya-gopīs, the śrutis and sages in the form of gopīs but for the women from Svarga. Why? By the order of Brahmā, they came to earth and becoming gopīs, showed affection for the Lord. They had not worshipped the Lord with prema or austerities but went by Brahmā’s order:**

*vasudeva-gṛhe sākṣād bhagavān puruṣaḥ paraḥ \|*  
*janiṣyate tat-priyārthaṁ sambhavantv amara-striyaḥ \|\|79\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa, who has full potency, will personally appear as the son of Vasudeva. Therefore all the women of Svarga should also appear in order to satisfy him. (SB** [**10.1.23**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/1.23)**)**

*ata eva deva-kanyāḥ praty evam \| yathā deva-kanyābhir jñātaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candro mathurāṁ gataeva \| anyāsāṁ bhagavato vicchedo’sti naivam \| yathādi-yāmale—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, Uddhava’s message was for the heavenly women. The others did not feel separation from the Lord as the heavenly women did, who understood that Kṛṣṇa had gone to Mathurā. In Ādi-yāmala it is said:**

*prokteyaṁ virahāvasthā spaṣṭa-līlānusārataḥ \|*  
*kṛṣṇena viprayogaḥ syān na jātu vraja-vāsinām \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The state of separation has been described, according to the external pastimes. There would never be separation from Kṛṣṇa for the people of Vraja. (BRS** [**3.3.128**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/rupa-brs1/3.3.128)**)**

*tathā skānde mathurā-khaṇḍe –*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Skanda Purāṇa, Mathurā-khaṇḍa, it is said:**

*vatsair vatsatarībhiś ca sadā krīḍati mādhavaḥ \|*  
*vṛndāvanāntara-gataḥ sa-rāmo bālakair vṛtaḥ \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mādhava plays eternally with the calves and older calves, surrounded by cowherd boys and Balarāma within Vṛndāvana.**

*tathā brahmāṇḍe—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa, it is said:**

*kair api prema-vairāgya-bhāgbhir bhāgavatottamaiḥ \|*  
*adyāpi dṛśyate kṛṣṇaḥ krīḍan vṛndāvanāntare \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is seen playing in Vṛndāvana even today by some great devotees having detachment and prema.**

*tathaiva brahma-vaivarte nāradaṁ prati brahmāha—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Brahma-vaivarta Purāṇa, Brahmā speaks to Nārada:**

*nityaṁ krīḍati viśvātmā gopair gopībhir eva ca \|*  
*pītavāsā jagat-svāmī vanamālī smitekṣaṇā \|\| ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord of the universe, soul of the universe, wearing yellow cloth, wearing a forest garland, with smiling glances, plays eternally with the cowherds and gopīs.**

*ata eva nitya-vṛndāvana-sthaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candra iti jñātavyam \| kalpa-koṭi-mahā-tapasā prema-bhaktyā mahā-sādhanena bhakta-dṛg-gocaro bhavati \| anyathā ka eva draṣṭuṁ samarthāḥ ? nityaṁ vṛndāvane sva-prakāśa iti jñātavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, it should be understood that Kṛṣṇa is eternally situated in Vṛndāvana. He is visible to the eyes of devotees by great sādhana, by prema-bhakti and great austerities for millions of kalpas. Who else is capable of seeing him? It is understood that he is self-manifesting in Vṛndāvana eternally.**

*yathā hastāmalake—ghana-cchanna-dṛṣṭir ghana-cchannam arkaṁ yathā niṣprabhaṁ manyate cātimūḍhaḥ ity ādi \| tathaivaṁ nirantaraṁ vṛndāvane sva-prakāśaḥ sādhana-vyatirekeṇa śrī-bhagavantaṁ rādhākāntaṁ draṣṭuṁ kathaṁ yogyaḥ \| evam ajñātvā mūḍhaiḥ kathyate—śrībhagavataḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candrasya vṛndāvana-tyāga ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**In Hastāmalaka-stotram (12), it is said:**

*ghana-cchanna-dṛṣṭir ghana-cchannam arkaṁ*  
*yathā niṣprabhaṁ manyate cātimūḍhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A foolish person thinks that the sun, covered by clouds, has lost its effulgence, when his vision is covered by the clouds. He is constantly manifested in Vṛndāvana. Who is eligible to see the Lord without sādhana. Thus the fool without knowing say that Kṛṣṇa leaves Vṛndāvana.**

*vṛndāraṇye niravadhi bhagavān kṛṣṇa ātma-svarūpo*  
*go-gopībhir vilasati paramānanda-pūrṇa-prakāśaḥ \|*  
*evaṁ brahmādibhir api nigamaiḥ stūyate tat-paratvaṁ*  
*māṇikyaṁ tat kuru hṛdābharaṇaṁ rāghaveṇehitaṁ yat \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhagavān Kṛsna, whose ātmā is his svarūpa, the full manifestation of the highest bliss, plays eternally in Vṛndāvana with the cows and gopīs. His supremacy is praised by Brahmā and the Vedas. Rāghava says, “Make this ruby the ornament of your heart.”**

*iti śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśe śrī-kṛṣṇasya vṛndāvanāntar-nitya-*  
*prakāśa-nirūpaṇaṁ nāma caturthaṁ ratnam \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The fourth chapter called ‘Establishing Kṛṣṇa’s eternal manifestation inside Vṛndāvana’ in Kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśa is completed.**

## Chapter 5 - Establishing Kṛṣṇa’s kaiśora svarūpa

*tasya nandātmajasyāpi brahmaṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*aṁśāṁśāvatārāṇāṁ pravakṣye paramotsukam \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With great joy, I will explain about the aṁśa avatāras of Kṛṣṇa, incidents concerning the Supreme Lord.**

*atha vṛndāvanān mathurāṁ gataḥ ka eṣaḥ? tad ucyatām \| tad atra sa eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ sarva-sampūrṇa-prakāśas tathālpa-prakāśa iti bhedatvāt pṛthaṅ manyate, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Who went from Vṛndāvana to Mathurā? That should be explained. The same Kṛṣṇa, the complete manifestation in Vṛndāvana, is considered different due to less manifestation.**

*hariḥ pūrṇatamaḥ pūrṇataraḥ pūrṇa iti tridhā \|*  
*śreṣṭha-madhyādibhiḥ śabdair nāṭye yaḥ paripaṭhyate \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is perfect, more perfect and most perfect. He is described as best, medium or inferior in the words of dramatic literature. (BRS** [**2.1.221**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/rupa-brs1/2.1.221)**)**

*prakāśitākhila-guṇaḥ smṛtaḥ pūrṇatamo budhaiḥ \|*  
*asarva-vyañjakaḥ pūrṇataraḥ pūrṇo’lpa-darśakaḥ \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When Kṛṣṇa manifests all qualities, he is described by the intelligent as most perfect. Manifesting the qualities incompletely, he is called more perfect, and manifesting even less qualities, he is called perfect. (BRS** [**2.1.222**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/rupa-brs1/2.1.222)**)**

*ity evaṁ vṛndāvane pūrṇatamaḥ śrī-bhagavān kṛṣṇa-candraḥ svaprakāśaḥ \| anyatrālpa-prakāśaḥ tad eva pūrṇataratvena \| dvārakā-nātho vāsudevo balarāmaś ca pūrṇo’pi saha-pradyumnāniruddhādiḥ \| tad anyac ca pūrṇa-kalpatvena brahma-viṣṇu-śiva-mahāviṣṇv-ādayaḥ \| tatra viṣṇur vāsudevo yathā mathurāṁ gatavantaṁ śrī-bhagavantaṁ sahasraśiraso’nantasya kroḍe akrūro’paśyad yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, in Vṛndāvana, Bhagavān Kṛṣṇa, self-manifesting, is most perfect. In other places, he manifests less qualities and is known as more perfect (pūrṇatara). The Lord of Dvāraka, Vāsudeva, and Balarāma along with Aniruddha as well as Pradyumna and others is perfect (pūrṇa). Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Śiva and Mahā-viṣṇu, different from them, are slightly less than perfect (pūrṇa-kalpa). When the Lord went to Mathurā, Akrūra saw Vāsudeva (pūrṇatara) on Ananta.**

*tasyotsaṅge ghana-śyāmaṁ pīta-kauśeya-vāsasam \|*  
*puruṣaṁ catur-bhujaṁ śāntaṁ padma-patrāruṇekṣaṇam \|\|4\|\| ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Akrūra then saw the Lord lying peacefully on the lap of Ananta. His complexion was like a dark-blue cloud. He wore yellow garments and had four arms and reddish lotus-petal eyes. (SB** [**10.39.46**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/39.46)**)**

*tathātraiva rukmiṇi-rabhase śrī-bhagavantaṁ vāsudevaṁ prati rukmiṇy uvāca— yat-karṇa-mūlam ari-karṣaṇa nopayāyād yuṣmat-kathā mṛḍaviriñca-sabhāsu gītā \|\| ity atra viṣṇor ullekho na kṛtaḥ \| yad eva tat śrīvāsudevaḥ viṣṇur iti sūcitam \| tad-guṇo yathābṛhan-nāradīye—*

**Перевод:**

**In relation to Rukmiṇī’s love, Rukmiṇī speaks to Vāsudeva (pūrṇatara):**

*yat-karṇa-mūlam ari-karṣaṇa nopayāyād*  
*yuṣmat-kathā mṛḍa-viriñca-sabhāsu gītā*

**Перевод:**

**O infallible Kṛṣṇa, let each of the kings you named become the husband of a woman whose ears have never heard your glories, which are sung in the assemblies of Śiva and Brahmā. (SB** [**10.60.44**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/60.44)**) Though Viṣṇu is not mentioned, it is understood that Vāsudeva Viṣṇu is being described. Similarly, his qualities are described in Bṛhan-nāradīya Purāṇa:**

*agratvād atha pūrvatvāt svayambhur iti kathyate \|*  
*haraḥ saṁsāra-haraṇād vibhutvād viṣṇur ucyate \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Brahma is called Svayambhu because he is foremost in the universe and the oldest. Śiva is called Hara because he destroys saṁsāra. He is called Viṣṇu because he pervades everywhere.**

*śrī-vāsudeva-svarūpa-viṣṇor vaibhavaṁ yathā—mathurāyāṁ kaṁsavadhāya gatvā sva-vaibhavaṁ darśitam \| tad eva śrī-bhāgavate— mallānām aśanir nṛṇāṁ nara-varaḥ strīṇāṁ smaro mūrtimān ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**The powers of Vāsudeva Viṣṇu are displayed when Kṛṣṇa goes to Mathurā to kill Kaṁsa. In Bhāgavatam, it is said:**

*mallānām aśanir nṛṇāṁ nara-varaḥ strīṇāṁ smaro mūrtimān*

**Перевод:**

**He was a thunderbolt for the wrestlers, the best of men for ordinary men, and personified Cupid for the women. (SB** [**10.43.17**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/43.17)**)**

*tathaiva dvārakāyāṁ ṣoḍaśa-sahasra-strīṇāṁ gṛhe ṣoḍaśa-sahasrāṇi pumāṁso bhūtvā rarāma \| mahā-muni-nāradena dṛṣṭaḥ \| tad anu arjunaṁ prati viśva-rūpaṁ darśitam \| tathā rukmiṇī-haraṇa-pārijāta-haraṇādimahā-mahā-yuddhe caturbhujatvaṁ prakaṭitam \| garuḍa-vāhanaś ca śaṅkha-cakra-gadā-padma-dhārī ca \| ataḥ śrī-rudra uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly, this form became sixteen thousand in the palaces of sixteen thousand wives in Dvārakā and enjoyed with them. This was seen by the great sage Nārada. Then he showed the universal form to Arjuna. He manifested four arms in a great battle in procuring the pārijāta tree and in kidnapping Rukmiṇī. He accepts Garuḍa as his carrier and holds the conch, cakra, club and lotus. Rudra says:**

*yaḥ paraṁ raṁhasaḥ sākṣāt tri-guṇāj jīva-saṁjñitāt \|*  
*bhagavantaṁ vāsudevaṁ prapannaḥ sa priyo hi me \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He who has completely surrendered to Vāsudeva, who is superior to māyā, to the jīva-śakti, is dear to me. (SB** [**4.24.28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/24.28)**)**

*ity ādi pramāṇena jñātavyam viṣṇor vāsudevasya paraḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ \| viṣṇur api vaikuṇṭheśvaraḥ \| ata eva mahāviṣṇuḥ \| tathātra pramāṇam āha — yadā vaikuṇṭha-dvāri jaya-vijayayor brahma-śāpo babhūva tadā bhagavatā śrī-viṣṇunā ājñaptam—yadi mayi śatru-bhāvaṁ kṛtvā patathaḥ, tadā janma-trayānantaraṁ yuvām ahaṁ mocayiṣyāmi iti \| ato jaya-vijayau hiraṇyākṣa-hiraṇyakaśipu-rūpau bhūtvā jātau \| viṣṇur api varāha-nṛsiṁha-rūpau bhūtvā tau jaghāna \| yugāntare punas tau rāvaṇakumbhakarṇau bhūtvā jātau \| viṣṇur api śrī-rāma-lakṣmaṇa-rūpau bhūtvā tau jaghāna \| janmāntare punas tau śiśupāla-dantavakra-rūpau bhūtvā jātau \| viṣṇur api śrī-vāsudeva-balabhadrau bhūtvā tau jaghāna \| evaṁ janma-trayānantaraṁ jaya-vijayau mukto babhūvatuḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**From these authoritative statements, it is understood that Kṛṣṇa is superior to Vāsudeva Viṣṇu. Viṣṇu is the lord of Vaikuṇṭha and thus Maha-viṣṇu. That is also proved in the following. When Jaya and Vijaya were cursed by the Kumāras at the door of Vaikuṇṭha, they were ordered by Viṣṇu, “If you both become my enemies and fall, after three births I will liberate you.” Then Jaya and Vijaya were born as Hiraṇyakṣa and Hiraṇyakaśipu. Viṣṇu also, in the forms of Varāha and Nṛsiṁha killed them. In another yuga, they were born as Rāvaṇa and Kumbhakarṇa. Viṣṇu as Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa killed them. Born again, they became Śiśupāla and Dantavakra. Viṣṇu as Vāsudeva and Balarāma (since they appeared in order to kill the demons) killed them. In this way after three births, Jaya and Vijaya were liberated.**

*ata etat sarvaṁ viṣṇor vaibhavam eva \| vṛndāvana-candrasya naitat, yataḥ sarveṣāṁ paraḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candrasya vaibhavaḥ \| ato nārada-pañcarātre rāmādayo’vatārāś ca kāryārthe sambhavanti ca iti \| ata ete sarve śrī-kṛṣṇasyāṁśā viṣṇu-svarūpāvatārā iti jñāpanīyam \| yato divya-vṛndāvana-stho bhagavān śri-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus all these are the powers of Viṣṇu, not Kṛṣṇa, since Kṛṣṇa’s powers are superior to everyone else. According to Nārada Pancarātra, Rāma and other avatāras arise for a purpose. Thus, it is understood that they are all aṁsas of Kṛṣṇa, avatāras which are Viṣṇu in svarūpa, since the Lord situated in Divya-vṛndāvana is Kṛṣṇa.**

*yathā śrī-goloka-saṁhitāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**As it is said in Goloka-saṁhitā:**

*jyotirmayaṁ tatra brahma tatra vṛndāvanaṁ mahat \|*  
*tatraiva rādhikā devī sarva-śakti-namaskṛtā \|*  
*tatraiva bhagavān kṛṣṇaḥ sarva-deva-śiromaṇiḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is resplendent Brahman. Within this place is great Vṛndāvana. There Rādhikā, offered homage by all śaktis, exists.There Bhagavān Kṛṣṇa, the crest jewel of all devatās, exists.**

*ata eva mahā-viṣṇur vaibhavam eva \| tatra kecid vadanti—sarvam etad asya śrī-vṛndāvana-candrasya vaibhavam eva \| sa eva kiṁ na vibhuḥ? aho bhadram uktam \| tasyāṁśa-vaibhavā viṣṇur vāsudevādayaḥ \| aho yadi naivam, tadā kathaṁ vāsudevo brahmādibhiḥ prārthito vaikuṇṭhaṁ gantum?*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, Mahā-viṣṇu is a powerful manifestation. Some say that all these are ultimately powers of Vṛndāvana Kṛṣṇa. Is he not all powerful? That is well said. Viṣṇu, Vāsudeva and others are his aṁśas and powerful manifestations. If it were not so, how could Vāsudeva be requested by Brahmā to go to Vaikuṇṭha?**

*yathā śrī-brahmovāca—*

**Перевод:**

**As Brahmā said:**

*bhūmer bhārāvatārāya purā vijñāpitaḥ prabho \|*  
*tvam asmābhir aśeṣātman tat tathaivopapāditam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! Previously we requested you to remove the burden of the earth. O unlimited Lord! That request has certainly been fulfilled. (SB** [**11.6.21**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/6.21)**)**

*śarac-chataṁ vyatīyāya pañca-viṁśādhikaṁ prabho \|*  
*tataḥ sva-dhāma paramaṁ viśasva yadi manyase*  
*sa-lokāl loka-pālān naḥ pāhi vaikuṇṭha-kiṅkarān \|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**You have passed one hundred and twenty-five years. Support of all things! If you so desire, kindly return now to your supreme abode. At the same time, we humbly beg that you always protect us and the inhabitants of the planets since we are your servants.(SB** [**11.6.25, 27**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/6.25)**)**

*ataḥ sarvopādhi-rahitasya śrī-kṛṣṇa-candrasya naitat \| sa evaṁ divya-vṛndāvaneśaḥ pūrṇa-rasamayaḥ \| ananta-vaikuṇṭha-nāthās tasya kiṅkarāḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**This does not refer to Kṛṣṇa, devoid of all upādhis. In this way he is the lord of Divya-vṛndāvana, and is full of rasa. The unlimited Lords of Vaikuṇṭha are his servants.**

*yathā brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**As stated in Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.43-4**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.43)**):**

*goloka-nāmni nija-dhāmni tale ca tasya*  
*devi-maheśa-hari-dhāmasu teṣu teṣu \|*  
*te te prabhāva nicayā vihitāś ca yena*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda by whom respective powers are given to the abodes of Durgā, Śiva and Viṣṇu, which are situated below his own planet of Goloka.**

*tathā govinda-vṛndāvane balabhadraṁ prati śrī-kṛṣṇa uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Govinda-vṛndāvana, Kṛṣṇa speaks to Balarāma:**

*aham ātmā paraṁ brahma sac-cid-ānanda-vigrahaḥ \|*  
*sadā-śiva-mahā-viṣṇu-brahma-rudrādi-kārakaḥ \|*  
*narākṛtir nitya-rūpī vaṁśī-vādya-priyaḥ sadā \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am ātmā, Supreme Brahman, the form of sac-cid-ānanda, the cause of Sadāśiva, Mahā-viṣṇu, Brahmā, Rudra and others. I have a human form which is eternal. I am fond of playing the flute.**

*ata eva teṣāṁ sarveṣāṁ paraḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candra eva \| yathā brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ brahmaṇaḥ stutiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus Kṛṣṇa is superior to all of them. Brahmā thus praises him in Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.41**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.41)**):**

*māyā hi yasya jagad-aṇḍa-śatāni-sūte*  
*traiguṇya-tad-viṣaya-veda-vitāyamānā \|*  
*sattvāvalambi para-sattvaṁ viśuddha-sattvam*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda whose material energy māyā, consisting of the three guṇas described elaborately in the Vedas, produces hundreds of universes, while he himself is pure sattva (viśuddha-sattva existence) — which is the cause of the material sattva — which manifests his blissful form.**

*tatra sattvāvalambī mahā-viṣṇuḥ \| para-sattvo vāsudevaḥ \| viśuddha-sattvo govindaḥ \| sa eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ \| tad iti \| tamo-rajobhyāṁ saṁvalitaṁ sattvaṁ asminn astīti sattvāvalambī viṣṇur vāsudeva eva \| viṣṇunā yathā sṛṣṭiṁ kartuṁ māyā vistāritā \| ato viṣṇu-māyā-ruddhā sṛṣṭiḥ \| lakṣmī-sarasvaty-ādi-parivāra iti rajo-guṇaḥ \| tamasā nānā-daitya-saṁhāraḥ kṛtaḥ \| yathā daityārir janārdano madhusūdana iti nāmnā jñātavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahā-viṣṇu supports sattva (sattvāvalambī). Vāsudeva is beyond sattva (para-sattva). Govinda is viśuddha-sattva. That is Kṛṣṇa. Supporting sattva means sattva endowed with tamas and rajas exists in him. Mahā-viṣṇu spreads out māyā to create the universe.The creation is covered by Viṣṇu’s māyā. He is attended by Lakṣmī, Sarasvatī, etc., thus there is rajo-guṇa8. The various demons are destroyed by tamas. Thus Viṣṇu is known by names like Daityāri (enemy of the demons), Janārdana and Madhusūdana.**

*tad eva vāsudevaś ca rajasā kṛta-puryāṁ dvārakāyāṁ ṣoḍaśa-sahasra-mahiṣīṣu ṣaṭ-pañcāśat-koṭi-sva-vaṁśo vistāritaḥ \| tamasā kaṁsa-narakādy-asura-vadhaḥ kṛtaḥ \| sattvena pṛthivīṁ pālayati \| ataḥ sattva-guṇo viṣṇuḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vāsudeva, by rajas, manifested his dynasty of 560 million with sixteen thousand queens in Dvārakā. He killed Kaṁsa and Naraka by tamas. Viṣṇu protects the world by sattva. Thus Viṣṇu is said to be sattva-guṇa.**

*evaṁ sattvāvalambī mahā-viṣṇur yathā brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**In this way, Brahma-saṁhitā says that Mahā-viṣṇu supports sattva-guna:**

*haimāny aṇḍāni jātāni mahā bhūtāvṛtāni tu \|*  
*praty aṇḍam evam ekāṁśād ekāṁśād viśati svayam \|*  
*sahasra-mūrdhā viśvātmā mahā-viṣṇuḥ sanātanaḥ \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They then take the form of golden eggs, the primitive forms of universes, covered with the five great elements before they combine. Eternal Mahā-viṣṇu, the soul of the universes, with countless heads, enters into each of these universal eggs by means of expanded forms (of Viṣṇu, Brahmā and Śiva).**

*vāmāṅgād asṛjad viṣṇuṁ dakṣiṇāṅgāt prajāpatim \|*  
*jyotir liṅga-mayaṁ śambhuṁ kūrca-deśād avāsṛjat \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From his left side Mahā-viṣṇu created Viṣṇu and from his right side Brahmā. He created the effulgent form of Śiva from between his eyebrows.**

*ahaṅkārātmakaṁ viśvaṁ tasmād etad vyajāyata \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then the universe became filled with ahaṅkāra by the agency of Śiva.**

*iti rajo-guṇaḥ \| sattvena sarvam etad vahaty eva \| ato rajo-guṇa-saṁvalita-sattvo mahā-viṣṇuḥ \| ata eva viśuddha-sattvaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ \| tathā hi brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**This is rajoguṇa (involving creation). By sattva, the Lord maintains everything. Thus sattva endowed with rajo-guṇa is Mahā-viṣṇu. Thus viśuddha-sattva is Kṛṣṇa. In Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.47-48**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.47)**) it is said:**

*yaḥ kāraṇārṇava-jale bhajati sma yoga-nidrām ananta-jagad-aṇḍa-sa-roma-kūpaḥ \|*  
*ādhāra-śaktim avalambya parāṁ sva-mūrtiṁgovindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\| ity ādi*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda who, as Mahā-viṣṇu, enjoys yoga-nidrā in the waters of the Kāraṇa ocean while holding unlimited universes in the pores of his skin and lying upon the supporting energy of Śeṣa, another spiritual form of himself.**

*yasyaika niśvasita kālam athāvalambya*  
*jīvanti loma-vilajā jagad-aṇḍa-nāthāḥ\|*  
*viṣṇur mahān sa iha yasya kalā-viśeṣo*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\| ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda whose first expansion is Mahā-viṣṇu, who determines the appearance and disappearance of the rulers within the universe (even Kṣirodakaśāyī-viṣṇu) by his exhalation and inhalation of breath.**

*para-sattvo vāsudevo yathā nārada-pañcarātre—*

**Перевод:**

**In Narada-pancarātra, para-sattva Vāsudeva is described:**

*divyātidivya-śrī-dehaṁ kāla-māyādy-agocaram \|*  
*śvetadvīpeśvaraṁ pūrṇaṁ vāsudevaṁ caturbhujam \|\|17\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Complete Vāsudeva has four arms, is the Lord of Śvetadvīpa, is endowed with a most attractive body and is beyond time and māyā.**

*viśuddha-sattvo govindo, yathā śakra-stutiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**Viśuddha-sattva Govinda is described in Indra’s prayers:**

*viśuddha-sattvaṁ tava dhāma śāntaṁ*  
*tapo-mayaṁ dhvasta-rajas-tamaskam \|*  
*māyā-mayo ’yaṁ guṇa-sampravāho*  
*na vidyate te ’grahaṇānubandhaḥ \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Your form, a manifestation of pure goodness, is undisturbed by change, shines with knowledge and is devoid of passion and ignorance. Your succession of qualities has no connection with māyā since they are accepted by you. (SB** [**10.27.4**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/27.4)**)**

*tathā vāsudevopaniṣadi—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Vāsudeva Upaniṣad, it is said:**

*yad-rūpam advayaṁ brahma madhyādy-anta-vivarjitam \|*  
*sva-prabhaṁ sac-cid-ānandaṁ bhaktyā jānāti cāvyayam \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By bhakti, one knows his form which is non-dual Brahman, devoid of beginning, middle and end, self-effulgent, full of saccid-ānanda, and indestructible.**

*tathā brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.41**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.41)**), it is said:**

*māyā hi yasya jagad-aṇḍa-śatāni-sūte*  
*traiguṇya-tad-viṣaya-veda-vitāyamānā \|*  
*sattvāvalambi para-sattvaṁ viśuddha-sattvam*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda whose material energy māyā, consisting of the three guṇas described elaborately in the Vedas, produces hundreds of universes, while he himself is para sattva and pure sattva (viśuddha-sattva existence).**

*tathā nārada-pañcarātre—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Nārada-pancarātra, it is said:**

*dvibhujaṁ tu ghana-śyāmaṁ kiśoraṁ vana-mālinam \|*  
*divyābharaṇa-divyāṅgaṁ gopa-kanyā-gaṇāvṛtam \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He has two arms, is dark like a cloud, of kaiśora age, wearing a forest garland. He has attractive ornaments on his attractive limbs and is surrounded by gopīs.**

*dayitaṁ prema-bhaktānām advaitaṁ brahma-vādinām \|*  
*mīna-kūrmādayo yasya aṁśāṁśāḥ sarva-devatāḥ \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is beloved of the devotees in prema and is nondual for the brahma-vādīs. Matsya, Kūrma and all devatās are aṁśas of his aṁśas.**

*tataḥ sac-cid-ānanda-svarūpo viśuddha-sattvo govindaḥ \| sa eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ sva-prakāśo divya-vṛndāvaneśo nitya-vṛndāvane prakāśo’bhūd iti veda-vedāntādibhir nirdiṣṭam \| tathā hi brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**Govinda is viśuddha-sattva, with a svarūpa of sac-cid-ānanda. The Vedas and Vedānta (Upaniṣads) have confirmed that he is Kṛṣṇa, self-manifesting, the Lord of Divya-vṛndavana, who has appeared in Nityā-vṛndāvana. In Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.1**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.1)**) it is said:**

*īśvaraḥ paramaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ sac-cid-ānanda-vigrahaḥ \|*  
*anādir ādir govindaḥ sarva-kāraṇa-kāraṇam \|\|23\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is the supreme controller. He is the enjoyer of innumerable consorts, and the possessor of an eternal form of knowledge and bliss. Though, he is without origin, he is the source of all other forms of God, and is the cause of all causes. He is Govinda, the boy who cares for the cows.**

*kintv evaṁ śrutaṁ tal-lakṣaṇam \| yady aṅga-cihnena jana-dṛg-gocarī-bhavati tadā pratīyate \| tatra śravaṇād darśanaṁ śreṣṭham \| darśanāt sparśanam ity ādi \| tad eva pāda-cihnena jñāyate \| yathā padma-purāṇe nāradaṁ prati brahmovāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus the qualities of Kṛṣṇa have been declared in scriptures. When he becomes visible with his markings, he is then perceived. Seeing is better than hearing about him. Touching him is superior to seeing him. He is known by his foot marks. In Padma Purāṇa, Brahmā speaks to Nārada:**

*śṛṇu nārada vakṣyāmi pādayoś cihna-lakṣaṇam \|*  
*bhagavat-kṛṣṇa-rūpasya hy ānandaika-ghanasya ca \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Please listen. I will describe the marks on the two feet of Bhagavān Kṛṣṇa, full of bliss. O sinless one!**

*avatārā hy asaṅkhyeyāḥ kathitā me tavānagha \|*  
*paraṁ samyak pravakṣyāmi kṛṣṇas tu bhagavān svayam \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I have described unlimited avatāras to you. Now I will speak about Kṛṣṇa, Svayam Bhagavān directly.**

*devānāṁ kārya-siddhy-artham ṛṣīṇāṁ ca tathaiva ca \|*  
*āvirbhūtas tu bhagavān svānāṁ priya-cikīrṣayā \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa has appeared to do activities for the devatās and sages and for pleasing his devotees.**

*yair eva jñāyate devo bhagavān bhakta-vatsalaḥ \|*  
*tāny ahaṁ veda nānyo’sti satyam etan mayoditam \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I know those marks by which Kṛṣṇa, affectionate to his devotees, is known. I speak the truth. There is nothing else.**

*ṣoḍaśaiva tu cihnāni mayā dṛṣṭāni tat-pade \|*  
*dakṣiṇe cāṣṭa-cihnāni itare sapta eva ca \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I have seen sixteen marks on his feet. On his right foot are eight marks and on his left foot are seven marks.**

*dhvajāḥ padmaṁ tathā vajram aṅkuśo yava eva ca \|*  
*svastikaṁ cordhva-rekhā ca aṣṭa-koṇas tathaiva ca \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On his right foot can be seen the marks of the flag, lotus, thunderbolt, goad, barley corn, svastika, an upward line and an octagon.**

*saptānyāni pravakṣyāmi sāmprataṁ vaiṣṇavottama \|*  
*indra-cāpaṁ trikoṇaṁ ca kalasaṁ cārdha-candrakam \|\|30\|\|*

*ambaraṁ matsya-cihnaṁ ca goṣpadaṁ saptamaṁ smṛtam \|*  
*jambū-phala-samākāraṁ dṛśyate yatra kutracit \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O best of the Vaiṣṇavas! Now I will tell you about the seven marks on his left foot. There is a rainbow, a triangle, a pot, a crescent moon, a compass, a fish and a hoof print of a cow. Sometimes a jambu fruit is seen.**

*aṅkāny etāni bho vidvan dṛśyante tu yadā kadā \|*  
*kṛṣṇākhyaṁ tu paraṁ brahma bhuvi jātaṁ na saṁśayaḥ \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O learned one! Whenever these marks are seen, Supreme Brahman known as Kṛṣṇa has appeared on earth without doubt.**

*etāni vatsa cihnāni dṛṣṭāni ca śrutāni ca \|*  
*vedāgra-kathitāny eva punaḥ kiṁ kathayāmy aham \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O child! These marks are seen and heard about, described in the best of the Vedas. What more should I describe?**

*purāṇāntare śaṅkha-cakrātapatrādi-cihna-trayaṁ ca \| yathā cādi-vārāhe mathurā-maṇḍala-māhātmye—*

**Перевод:**

**In another Purāṇa, the marks of conch, cakra and umbrella are also described. In Ādi-varāha Purāṇa, in the glorification of Mathurā, it is said:**

*yatra kṛṣṇena sañcaritaṁ krīḍitaṁ ca yathā-sukham \|*  
*cakrāṅkita-padā tena sthāne brahma-maye śubhe \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The auspicious place made of Brahman where Kṛṣṇa joyfully played is imprinted with his foot marked with a cakra.**

*yathā krama-dīpikāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**In Krama-dīpikā (3.15) it is said:**

*matsyāṅkuśāri-dara-ketu-yavābja-vajra*  
*saṁlakṣitāruṇa-karāṅghri-talābhirāmam \|*

**Перевод:**

**His charming red hands and feet are marked with fish, goad, cakra, conch, flag, barley corn, lotus, and thunderbolt.**

*ari-daraṁ cakra-śaṅkham \| iti matsya-dhvajātapatraṁ ceti rūpeṇa cihnitaṁ caraṇa-dvayam iti \| etac cihna-trayeṇonaviṁśati-cihnāni śrī-bhagavac-caraṇa-kamale nirdiṣṭānīti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ari-dara mean cakra and conch. It is also said that his feet are also marked with fish, flag and umbrella. With these three marks (conch, cakra, umbrella) his feet are thus marked with nineteen symbols.**

*dvayaṁ vātha trayaṁ vātha catvāri pañca eva ca \|*  
*dṛśyate vaiṣṇava-śreṣṭha avatāre kathañcana \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O best of devotees! Sometimes in an avatāra, two, three, four or five of these marks are seen.**

*athāparaṁ ca vatsa-haraṇe mahāścaryaṁ dṛṣṭvā brahmāha—*

**Перевод:**

**After stealing the calves, Brahmā saw something most astonishing and said:**

*adyaiva tvad ṛte’sya kiṁ mama na te māyātvam ādarśitam*  
*eko’si prathamaṁ tato vraja-suhṛd-vatsāḥ samastā api \|*  
*tāvanto’si caturbhujās tad akhilaiḥ sākaṁ mayopāsitās*  
*tāvanty eva jaganty abhūs tad amitaṁ brahmādvayaṁ śiṣyate \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Today, apart from you, the calves and cowherd boys and fourarmed forms you manifested, all that I saw of the material realm and the beings worshipping you was nothing but your māyā.First you were alone, then you were all the calves and boys and then you became the four-armed forms in all the universes which were worshipped by persons all in the universe including me.Then you remained as the unlimited Brahman beyond anything else. (SB** [**10.14.18**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.18)**)**

*ityādi śravaṇa-darśanānudhyāna-nānāpramāṇenaiva jñāpanīyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**This is understood by various proofs such as śabda, pratyakṣaand anumāna.**

*atha vāsudevādayo brahmādayo matsya-kūrmādayaḥ ka ity ucyatām \| tad eva bhāgāṁśa-kalā-śaktyāveśatvena nirūpitāḥ \| yatha śrī-kṛṣṇa-yāmale—*

**Перевод:**

**Who are Vāsudeva, Brahmā and Matsya and Kūrma? These should be described. They are defined as bhāga, aṁśa, kalā, śakti and āveśa of the Lord. In Kṛṣṇa-yāmala, it is said:**

*bhāgas tv ardhaṁ tad-ardhaṁ ca aṁśa ity abhidhīyate \|*  
*tad-ardhaṁ kulam ākhyātaṁ kalā tasyārdham ucyate \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The bhāga manifests half the powers of Kṛṣṇa. The aṁśa manifests half of that. The kula manifests half of that and the kalā manifests half of that.**

*tad-ardhaṁ śaktir ākhyātā āveśaḥ syāt tad-ardhakaḥ \|*  
*evaṁ catuḥ-ṣaṣṭi-bhāgair avatārāḥ parātmanaḥ \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Half of that is called śakti. The āveśa manifests half of that. The avatāras of the Lord manifest in this way as the sixty-fourth part.**

*tan-nirūpaṇam āha—tad-ardha-bhāgo rādhā \| tad yathā padma-purāṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**This is described. The half bhāga is Rādhā. In Padma Purāṇa, it is said:**

*ādyā śaktiḥ svayaṁ rādhā mukundārdhāṅga-saṅgatā \|*  
*suśīlā sugatiḥ sādhvī vṛndāvana-vilāsinī \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The original śakti is Rādhā, half of Mukunda’s body. She is of gentle nature, chaste and plays in Vṛndāvana.**

*tathā saṁmohana-tantre prathama-paṭale—*

**Перевод:**

**In Saṁmohana-tantra, Chapter 1, it is said:**

*pūrṇānanda-svarūpaṁ yat tan nityaṁ netarat punaḥ \|*  
*tad-ānanda-mayī rādhā tad-ānanda-mayo hariḥ \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa has a svarūpa full of complete bliss eternally. It is not otherwise. Rādhā is filled with his bliss and Kṛsna is filled with her bliss.**

*na bhautiko deha-bandhas tayor ānanda-rūpayoḥ \|*  
*ekaṁ brahma dvidhā-bhūtaṁ yogināṁ jñāna-hetave \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The connection with the body for the couple, who are forms of bliss is not material. One Brahman has become two for the knowledge of yogīs.**

*dāhakena yathā vahnau vahniṁ prāpya vijṛmbhate \|*  
*śakti-śaktimator aikhyaṁ yathā jñeyaṁ manīṣibhiḥ \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wise know that the śakti and śaktiman are one just as fire is one with the spark in the expanded fire.**

*tathārdhāṅgāt samutpannā ardhāṅga-svarūpā rādhā \| yathā Govinda-vṛndāvane balarāmaṁ prati śrī-kṛṣṇa uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā is thus the svarūpa of half-body of Kṛṣṇa arising from half of his body. Kṛṣṇa speaks to Balarāma in Govinda-vṛndāvana:**

*śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi balarāma yathā mama \|*  
*tribhaṅgatvaṁ ca tāṁ vaṁśīṁ gṛhītvā hṛṣṭa-mānasaḥ \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Balarāma! Please listen. I will describe to you my three-fold bending form. Taking my flute joyfully,**

*divya-nīpāṅghri-patale maṇi-baddhe mahā-prabhe \|*  
*suvarṇa-vedikā-madhye nirmale pratinirmale \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At the base of an attractive kadamba tree, on a greatly effulgent, golden platform bound with gems, most pure,**

*sampaśyann ātmanātmānaṁ svayam eva vimohitaḥ \|*  
*etasminn eva samaye yāto me hṛdaye rasaḥ \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I became bewildered in mind while looking at my reflection, because of the taste in my heart that arose at that time.**

*śṛṅgārākhyaḥ sukhamayaḥ sarva-lokaika-mohanaḥ \|*  
*ātmānaṁ rantum icchāmi nārītvaṁ manasepsitam \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Filled with the joy of mādhurya-rasa, which enchants the whole world, I desired to enjoy myself, as a woman, which was desired in my mind.**

*iti sañcintite citte manas tatra svatāṁ gatam \|*  
*rasād ānanda ānandād anubhāva-vibodhinī \|*  
*svayam ātmā dvidhābhūtā paramānanda-rūpiṇī \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking in that way in my heart, my mind thought internally. From rasa arises bliss. From bliss, one manifests anubhāvas.Spontaneously my ātmā divided in two with a form of the highest bliss.**

*rasa-svarūpiṇī devī vāmāṁśena vinirgatā \|*  
*vidyut-puñja-nibhā gaurī divyābharaṇa-bhūṣitā \|*  
*kṛṣṇārdha-svarūpā rādhā sarva-śakti-mayī smṛtā \|\|48\|\| ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**The very svarūpa of rasa manifested from my left side. She was golden, like lightning, decorated with attractive ornaments. Rādhā is half of Kṛṣṇa’s body, full of all śaktis.**

*tathā śrī-kṛṣṇa-yāmale caturdaśādhika-śatatama-paṭale śrī-vāsudevaṁ prati tripurovāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Kṛṣṇa-yāmala, Chapter 114, Tripurā speaks to Vāsudeva.**

*a-kāreṇocyate kṛṣṇa u-kāreṇaiva rādhikā \|*  
*kalayātmā kalābhijñā vāsanā-vara-vigrahāt \|*  
*binduvattvaṁ paraṁ tattvam anayoḥ pāda-cāraṇe \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The syllable a indicates Kṛṣna. The syllable u indicates Rādhā, wise in the arts, non-different from Kṛṣṇa as his part, since she is the form of his desire. The syllable ṁ indicates the supreme tattvaat the lotus feet of the couple.**

*tathā govinda-vṛndāvane dvitīya-paṭale balabhadraṁ prati śrī-kṛṣṇa uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Govinda-vṛndāvana, Chapter 2, Kṛṣṇa says to Balarāma:**

*tri-tattva-rūpiṇī sā tu rādhikā mama vallabhā \|*  
*prakṛteḥ para evāhaṁ sāpi śakti-svarūpiṇī \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā, my beloved is the form of three tattvas. I am beyond prakṛti and so is she, being the svarūpa of my śakti.**

*prakāśa-traya-rūpeṇa nirguṇākāra-cit-paraḥ \|*  
*evaṁ sarvatra sarveśaḥ sāpi sarveśvareśvarī \|*  
*kriyā-rūpeṇa sā proktā dvayoḥ sama-rasātmikā \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am supreme knowledge, the controller of all, with form beyond the guṇas manifesting in three forms. She also is the supreme controller, with form of kriyā, having equivalent rasa as the other two śaktis (jñāna and icchā śaktis).**

*ity evaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇārdha-bhāgo rādhā sarva-śakti-svarūpā ca \| tathā saṁmohana-tantre nārada-stutiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus Rādhā is half of Kṛṣṇa, the svarūpa of all śaktis. In Saṁmohana-tantra, Nārada offers praise:**

*kā tvam āścarya-vibhave brahma-rudrādi-durgame \|*  
*yogīndrāṇāṁ dhyāna-pathaṁ na tvaṁ spṛśasi kutracit \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Who are you? O wealth of astonishment, difficult to understand for Brahmā and Śiva. You never touch the path of the yogīs’ meditation.**

*icchā-śaktir jñāna-śaktiḥ kriyā-śaktis tatheśituḥ \|*  
*tavāṁśa-mātram ity evam aṇīyāṁśaḥ pravartate \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord’s icchā-śakti, jñāna-śakti and kriyā-śakti are your aṁśas. In this way, the small aṁśas function.**

*yā yā vibhūtayo’cintyāḥ śaktayaś cāru-māyinaḥ \|*  
*pareśasya mahā-viṣṇos tāḥ sarvās te kalākalāḥ \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the vibhūtis and inconceivable śaktis, most enchanting, of the Supreme Lord, Mahā-viṣṇu, are portions of portions of you.**

*iti sarvāḥ śaktayaḥ śrī-rādhāyāṁ vidyante \|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus all śaktis reside in Rādhā.**

*atha kenacid uktam—ādyā-śaktir bhagavato durgeti sarvatra khyātiḥ \| katham anyā ? tad atrāvadhīyatāṁ varāha-saṁhitāyāṁ saptāvaraṇa-vivaraṇe vṛndāvana-sthāna-nirūpaṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**Someone says that the original śakti of the Lord is Durgā. This is well known. How is that someone else is called the original śakti? The truth should be understood from Varāha-saṁhitā, in describing the seven āvaraṇas, while defining Vṛndāvana:**

*tatropari ca māṇikya-svarṇa-siṁhāsane sthitam \|*  
*aṣṭādalārūṇāmbhojaṁ tatraiva sukha-nirmitam \|\|55\|\|*

*govindasya priyaṁ sthānaṁ kim asya mahimocyate \|*  
*śrī-govindaṁ tu tatrasthaṁ vallavī-vṛnda-vallabham \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There was a ruby and gold throne placed upon a charming eight petal lotus. How can one describe the glory of this dear place of Govinda? Govinda, dear to the gopīs, was situated there.**

*tat-sparśa-gandha-puṣpādi-nānā-saurabha-sannibham \|\|57\|\|*

*tat-priyā prakṛtis tv ādyā rādhikā tasya vallabhā \|*  
*tat-kalā-koṭi-koṭy-aṁśā durgādyā triguṇātmikā \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the fragrance of his touch, the place had the fragrance of various flowers. Rādhā, dear to him, is the original prakṛti. Durgā and others who supervise the three guṇas, are a millionth portion of a portion of Rādhā.**

*sarva-śaktiḥ śrī-bhagavatā kṛṣṇena rādhāyām āropitā, abhedatvāt \| svayaṁ nirviṇṇaḥ parama-rasa-mayaḥ paramānanda-svarūpaḥ \| nirguṇaḥ prakṛteḥ paro nitya-prakāśas tathāpi rādhāyāś cābhedatvāt tasmin bhagavati sarva-śaktitvaṁ sa-guṇatvaṁ prākṛtatvaṁ nirūpitam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All śaktis are imparted to Rādhā by Kṛṣṇa, Bhagavān since the two are non-different. He is personally indifferent to matter, and full of the highest rasa, the svarūpa of the highest bliss. He is devoid of the guṇas, beyond prakṛti, eternally manifest. Still because he is non-different from Rādhā, within him exist all śaktis, all guṇasand all prakṛti (Durgā, etc., are her aṁśas).**

*sarva-śaktir yathā—kriyā-śaktir icchā-śaktir jñāna-śaktir iti tridhā \| tatra kriyā-śaktir yathā—brahma-viṣṇu-maheśa-mahāviṣṇu-nārāyaṇādayaḥ \| yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**All śaktis means the three śaktis: kriyā, icchā and jñāna. Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Śiva, Mahā-viṣṇu, Nārāyaṇa, etc., deal with kriyā-śakti:**

*sattvaṁ rajas tama iti nirguṇasya guṇās trayaḥ \|*  
*sthiti-sarga-nirodheṣu gṛhītā māyayā vibhoḥ \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The three guṇas of sattva, rajas and tamas, belonging to the Lord without guṇas, are accepted by the māyā of the Lord for creation, maintenance and destruction. (SB** [**2.5.18**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/5.18)**)**

*tathā brahma-vaivarte—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in the Brahma-vaivarta Purāṇa it is said:**

*evaṁ pratyaṇḍakaṁ brahmā ko’haṁ jānāmi kiṁ vibho \|*  
*rajo-guṇa-prabhāvo’haṁ sṛjāmy etat punaḥ punaḥ \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā exists in each universe. Who am I? What do I know, O Lord? I am the power of rajo-guṇa and create the universe again and again.**

*sattvastho bhagavān viṣṇuḥ pāti sarvaṁ carācaram \|*  
*rudra-rūpī ca kalpānte saṁharaty etad eva hi \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Viṣṇu, Bhagavān, situated in sattva, maintains all moving and nonmoving beings.Rudra destroys the universe at the end of the kalpa.**

*evaṁ pravartitaṁ cakraṁ nityaṁ cānityavan mune \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O sage! The temporary cycle repeats itself continually.**

*mahāviṣṇur yathā brahma-saṁhitāyām \[5.14-16\]—*

**Перевод:**

**Mahā-viṣṇu is described in Brahma-saṁhitā:**

*sahasra-mūrdhā viśvātmā mahā-viṣṇuḥ sanātanaḥ \|*  
*vāmāṅgād asṛjad viṣṇuṁ dakṣiṇāṅgāt prajāpatim \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Eternal Mahā-viṣṇu, with countless heads, is the soul of the universes. From his left side Mahā-viṣṇu created Viṣṇu and from his right side Brahmā.**

*jyotir liṅga-mayaṁ śambhuṁ kūrca-deśād avāsṛjat \|*  
*ahaṅkārātmakaṁ viśvaṁ tasmād etad vyajāyata \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He created the effulgent form of Śiva from between his eyebrows. Then the universe became filled with ahaṅkāra by the agency of Śiva.**

*nārāyaṇo, yathā drumila uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Drumila describes Nārāyaṇa:**

*bhūtair yadā pañcabhir ātma-sṛṣṭaiḥ*  
*puraṁ virājaṁ viracayya tasmin \|*  
*svāṁśena viṣṭaḥ puruṣābhidhānam*  
*avāpa nārāyaṇa ādi-devaḥ \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the primeval Nārāyaṇa accepted the form called puruṣa, he created the universe from the five elements produced from himself and then entered within the universe by his own portion.(SB** [**11.4.3**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/4.3)**)**

*ayam eva mahāviṣṇuḥ śrī-kṛṣṇasya kalāḥ, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Maha-viṣṇu is the kalā of Kṛṣṇa:**

*viṣṇur mahān sa iha yasya kalā-viśeṣo*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda whose first expansion is Mahā-viṣṇu.**

*ādi-devo govindaḥ \| iti kriyā-śaktiḥ \| athecchā-śaktir yathā brahma-saṁhitāyām —*

**Перевод:**

**The ādi-deva (in (SB** [**11.4.3**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/4.3)**) is Govinda. Thus, the kriyā-śakti has been described. Now the icchā-śakti will be described. In Brahma-saṁhitā (5.44) it is said:**

*sṛṣṭi sthiti pralaya sādhana śaktir ekā*  
*chāyeva yasya bhuvanāni bibharti durgā \|*  
*icchānurūpam api yasya ca ceṣṭate sā*  
*govindam ādi puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda whose energy called Durgā, possessing the powers of carrying out creation, maintenance and destruction, supports all the worlds, but who, being just his shadow, acts only according to his will.**

*atha śrī-bhagavad-gītāsūpaniṣatsu śrī-bhagavān uvāca —mayādhyakṣeṇa*  
*prakṛtiḥ sūyate sa-carācaram iti icchā-śaktiḥ śrī-bhagavatī durgā \|*

**Перевод:**

**In Bhagavad-gītā, the Lord says:**

*mayādhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate sa-carācaram*

**Перевод:**

**Prakṛti under my control produces all moving and nonmoving beings. (BG** [**9.10**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/9.10)**) This is the icchā-śakti, Goddess Durgā.**

*atha jñāna-śaktir yathā śrī-bhagavad-avadhāna-mātreṇa sṛṣṭer udbhavaḥ prabhavaḥ pralayaś ca bhavati, yathā śruter vākya-vṛttau*

**Перевод:**

**Now jñāna-śakti will be described. Just by the awareness of the Lord, creation, maintenance and destruction of the universe takes place. In the Vākya-vṛtti (19), it is said:**

*anāpanna-vikāraḥ sann ayaskāntavad eva yaḥ \|*  
*buddhyādīṁś cālayet pratyak so’ham ity avadhāraya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Meditate that “I am the one changeless, innermost self that moves the intellect, etc., as a magnet does the iron filings.”**

*ayaskānta-sannidhāne lauhaṁ ca calati yathā ayaskānto na kiñcit karoti, na kiñcit pālayati \| na kiñcit saṁharati ca ity evaṁ jñāna-śaktiḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In proximity to a magnet, iron moves but the magnet does not do anything. Similarly, the Lord does not create, maintain or destroy the universe (but inspires all these). This is his jñāna-śakti.**

*athāṁśa-bhāgo yathā vāsudeva-saṅkarṣaṇa-pradyumnāniruddhādayaḥ \|*  
*yathā varāha-saṁhitāyāṁ śrī-bhagavān varāha uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**The aṁśa-bhāgas, Vāsudeva, Sankarṣaṇa, Pradyumna, Aniruddha and others, are now described. In Varāha-saṁhitā, Varāha speaks:**

*rādhayā saha govindaṁ svarṇa-siṁhāsana-sthitam \|*  
*pūrvokta-rūpa-lāvaṇyaṁ divya-bhūṣā-srag-ambaram \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Govinda, with great beauty and charm previously described, wearing attractive ornaments, garland and clothing, is situated on a golden throne with Rādhā.**

*evaṁ bhagavataḥ saptāvaraṇasya pañcamāvaraṇaṁ, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**The fifth āvaraṇa of the seven āvaraṇas of the Lord are described:**

*tad-bāhye svarṇa-prācīre koṭi-sūrya-samujjvale \|*  
*caturdikṣu mahodyāna-mañju-saurabha-mohite \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Outside the fourth āvaraṇa there is a gold wall, brighter than a million suns, enchanting with the attractive fragrance of a huge garden in all directions.**

*paścime sammukhe śrīmat-pārijāta-drumāśraye \|*  
*tatrādhas tu svarṇa-pīṭhe svarṇa-mandira-maṇḍite \|\|69\|\|*

*tan-madhye maṇi-māṇikya-ratna-siṁhāsanojjvale \|*  
*tatropari parānandaṁ vāsudevaṁ jagad-gurum \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Facing west, under a pārijāta tree on a golden platform, in a golden, ornamented pavilion sits Vāsudeva, guru of the universe, full of the highest bliss on shining throne made of gems and rubies.**

*śaṅkha-cakra-gadā-padma-dhāriṇaṁ vana-mālinam \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He holds conch, cakra, club and lotus and wears a forest garland.**

*rukmiṇī satyabhāmā ca nāgnajityā sulakṣaṇā \|\|72\|\|*

*mitravindā sunandā ca tathā jāmbavatī priyā \|*  
*suśīlā cāṣṭa-mahiṣī vāsudevāgrataḥ sthitāḥ \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rukmiṇī, Satyabhāmā, Nāgnajitī, Sulakṣaṇā, Mitravindā, Sunandā, dear Jāmbavatī, Suśīlā, the eight queens, are situated in front of Vāsudeva.**

*uddhavādyāḥ pāriṣadā vṛtās tad-bhakti-tat-parāḥ \|*  
*uttare divya udyāne hari-candana-saṁsthite \|*  
*suvistīrṇe svarṇa-pīṭhe maṇi-maṇḍapa-maṇḍite \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava and other associates, all with great devotion, surround him in a huge northern garden, endowed with hari-candana trees, with a golden platform in a jeweled pavilion.**

*tatrādhastu svarṇa-pīṭhe maṇi-maṇḍapa-maṇḍite \|*  
*tan-madhye hema-nirmāṇa-dale siṁhāsanojjavale \|\|75\|\|*

*tatraiva saha revatyā saṅkarṣaṇa-halāyudham \|*  
*īśvarasya priyānantam abhinna-guṇa-rūpiṇam \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Below that, on a shining throne made of gold petals, within jeweled pavilion on golden base, resides Sankarṣaṇa with a plough, who is infinitely dear to the Lord, with similar qualities, accompanied by Revatī.**

*śuddha-sphaṭika-saṁkāśaṁ raktāmbuja-dalekṣaṇam \|*  
*nīla-paṭṭa-dharaṁ snigdhaṁ divya-bhūṣā-srag-ambaram \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He has a complexion of pure crystal and red lotus eyes. He wears charming blue cloth and attractive ornaments and garland.**

*madhu-pāne sad-āsaktaṁ madhu-ghūrṇita-locanam \|*  
*pravīra-dakṣiṇe bhāge mañjunābhyantara-sthite \|\|78\|\|*

*santāna-vṛkṣa-mūle tu maṇi-mandira-maṇḍitam \|*

**Перевод:**

**He is attached to drinking honey and his eyes roll because of intoxication. To the south, situated within that charming area, at the base of a santāna tree is a jeweled pavilion.**

*tan-madhye maṇi-māṇikya-divya-siṁhāsanojjvale \|*  
*pradyumnaṁ sa-ratiṁ devaṁ tatropari samāsthitam \|\|79\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Pradyumna along with Rati is joyfully situated on an attractive golden throne studded with rubies and gems amidst that.**

*jagan-mohana-saundarya-sāra-śreṇī-rasātmakam \|*  
*asitāmbuja-puñjābham aravinda-dalekṣaṇam \|*  
*pūrvodyāne mahāraṇye sura-druma-samāśraye \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is filled with great rasa, the essence of beauty that enchants the world. He has the complexion of a blue lotus and lotus eyes, in a huge forest in the eastern garden, under a desire tree.**

*tasyādhas tu mahā-pīṭhe hema-maṇḍapa-maṇḍite \|*  
*tasya madhya-sthite rājad-divya-siṁhāsanojjvale \|\|81\|\|*

*śrīmatyā ūṣayā śrīmad-aniruddhaṁ jagat-patim \|*  
*sāndrānandaṁ ghana-śyāmaṁ susnigdha-nīla-kuntalam \|*  
*nīlotpala-dala-snigdhaṁ cāru-cañcala-locanam \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On a huge platform under it, in a golden pavilion, on an attractive, shining throne, sits Aniruddha, lord of the universe, with beautiful Ūṣā. He is dark in complexion, with shiny black locks, and filled with intense bliss. He has beautiful, restless eyes like the petals of a blue lotus.**

*priya-bhṛtya-gaṇārādhyaṁ yantra-saṅgītaka-priyam \|*  
*pūrṇa-brahma-rasānandaṁ śuddha-sattva-svarūpiṇam \|\|83\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**He is worshipped by affectionate servants and is fond of instrumental music. He is filled with the full bliss of Brahman.He is the very form of śuddha-sattva.**

*evaṁ śrī-vāsudevādayaḥ śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇasyāvaraṇenety aṁśa-bhāgāḥ \| tathā śrī-kṛṣṇa-yāmale—*

**Перевод:**

**Vāsudeva and others, being the āvaraṇa of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, are aṁśa-bhāgas. In Kṛṣṇa-yāmala it is said:**

*turīyātīta evāsau śrī-kṛṣṇaḥ prema-nāyakaḥ \|*  
*pañca-bhedai ramaty atra sarva-tejo-mayaḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*turīyātīta evāsau turīyatvaṁ nigadyate \|\|84\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa, surpassing turīya, the state of pure consciousness, the hero of prema, full of all splendor, enjoys in five ways. He is beyond turīya, and is called turīyatva.**

*atha brahmādayaḥ ke ? iti yad uktaṁ tad evāṁśa-bhāgaḥ \| tathā bṛhan-nāradīye prathama-ślokaḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**Previously it was said “Who are Brahmā and others?” They are aṁśa-bhāgas. In Bṛhan-nāradīya Purāṇa, first verse, it is said:**

*vande vṛndāvanāsīnam indirānanda-vigraham \|*  
*upendraṁ sāndra-kāruṇyaṁ parānanda-vibhuṁ param \|\|85\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to Kṛṣṇa, sitting in Vṛndāvana, the form giving bliss to Lakṣmī. Full of intense mercy and the highest bliss, the supreme.**

*brahma-viṣṇu-maheśākhyā yasyāṁśā loka-sādhakāḥ \|*  
*tam ādi-devaṁ cid-rūpaṁ viśuddhaṁ paramaṁ bhaje \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His aṁśas are Brahmā, Viṣṇu, and Maheśa, who govern the world. I worship him, the original Lord, with spiritual form, pure, the highest entity.**

*tad atra indirānanda-vigraham iti viśeṣaṇaṁ katham ? tatrāha—śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ prāptuṁ lakṣmīs tapati \| yathā śrī-saṁkṣepa-bhāgavatāmṛte—*

**Перевод:**

**Why is he called the form who gives bliss to Lakṣmī? Lakṣmī performed austerities to attain Kṛṣṇa. It is said in Laghu-bhāgavatāmṛta:**

*sadā vakṣaḥ-sthalasthāpi vaikuṇṭeśitur indirā \|*  
*kṛṣṇoraḥ-spṛhayāsyaiva rūpaṁ vivṛṇute’dhikam \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lakṣmī, though always fixed on the chest of Nārāyaṇa, lord of Vaikuṇṭha, by desiring Kṛṣṇa’s chest, showed the superiority of Kṛṣṇa’s form.**

*paurāṇikam upākhyānam atra saṅkṣipya likhyate \|\|88\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The story from the Purāṇas is written in abbreviated form.**

*śrīḥ prekṣya kṛṣṇa-saundaryaṁ tatra lubdhā tatas tapaḥ \|*  
*kurvatīṁ prāha tāṁ kṛṣṇaḥ kiṁ te tapasi kāraṇam \|\|89\|\|*

*vijihīrṣe tvayā goṣṭhe gopī-rūpeti sābravīt \|*  
*tad durlabham iti proktā lakṣmīs taṁ punar abravīt \|\|90\|\|*

*svarṇa-rekheva te nātha vastum icchāmi vakṣasi \|*  
*evam astv iti sā tasya tad-rūpā vakṣasi sthitā \|\|91\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lakṣmī seeing the beauty of Kṛṣṇa, desired to attain him. Seeing her perform austerities, Kṛṣṇa asked, “Why are you doing austerities?” She said, “I desire to perform pastimes with you in Vṛndāvana.” Kṛṣṇa said, “That is rarely achieved.” She said, “I desire to be a golden line on your chest.” He said, “Let that be!” She then became situated on his chest as a golden line.**

*yathoktaṁ śrī-daśame nāgapatnībhiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**In the Tenth Canto, the wives of Kālīya say:**

*kasyānubhāvo’sya na deva vidmahe*  
*tavāṅghri-reṇu-sparśādhikāraḥ \|*  
*yad-vāñchayā śrīr lalanācarat tapo*  
*vihāya kāmān suciraṁ dhṛta-vratā \|\|92\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, we do not know how the serpent Kāliya has attained this great opportunity of being touched by the dust of your lotus feet. For this end, the goddess of fortune performed austerities for centuries, giving up all other desires and taking austere vows. (SB** [**10.16.36**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/16.36)**)**

*nāmno’pi mahimetasya sarvato’dhika īryate \|\|93\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even the name of Kṛṣṇa is considered to be far superior to Nārāyaṇa’s name. (Laghu-bhāgavatāmṛta)**

*ataḥ svayaṁ-padādibhyo bhagavān kṛṣṇa eva hi \|*  
*svayaṁ-rūpa iti vyaktaṁ śrīmad-bhāgavatādiṣu \|\|94\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, by relating the word svayam three times with Kṛṣṇa alone, the supreme position of his form as svayaṁ-rūpa is made clear in the Bhāgavatam and other scriptures. (Laghu-bhāgavatāmṛta)**

*yathā brahma-saṁhitāyām — lakṣmī-sahasra-śata-sambhrama-sevyamānaṁ govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi ity evam indirānanda-vigraham iti viśeṣaṇaṁ ca \|*

**Перевод:**

**In Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.29**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.29)**), the phrase lakṣmī-sahasra-śata-sambhrama-sevyamānaṁ govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi: I worship Govinda who is served with devotion by hundreds of thousands of Lakṣmīs is actually a description of indirānanda-vigraham (a form which gives bliss to Lakṣmīs).**

*atha viṣṇu-mahāviṣṇu-brahma-śiva-matsya-kūrmādaya iti bhagavataḥ śrī-rādhā-kāntasyāṁśa-kula-kalā-śakty-āveśādiṣu vartante \| eteṣāṁ aṁśādīnāṁ nirṇayaṁ kartuṁ kartā śrī-bhagavān eva nānyaḥ \| purāṇādiṣu yad dṛśyate, tad atra likhyate \| yathā brahma-saṁhitāyām—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus forms like Viṣṇu, Mahā-viṣṇu, Brahmā, Śiva, Matsya, and Kūrma exist as aṁśas, kulas, kalās, śaktis and āveśas of Rādha’s beloved, Kṛṣṇa. Bhagavān ascertains these aṁśas, etc., and no one else. What is seen in Purāṇas has been written here. In Brahmā-saṁhitā (**[**5.48**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.48)**) it is said:**

*yasyaika niśvasita kālam athāvalambya*  
*jīvanti loma vilajā jagad aṇḍa nāthāḥ \|*  
*viṣṇur mahān sa iha yasya kalā viśeṣo*  
*govindam ādi puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|95\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda, whose first expansion is Mahā-viṣṇu, who determines the appearance and disappearance of the rulers within the universe (even Kṣirodakaśāyī-viṣṇu) by his exhalation and inhalation of breath.**

*tathā varāha-saṁhitāyāṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-svarūpa-vivaraṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Varāha-saṁhitā, in the description of Kṛṣṇa’s svarūpa, it is said:**

*dhvaja-vajrāṅkuśāmbhoja-karāṅghritala-śobhitam \|*  
*nakhendu-kiraṇa-śreṇī-pūrṇa-brahmaika-kāraṇam \|\|96\|\|*

*kecid vadanti tad-raśmi-brahma-cid-rūpam avyayam \|*  
*tad-aṁśāṁśaṁ mahāviṣṇuṁ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ \|\|97\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some say he is the cause of complete Brahman through the rays from his toenails, with feet and hands decorated with flag, thunderbolt, goad and lotus, whose rays are indestructible brahman. The wise say that a portion of his portion is Mahā-viṣṇu.**

*tathā tatraiva śrī-kṛṣṇasya māhātmya-kathana-prasaṅge pṛthivīṁ prati śrī-bhagavān varāha uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In the same Varāha-saṁhitā, in telling the glories of Kṛṣṇa, Lord Varāha speaks to the earth:**

*yad-aṅghri-nakha-candrāṁśu-mahimānto na vidyate \|*  
*tan māhātmyaṁ kiyad devi procyate tvaṁ sadā śṛṇu \|\|98\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Always listen to the glories of the person who has no end to the greatness of the rays from his toenails which are explained.**

*ādyanta-rahitaḥ sūkṣma-sthūlātītaḥ parātparaḥ \|*  
*svayaṁ-jyotiḥ svayaṁ kartā svayaṁ hartā svayaṁ prabhuḥ \|\|99\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is without beginning or end, is beyond subtle and gross, is supreme, is self-manifesting, is the creator and destroyer and the master.**

*kaṭākṣa-mātra-brahmāṇḍa-koṭi-sṛṣṭi-vināśa-kṛt \|*  
*sadā-śiva-mahāviṣṇu-rudra-brahmādi-kārakaḥ \|*  
*narākṛtir nitya-rūpī vaṁśī-vādya-priyaḥ sadā \|\|100\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He creates and destroys millions of universes just by his glance. He is the source of Sadāśiva, Mahā-viṣṇu, Rudra and Brahmā. He has a human form which is eternal and is fond of playing the flute.**

*tathā nārada-pañcarātre nāradānanta-saṁvāde bhakti-rahasye—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Nārada-pañcarātra, in a conversation between Nārada and Ananta, in speaking of secret of bhakti it is said:**

*tāmra-parṇī-nadī-tīre drāviḍe’sti kim adbhutam \|*  
*bhaktir mūrtimatī jātā malaya-dhvaja-mandire \|\|101\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the bank of the Tāmraparṇi in south India, there is something astonishing. Bhakti personified was born in the house of Malayadhvaja.**

*nāmnā premṇā sadānandā dhyāyantī puruṣottamam \|*  
*tal-loka-vāsinaṁ devaṁ vṛndāraṇya-purandaram \|\|102\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Always blissful, she meditated in prema on the supreme person, the king of Vṛndāvana, living on his planet.**

*divyātidivyaṁ śrī-dehaṁ kāla-māyādya-gocaram \|*  
*dayitaṁ prema-bhaktānām advaitaṁ brahma-vādinām \|*

**Перевод:**

**He was most attractive, with beautiful body, untouched by time and māyā, beloved for the prema-bhaktas and advaita (non-dual) for the brahma-vādīs.**

*mīna-kūrmādayo yasyā aṁśāṁśāḥ sarva-devatāḥ \|\|103\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Portions of his aṁśas are Matsya, Kūrma and all the devatās.**

*tathaivātra prema-tattva-nirūpaṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**In the same Nārada-pañcarātra, in defining prema it is said:**

*sāṅkhya-tattvaṁ pravakṣyāmi ātma-tattvaṁ viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*bhaktiṁ muktiṁ vadiṣyāmi prema-tattvaṁ vadāmy aham \|\|104\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will speak to you about the tattva of Sāṅkhya and particularly ātmā. I will speak about bhakti and mukti. I will speak about the tattva of prema.**

*guptam advaya-nirlepaṁ saccidānanda-vigraham \|*  
*brahmāṇḍa-koṭi-koṭīnāṁ sraṣṭāraṁ pālakaṁ vibhum \|\|105\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will speak about he who is hidden, non-dual and uncontaminated, with a form of sac-cid-ānanda, creating and protecting millions upon millions of universes, and all-pervading.**

*brahma-viṣṇu-maheśānāṁ nāthānāṁ nātham advayam \|*  
*ananta-phaṇā-māṇikya-sevitaṁ caraṇāmbujam \|\|106\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is the master of Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Maheśa, who is non-dual and whose feet are served by Ananta’s jeweled hoods.**

*ato yāvad evāvatāra-svarūpās te sarve śrī-kṛṣṇa-candrasyāṁśa-kalādayaḥ \| yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus how many ever avatāras exist, they are all aṁśas and kalās of Kṛṣṇa.**

*rāmādi-mūrtiṣu kalā-niyamena tiṣṭhan*  
*nānāvatāram akarod bhuvaneṣu kintu \|*  
*kṛṣṇaḥ svayaṁ samabhavat paramaḥ pumān yo*  
*govindam ādi puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|107\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda who, though appearing in the worlds in eternal forms such as Rāma and Nṛsiṁha with their characteristic powers, also appears personally in the supreme form of Kṛṣṇa with the most attractive pastimes. (Brahma-saṁhitā** [**5.38**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.38)**)**

*atha yadi śrī-kṛṣṇaḥ parama-pumān nirīhaḥ \| tasyāvatārāḥ katham ? tad āha, yathā nārada-pañcarātre gṛhyopaniṣadi—*

**Перевод:**

**If Kṛṣṇa, the supreme person, is without desire, why does he become an avatāra? In Nārada-pancarātra, Gṛhyopaniṣad it is said:**

*rāmādayo’vatārāś ca kāryārthe sakalā bhuvi \|*  
*bhārāvatārā bhūmyāś ca mahā-bhāra-vināśanāḥ \|\|108\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rāma and other avatāras all appear on earth for certain works. They relieve earth of its burden, destroying the great burden.**

*tad eva śrī-vāsudevādayaḥ pṛthvī-bhāra-haraṇāya \| brahmādayaḥ sṛjana-pālana-saṁharaṇāya, matsyas tu vedoddharaṇāya, kūrmas tu mandara-dhāraṇāya, varāhas tu pṛthivy-uddhārāya hiraṇyākṣa-vadhāya ca, nṛsiṁhas tu hiraṇyakaśipu-vadhāya, vāmanas tu bali-cchlanāya, paraśurāmas tu pṛthvī-niḥkṣatrī-karaṇāya, śrī-rāmas tu rāvaṇādi-rākṣasavadhāya, balarāmas tu pralambādi-mahā-mahā-daitya-vadhāya, buddhas tu bhūta-dayā-vistāraṇāya, kalkī ca mleccha-saṁharaṇāya \| pareśatvaṁ kalkino’pi viṣṇu-dharme vilokyate \| tathā vyāsas tu veda-dharmaprakāśanāya \| evaṁ śrībhagavato’vatārā asaṅkhyāḥ prayojanāpekṣakāḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, Vāsudeva and others appear to relieve the earth of its burden. Brahmā and others appear to perform creation, maintenance and destruction. Matsya appears to rescue the Vedas, Kūrma to support Mandara Mountain, Varāha to lift up the earth and kill Hiraṇyākṣa, Nṛsimha to kill Hiraṇyakaśipu, Vāmana to trick Bali, Paraśurāma to rid the earth of kṣatriyas, Rāma to kill Rāvana and other demons, Balarāma to kill Pralamba and other great demons, Buddha to give mercy to the living entities and Kalki to destroy the mlecchas. In Viṣṇu-dharma it is seen that Kalki is the Supreme Lord. Similarly, Vyāsa appears to reveal Vedas and dharma. In this way, the unlimited avatāras of the Lord have specific purposes.**

*tatha śrī-bhagavad-gītopaniṣatsu śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Bhagavad-gītā the Lord says:**

*yadā yadā hi dharmasya glānir bhavati bhārata \|*  
*abhyutthānam adharmasya tadātmānaṁ sṛjāmy aham \|\|109\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whenever there is a decrease in dharma and a rise of adharma, I appear. (BG** [**4.7**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/4.7)**)**

*ataḥ sarve’vatārāḥ sa-nimittāḥ \| atha ete’vatārāḥ kiṁ-svarūpāḥ ? tad āha brahmāṇḍa-purāṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, all the avatāras have a purpose. What are the svarūpas of these avatāras? In Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa it is said:**

*etasyaivāpare’nantā hy avatārā manoramāḥ \|*  
*mahāgner iha yadvat syur ulkāḥ śata-sahasraśaḥ \|\|110\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are unlimited, attractive avatāras, like hundreds of thousands of sparks from a huge fire.**

*tathaiva—*  
*vanyā-jalaṁ prāyam aśeṣa-nistṛtaṁ*  
*viṣvak-kṣitiṁ vyāpya vivardhate bhṛśam \|*  
*yasmāt samudbhūtam aho tataḥ punaḥ*  
*kāle bhūyaḥ praviśet tathaivam \|\|111\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A flood of water, unlimitedly manifesting, spreads over the whole earth and increases greatly. Again, in time everything enters into the person from whom it arose.**

*śrī-saṅkṣepa-bhāgavatāmṛte—*

**Перевод:**

**In Laghu-bhāgavatāmṛta it is said:**

*ata eva purāṇādau kecin nara-sakhyātmatām \|*  
*mahendrānujatāṁ kecit kecit kṣīrābdhi-śāyitām \|\|112\|\|*

*sahasra-śīrṣatāṁ kecit kecid vaikuṇṭha-nāthatām \|*  
*brūyuḥ kṛṣṇasya munayas tat-tad-vṛttānta-gāminaḥ \|\|113\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the scriptures some sages, having realized a certain incident, say that Kṛṣṇa is Nārāyaṇa, friend of Nara, Indra’s younger brother, Kṣīrodakaśāyī, Mahā-viṣṇu, or the Lord of Vaikuṇṭha.**

*tathā nārada-pañcarātre—*

**Перевод:**

**In Nārada-pancarātra, it is said:**

*tad-ājñā-kāriṇaḥ sarve brahmāṇḍeśvara-rūpiṇaḥ \|*  
*līlā-sukha-mayātmānas tat-prema-rūpa-bhāvanāḥ \|\|114\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the controllers of universes, carry out his orders. Joyful with his pastimes, they meditate on his form of prema.**

*tathā—*  
*nasy ota-gāva iva yasya vaśe bhavanti*  
*brahmādayas tanu-bhṛto mithur ardyamānāḥ \|*  
*kālasya te prakṛti-pūruṣayoḥ parasya*  
*śaṁ nas tanotu caraṇaḥ puruṣottamasya \|\|115\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Like oxen bound through the nose, embodied beings like Brahmā who are suffering in competition with each other, are controlled by you, the controller, superior to prakṛti and the jīva. May the lotus feet of you, the Supreme Lord bestow fortune upon us! (SB** [**11.6.14**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/6.14)**)**

*tathā gokula-saṁhitāyāṁ śrī-bhagavato jihvā-mūlāt sarasvaty-udbhūya śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ prati sākāṅkṣaṁ kaṭākṣam akarot iti dṛṣṭvā śrī-bhagavān sarasvatīṁ prati śaśāpa \| bhavati taru-rūpā bhava \| ante brahmā syāt samudbhūya brahmaṇaḥ patnītvam āyāsyatīti śāpa-dvayaṁ śrutvā sarasvatī cukopa \| sarasvaty api śrī-bhagavantaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ pratiśaptavatī \| sarasvaty uvāca—he bhagavan ! ekāparādhe śāpa-dvayaṁ dattaṁ yathā, tathā aham api śapāmi—bhagavan ! aṅgajayā saha ramiṣyasi iti \| tad anu sāparādhaiva sarasvatī stutiṁ cakāra—*

**Перевод:**

**In Goloka-saṁhitā, the following is related. Sarasvatī arose from the base of the Lord’s tongue and then cast an amorous glance at Kṛṣṇa. Seeing this, the Lord cursed her. “Become a tree. When Brahmā appears, you will become his wife.” Hearing the two curses, Sarasvatī became angry. She uttered a counter curse. She said, “O Lord! For one offense, you have given two curses. I will curse you, O Lord! You will enjoy with someone born of your own limbs.” Offending Sarasvatī then praised him as follows.**

*jagat sarvaṁ tvayi nyastaṁ nyastāḥ prakṛtayas tathā \|*  
*puruṣāś ca tathā kṛṣṇa tvayi sarvaṁ pratiṣṭhitam \|\|116\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The whole universe is placed in you. Prakṛti’s elements are placed in you. The puruṣas are in you. O Kṛṣṇa! Everything is established in you.**

*tvayy eva vilayaṁ yānti utpatsyanti ramanti ca \|*  
*doṣa eṣa kṛto’jñānāt kṣamasva parameśvara \|*  
*ity uktvā sā mahā-devī virarāma sarasvatī \|\|117\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They all merge in you, manifest from you and enjoy. Out of ignorance I have made a mistake. O Supreme Lord! Forgive that. Saying that, Sarasvatī, the great goddess, stopped speaking.**

*tathā brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**In Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.51**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.51)**), it is said:**

*agnir mahi gaganam ambu marud diśaś ca*  
*kālas tathātma manasīti jagat trayāṇi \|*  
*yasmād bhavanti vibhavanti viśanti yaṁ ca*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|118\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda from whom the three worlds composed of earth, water, fire, air, ether, space, time, mind and ātmās appear, by whom they continue their existence and within whom they enter at the time of universal destruction.**

*atha śrī-kṛṣṇasyāvatārāṇāṁ svarūpam āha padma-purāṇe nirvāṇa-khaṇḍe rahasyādhyāye śrī-bhagavān uvāca vyāsaṁ prati—*

**Перевод:**

**In Padma Purāṇa, Nirvāṇa-khaṇḍa, in the Rahasyadhyāya the Lord explains to Vyāsa, the svarūpa of his avatāras:**

*yad idaṁ me tvayā pṛṣṭaṁ rūpaṁ divyaṁ sanātanam \|*  
*niṣkalaṁ niṣkriyaṁ śāntaṁ sac-cid-ānanda-vigraham \|\|119\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You asked about my form. It is attractive, eternal, without parts, without action, peaceful. It is a form of sac-cid-ānanda.**

*pūrṇaṁ padma-palāśākṣaṁ nātaḥ parataraṁ mama \|*  
*satyaṁ vyāpi parānandaṁ cid-ghanaṁ śāśvataṁ param \|*  
*mamāvatāro nityo’yam atra mā saṁśayaṁ kṛthāḥ \|\|120\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**My form is complete, with lotus eyes. There is nothing superior to me. That form is the truth, all-pervading, the highest bliss, concentrated knowledge and perpetual. My avatāra is eternal. Do not doubt this.**

*ataḥ sarvopari-vaibhavaḥ sarveṣām ādhāra-svarūpaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ sarveṣām ātma-svarūpaḥ \| athaitasya samaṁ tan māyayācchannāḥ kecid anyaṁ kurvantīti tatrāha—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus Kṛṣṇa has more powers than all others, is the support for all, and is the atmā of all beings. But persons covered by māyāequate him with others.**

*yasyaivāṅghri-kalāṁśa-sambhava-mahāviṣṇus tv anekas tatas*  
*tasyaikasya ca roma-kūpa-jaṭhare brahmāṇḍako’nekaśaḥ \|*  
*tasyaikāṇḍaka-madhyato bhagavato’nekāvatārāḥ sthitāḥ*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇasya ca tasya sāmyam akarod anyaṁ tv aho’syājñatā \|\|121\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Many Mahā-viṣṇus arise as portions of portions of his feet. Many universes arise from just one of his hair pores. Many avatārasof the Lord are situated within one of his universes. Only the ignorant person will equate him with others.**

*tac ca—*  
*yathā koṭīśvaro rājā tat-tulyaḥ kiṁ śatādhipaḥ \|*  
*palaṁ palārdhaṁ karṣaṁ vā tulyaṁ mūlyaṁ kim iṣyate \|\|122\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Can one compare the leader of a hundred people to the king, controlling ten million? Can one equate a pala, to half a pala or to a karṣa (one-fourth pala)?**

*suvarṇasya ca ratnasya vastu caikaṁ na cānyathā \|*  
*gaṅgāyāḥ kumbha-saṁsthāpyaṁ jalaṁ gaṅgā-jalaṁ smṛtam \|*  
*gaṅgāyāṁ ca vinikṣiptaṁ punar gaṅgeva tad yathā \|\|123\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gold is always gold and a gem is always a gem and not otherwise.Gaṅgā water placed in a pot is still Gaṅgā water. Throwing it back into the Gaṅgā, it is also Gaṅgā.**

*tad eva sampūrṇānanda-vigrahaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-candraḥ \| tasyāṁśa-kalātvenānye nirūpitāḥ \| ity ādi sarvaṁ pūrvam uktaṁ tasmiṁś ca jñātavyam eva \| ity ādi śrī-bhagavato’nanta-mahimno guṇa-prakāśādi yat kiñcit purāṇadiṣu dṛṣṭaṁ tad uktam \| samyag brahmādayo vaktuṁ na samarthāḥ \| yathā śrī-bhāgavate—*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is the form of complete bliss. Others are defined as his aṁśas and kalās. Everything was previously explained. That should be understood. What was stated was seen in Purāṇas and other scriptures concerning the Lord’s unlimited glories and manifestation of qualities has been stated. Brahmā and others are not capable of completely describing him. In Bhāgavatam, it is said:**

*ko vetti bhūman bhagavan parātman*  
*yogeśvarotīr bhavatas trilokyām \|*  
*kva vā kathaṁ vā kati vā kadeti*  
*vistārayan krīḍasi yoga-māyām \|\|124\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O unrestricted Lord! O Lord endowed with all powers! O Supersoul! O Lord spread throughout all time! Who knows where, how, how many, and when your pastimes will take place in the universe? Spreading yoga-māyā, you play. (SB** [**10.14.21**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.21)**)**

*nārāyaṇas tvaṁ na hi sarva-dehinām*  
*ātmāsy adhīśākhila-loka-sākṣī \|*  
*nārāyaṇo’ṅgaṁ nara-bhū-jalāyanāt*  
*tac cāpi satyaṁ na tavaiva māyā \|\|125\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lord, of whom Mahā-viṣṇu is your expansion! Nārāyaṇa, Mahā-viṣṇu, the source of the other puruṣas is not you. Nor is the third puruṣa, dwelling within all beings, nor is the second puruṣa, the witness of all planets. Mahā-viṣṇu, called Nārāyaṇa because he is the shelter of the Kāraṇa Ocean, which gives rise to material creation, is simply your expansion. That expansion of yours is real, not illusory. (SB** [**10.14.14**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.14)**)**

*tathā—*  
*yasyaiva yo’nuguṇa-bhuk bahudhaika eva*  
*śuddho’py aśuddha iva mūrti-vibhāga-bhedaiḥ \|*  
*jñānānvitaḥ sakala-sattva-vibhūti-kartā*  
*tasmai nato’smi puruṣāya sadāvyayāya \|\|126\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to the indestructible Lord, endowed with qualities, who is one but has many forms, who is pure but seems impure, with differences in his forms, who is endowed with knowledge, and creates all sāttvika powers.**

*tathā—*  
*āsthāya yogaṁ nipuṇaṁ samāhitas*  
*taṁ nādhyagacchaṁ yata ātma-sambhavaḥ \|*  
*nato ’smy ahaṁ tac-caraṇaṁ samīyuṣāṁ*  
*bhavac-chidaṁ svasty-ayanaṁ sumaṅgalam \|\|127\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Performing yoga with fixed mind, I did not know the Lord, since I was created by him. I offer my respects to lotus feet of the Lord, which destroy material existence for the devotees and bestows the highest auspiciousness. (SB** [**2.6.35-36**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/6.35)**)**

*ity evaṁ—*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇaḥ paramaḥ pumāṁś ca paramānanda-svarūpo vibhū*  
*rādhā-prema-samanvito rasamayaḥ śyāmo jagan-mohanaḥ \|*  
*evaṁ tad-guṇa-varṇanaṁ marakataṁ ratnaṁ kirīṭaṁ kuru*  
*kṣipraṁ rāghava-kṛn-nivedanam idaṁ śrutvānya-cittaṁ tyaja \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is the supreme person, with a svarūpa of the highest bliss, who is all pervading, endowed with Rādhā’s prema, who is full of rasa, who is black and enchants the universe. Wears a jewel sapphire crown of the description of his qualities. Hearing the descriptions of Rāghava, quickly give up any other thoughts.**

*iti śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśe śrīman-nanda-kiśora-svarūpa-kṛṣṇa-candra-prakāśa-nirūpaṇaṁ nāma pañcamaṁ ratnam \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The fifth chapter called ‘Establishing Kṛṣṇa’s kaiśora svarūpa’ in Kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśa is completed.**

## Chapter 6 - Description of bhakti

*atha pravakṣye śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇāmbuja-sevanam \|*  
*samasta-duḥkha-damanaṁ nityānanda-sukha-pradam \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will now speak about service to Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet, which will destroy all suffering and bestow eternal happiness and bliss.**

*tad eva śrī-bhagavataś caraṇa-lābhasya kim upāyas tad āha – aho ! ananyayā bhaktyā, yathā śrī-bhagavan-niyamaḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**What is the method of attaining the Lord’s feet? Pure bhakti. The Lord states:**

*bhaktyāham ekayā grāhyaḥ śraddhayātmā priyaḥ satām \|*  
*bhaktiḥ punāti man-niṣṭhā śvapākān api sambhavāt \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Only by bhakti with full faith can the devotees obtain me, Paramātmā, the object of love. Bhakti fixed in me purifies even a dog eater of his low birth. (SB** [**11.14.21**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/14.21)**)**

*tathā śukoktiḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly Śukadeva says:**

*na dānaṁ na tapo nejyā na śaucaṁ na vratāni ca \|*  
*prīyate’malayā bhaktyā harir anyad-viḍambanam \|\|3\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Charity, austerity, worship, cleanliness and vows cannot please the Lord. The Lord is pleased by pure bhakti. Other than bhakti, everything else is a cause of mockery. (SB** [**7.7.52**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-7/7.52)**)**

*bhaktiḥ kim iti tad āha nārada-pañcarātre—*

**Перевод:**

**Nārada-pancarātra explains bhakti:**

*sarvopādhi-vinirmuktaṁ tat-paratvena nirmalam \|*  
*hṛṣīkeṇa hṛṣīkeśa-sevanaṁ bhaktir ucyate \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti is defined as service to the Lord using the senses. It should be free of all upādhis, and pure.**

*tasyāṁ bhaktau tri-vidhaṁ lakṣaṇaṁ—sādhanī, jñānānvitā, prema-lakṣaṇā ca \| sādhanī yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**There are three types of bhakti: sādhanī, jnānānvitā and premalakṣaṇā. Sādhanī is described as follows:**

*śravaṇaṁ kīrtanaṁ caiva smaraṇaṁ pāda-sevanam \|*  
*arcanaṁ vandanaṁ ceti sādhanī ṣaḍ-vidhā matā \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing, chanting, remembering, serving the feet, deity worship, and offering prayers are the six types of sādhanī bhakti.**

*etāsām ādau śravaṇa-bhaktir iti śreṣṭhā, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Among them, hearing is the best:**

*śravaṇāj jāyate śraddhāpy aratir bhaktir uttamā \|*  
*yan na śrutaṁ ca tasyaiva kathaṁ saṅkīrtanādayaḥ \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From hearing arises faith, detachment and pure bhakti. Without hearing about a topic, how can its saṅkīrtana, etc., be performed.**

*ata ādau bhakti-śravaṇam, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing is explained in the following:**

*pibanti ye bhagavata ātmanaḥ satāṁ*  
*kathāmṛtaṁ śravaṇa-puṭeṣu sambhṛtam*  
*punanti te viṣaya-vidūṣitāśayaṁ*  
*vrajanti tac-caraṇa-saroruhāntikam \|\|7\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who drink the sweet pastimes of the Lord and his devotees held in the cups of their ears clean their hearts of all contamination and attain the lotus feet of the Lord for service. (SB** [**2.2.37**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/2.37)**)**

*saṁsāra-sindhum atidustaram uttitīrṣor*  
*nānyaḥ plavo bhagavataḥ puruṣottamasya \|*  
*līlā-kathā-rasa-niṣevaṇam antareṇa*  
*puṁso bhaved vividha-duḥkha-davārditasya \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For a person who is suffering in the fire of countless miseries and even for persons who desires to cross the insurmountable ocean of material existence, there is no suitable boat except cultivating the nectar of the narrations of the Supreme Lord’s pastimes. (SB** [**12.4.40**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-12/4.40)**)**

*ataḥ śravaṇāt saṅkīrtana-bhaktir jāyate, tathā—*

**Перевод:**

**From hearing, saṅkīrtana-bhakti arises:**

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-nāma-guṇa-saṁśravaṇaṁ samasta-*  
*bhakter nidānam api sadbhir udāhṛtaṁ tat \|*  
*yasmād bhaved ratir alaṁ kramaśo’py akhaṇḍa-*  
*saṅkīrtana-smaraṇa-sevana-vandanādau \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees say that hearing about the name and qualities of Kṛṣṇa is the cause of bhakti. From hearing one gradually develops attraction to continuous saṅkīrtana, remembering, serving and offering prayers.**

*tad eva harer nāmnāṁ guṇānāṁ ca gānaṁ kīrtanam ucyate \| yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Singing about the qualities of the Lord and singing his names is called kīrtana. As stated in:**

*etāvatālam agha-nirharaṇāya puṁsāṁ*  
*saṅkīrtanaṁ bhagavato guṇa-karma-nāmnām \|\|10\|\| ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Chanting the names and activities of the Lord destroys all sins of men. (SB** [**6.3.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/3.24)**)**

*tathaiva—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly it is said:**

*śṛṇvan subhadrāṇi rathāṅga-pāṇer*  
*janmāni karmāṇi ca yāni loke \|*  
*gītāni nāmāni tad-arthakāni*  
*gāyan vilajjo vicared asaṅgaḥ \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the most auspicious birth and activities of Kṛṣṇa which are sung by all people and singing his names which describe those activities while having no attachment to anything else, a person should without shame wander in the world. (SB** [**11.2.39**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.39)**)**

*tathā padyāvalyāṁ \[20\]—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in the Padyāvalī, it is said:**

*vepante duritāni moha-mahimā sammoham ālambate*  
*sātaṅkaṁ nakha-rajanīṁ kalayate śrī-citraguptaḥ kṛtī \|*  
*sānandaṁ madhuparka-sambhṛti-vidhau vedhāḥ karoty udyamaṁ*  
*vaktuṁ nāmni taveśvarābhilaṣite brūmaḥ kim anyat param \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sins tremble and the glories of illusion become bewildered. Citragupta writes his records in fear. Brahmā starts to prepare madhuparka in joy. What else can we say about your desired names?**

*bṛhan-nāradīye—*

**Перевод:**

**In Bṛhan-nāradīya Purāṇa, it is said:**

*yan-nāmoccāraṇād eva mahā-pātaka-nāśanam \|*  
*yaṁ samabhyarcya viprendrāḥ paraṁ mokṣaṁ labhen naraḥ \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By uttering the Lord’s names mahā-pātakas11 are destroyed. O best of sages! Worshipping him, a person attains liberation.**

*evaṁ saṅkīrtanāt smaraṇaṁ jāyate \| brahma-vaivarte—*

**Перевод:**

**From saṅkīrtana, remembrance arises. In Brahma-vaivarta Purāṇa, it is said:**

*svasti śrī-viṣṇu-lokād dhari-caraṇa-rajaḥ-puñja-piñjottamāṅgaḥ*  
*kālāriḥ saṁyamanyāṁ madhuripu-vacanād ādiśaty arka-putram \|*  
*bhavyaṁ cānyan murāreḥ smaraṇa-vighaṭitāśeṣa-pāpāndhakārāḥ*  
*pūrṇā apy uddhavanto na katham api na vā vāraṇiyās tvayaiva \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The enemy of time, carrying on his head piles of dust from the Lord’s feet from Viṣṇu’s planet, spoke, inspired by the words of Viṣṇu to Yama in Saṁyama. “The darkness of unlimited sins has been completely destroyed by remembrance of Viṣṇu. They no longer needed to be restrained by you.”**

*tathaiva skānde—*

**Перевод:**
