**In Skanda Purāṇa, it is said:**

*tadaiva puruṣo mukto janma-duḥkha-jarādibhiḥ \|*  
*bhaktyā tu parayā nūnaṁ yadaivaṁ smarate harim \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When a person remembers the Lord with pure bhakti, he is liberated from birth, suffering and old age.**

*tatra śrī-kṛṣṇa-smaraṇena na kevalaṁ duḥkha-haraṇam, bhakti-muktidam eva \| yathā govinda-vṛndāvane prathama-paṭale—*

**Перевод:**

**Not only is suffering destroyed by remembering Kṛṣṇa, one attains bhakti and liberation. In Govinda-vṛndāvana, first chapter, it is said:**

*kṛṣṇa eva paraṁ brahma sac-cid-ānanda-sundaraḥ \|*  
*smṛti-mātreṇa yeṣāṁ vai bhakti-mukti-phala-pradaḥ \|\|16\|\| ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Brahman, beautiful with sac-cid-ānanda.Those who merely remember him attain bhakti and liberation as results.**

*evaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa-pādāmbuja-smaraṇena pāda-sevanādau matir jāyate’ntar-nirmalatvena, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, by remembering Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet, one becomes inclined to serve the Lord’s feet due to purity of heart:**

*praviṣṭaḥ karṇa-randhreṇa svānāṁ bhāva-saroruham \|*  
*dhunoti śam alaṁ kṛṣṇaḥ salilasya yathā śarat \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having entered the devotees’ lotus hearts through the ears, Kṛṣṇa destroys the contamination just as the autumn season purifies the muddy water.**

*atha pāda-sevanam, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Serving the feet is described:**

*taṁ sukhārādhyam ṛjubhir ananya-śaraṇair nṛbhiḥ \|*  
*kṛtajñaḥ ko na seveta durārādhyam asādhubhiḥ \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What grateful person would not worship the Supreme Lord who is willingly worshipped by the sincerely surrendered souls and who is not worshipped by the crooked? (SB** [**3.19.36**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/19.36)**)**

*tvayy ambujākṣākhila-sattva-dhāmni*  
*samādhināveśita-cetasaike \|*  
*tvat-pāda-potena mahat-kṛtena*  
*kurvanti govatsa-padaṁ bhavābdhim \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O lotus-eyed Lord! By concentrating on your pure form of śuddha-sattva in meditation some people cross the ocean of nescience as easily as one steps over the hoof print of a calf, by those lotus feet which act as a boat, obtained by the grace of devotees. (SB** [**10.2.30**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/2.30)**)**

*athaivaṁ pāda-sevanād arcane matir jāyate \| tad arcanaṁ yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**From serving the feet, one develops the inclination to deity worship. That is described as follows:**

*yat-pādayor aśaṭha-dhīḥ salilaṁ pradāya*  
*dūrvāṅkurair api vidhāya satīṁ saparyām \|*  
*apy uttamāṁ gatim asau bhajate tri-lokīṁ*  
*dāśvān aviklava-manāḥ katham ārtim ṛcchet \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By offering even water, newly grown grass, or flower buds at your lotus feet, by performing excellent worship, a sincere person attains Vaikuṇṭha. This Bali Mahārāja, without duplicity, has offered everything in the three worlds to you. How then can he deserve to suffer from arrest? (SB** [**8.22.23**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-8/22.23)**)**

*nāradīye—*

**Перевод:**

**In Nāradīya Purāṇa it is said:**

*jalenāpi jagannāthaḥ pūjito kleśahā hariḥ \|*  
*paritoṣaṁ vrajaty āśu tṛṣṇārtaḥ salilair yathā \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Worshiped by water, the Lord destroys suffering just as a thirsty person quickly becomes satisfied by water.**

*mānuṣaṁ durlabhaṁ prāpya yo hariṁ nārcayet sakṛt \|*  
*mūrkhaḥ parataras tasmāt ko’nyas tasmād acetanaḥ \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having attained rare human birth, a person who does not worship the Lord even once is a fool. Who is more unconscious than he?**

*tathaiva—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly:**

*dhyātaḥ smṛtaḥ pūjito vā praṇato vā janārdanaḥ \|*  
*saṁsāra-pāśa-vicchedī kas taṁ na pratipūjayet \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Meditated upon, remembered, worshipped or offered respects, the Lord destroys the rope of saṁsāra. Who would not worship him?**

*atha praṇāmam āha nārasiṁhe—*

**Перевод:**

**In Narasiṁha Purāṇa, offering respects is described:**

*namaskāraḥ smṛto yajñaḥ sarva-yajñeṣu cottamaḥ \|*  
*namaskāreṇa caikena sāṣṭāṅgena hariṁ vrajet \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Offering respects is considered to be yajña. It is the best of yajñas. By offering of respects using the eight limbs just once, one goes to the Lord.**

*pādme devadūta-vikuṇḍala-saṁvāde—*

**Перевод:**

**In Padma Purāṇa, in a conversation between Devadūta and Vikuṇḍala, it is said:**

*kṛtvāpi bahuśaḥ pāpaṁ naro moha-samanvitaḥ \|*  
*na yāti narakaṁ natvā sarva-pāpa-haraṁ harim \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A person who has committed many sins out of bewilderment does not go to hell after offering respects to the Lord who destroys all sins.**

*tatra daṇḍa-praṇāmaṁ yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**The daṇḍa praṇāma is described:**

*daṇḍa-praṇāmaṁ kurute viṣṇave bhakti-bhāvataḥ \|*  
*reṇu-saṅkhyaṁ vaset svarge manvantara-śataṁ naraḥ \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The persons who offer respects like a stick on the ground with bhakti lives in Svarga for a hundred Manvantaras multiplied by the number of particles of dust on his body.**

*tatra pradakṣiṇa-māhātmyaṁ, yathā vārāhe—*

**Перевод:**

**In Varāha Purāṇa, the glories of pradakṣiṇa are described;**

*evaṁ kṛtvā tu kṛṣṇasya yaḥ kuryād dviḥ pradakṣiṇām \|*  
*sapta-dvīpavatī-puṇyaṁ labhate tu pade pade \|*

**Перевод:**

**One who after doing this, circumambulates Kṛṣṇa twice, attains all the puṇyas contained in the seven dvīpas with each step.**

*tat khyātaṁ yat sudharmasya pūrvasmin gṛdhra-janmani \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-pradakṣiṇābhyāsān mahā-siddhir abhūd iti \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The proof is Sudharma who in his previous birth was a vulture, attained great perfection by circumambulating Kṛṣṇa.**

*tathā—*  
*patitaḥ skhalito vārtaḥ kṣud-bādhā-vivaśo gṛṇan \|*  
*haraye nama ity uccair mucyate sarva-pātakāt \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Falling, stumbling, hopeless, overcome with hunger, one who loudly chants, “I offer respects to the Lord,” is freed of all sins.**

*atha jñānānivitā, yathā dāsyaṁ sakhyaṁ tathā cātma-nivedanam iti trayam \| tatra dāsyaṁ, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Now jnānānvita-bhakti is described. There were three types: dāsya, sakhya and ātma-nivedana. Dāsya is described as follows:**

*yan-nāma-śruti-mātreṇa pumān bhavati nirmalaḥ \|*  
*tasya tīrtha-padaḥ kiṁ vā dāsānām avaśiṣyate \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What is impossible for those who have the mood of servants (dāsas) of the Lord, whose feet are like holy places? Merely by hearing their names, a person becomes completely purified. (SB** [**9.5.16**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-9/5.16)**)**

*śrī-kṛṣṇa eva sarveṣāṁ paraṁ sarvopāsanīya iti niścaya-jñānena sadbhiḥ śrī-bhagavad-dāsatvaṁ svīkṛtam \| tatas tad anusandhīyate \| tad eva—*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is the most worshippable among all beings. With this certitude, the servitude to the Lord (dāsya) is accepted. This should be examined.**

*etāḥ paraṁ tanu-bhṛto bhuvi gopa-vadhvo*  
*govinda eva nikhilātmani rūḍha-bhāvāḥ \|*  
*vāñchanti yad bhava-bhiyo munayo vayaṁ ca*  
*kiṁ brahma-janmabhir ananta-kathā-rasasya \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Among all persons on earth, these cowherd women alone have attained perfect bodies, for they have achieved mahābhava for Govinda, the source of all forms of the Lord. Their pure love is desired by those who fear material existence, by great sages, and by ourselves as well. For one who has tasted the narrations of the infinite Lord, what is the use of taking birth as a high-class brāhmaṇa, or even as Brahmā himself? (SB** [**10.47.58**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/47.58)**)**

*tatra dāsyam eva dvividham \| dāsatvaṁ dāsītvaṁ ca \| tatra dāsa-bhāvaḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**There are two types of dāsya: servant and maidservant. Among them dāsya-bhāva (mood of servant) is described:**

*ahaṁ hare tava pādaika-mūla-*  
*dāsānudāso bhavitāsmi bhūyaḥ \|*  
*manaḥ smaretāsu-pater guṇāṁs te*  
*gṛṇīta vāk karma karotu kāyaḥ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! I shall become the servant of servants whose only shelter is your lotus feet. May my mind remember the Lord of my life, may my words chant your glories and may my body serve you.(SB** [**6.11.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/11.24)**)**

*viṣṇu-dharmottare—*

**Перевод:**

**In Viṣṇu-dharmottara it is said:**

*paramātmam aśeṣasya jagataḥ prabhavāpyayam \|*  
*śaraṇyaṁ śaraṇaṁ gacchan govindaṁ nāvasīdati \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Surrendering to Govinda, the object of surrender, the Supreme Lord, creator and destroyer of unlimited worlds, one is not disheartened.**

*tatra dāsī-bhāvo, yathā saṁmohana-tantre—*

**Перевод:**

**Dāsī-bhāva is described in Saṁmohana-tantra:**

*dāsa-bhāvaḥ sakhya-bhāvaḥ putra-bhāvas tathaiva ca \|*  
*nārī-bhāvo viśeṣeṇa guhyād guhyatamaṁ smṛtaḥ \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are dāsa, sakhya and putra bhāvas. The bhāva of a woman is particularly the most secret of secrets.**

*tathādi-purāṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**In Ādi Purāṇa it is said:**

*gopī-bhāvena ye bhaktā mām evaṁ samupāsate \|*  
*teṣu teṣv iva tuṣṭo’haṁ satyaṁ satyaṁ vadāmy aham \|*  
*bhāvānurūpaṁ sarvatra pārtha vyavaharāmy aham \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am satisfied with those who worship me in the mood of a gopī.I say this in truth. I deal with everyone according to their bhāva.**

*atra sakhya-bhāvo, yathā—śrī-kṛṣṇa eva parama-brahma rasa-maya-līlā-vigrahaḥ \| yad yad vāñchanti, tat tat prāpnuvanti iti jñānena sakhya-bhāvaṁ kurvanti santaḥ \| tad yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Sakhya-bhāva is explained. The devotee thinks, “Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Brahman and a form endowed with pastimes of rasa.Whatever one desires one will attain.” In this mood they act with sakhya-bhāva.**

*kāmaṁ krodhaṁ bhayaṁ sneham aikyaṁ sauhṛdam eva ca \|*  
*nityaṁ harau vidadhato yānti tan-mayatāṁ hi te \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Persons who constantly direct their lust, anger, fear, protective affection, and feelings of impersonal oneness or friendship toward the Lord are sure to attain realization of him. (SB** [**10.29.15**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/29.15)**)**

*yathā mahābhārate—*

**Перевод:**

**As stated in Mahābhārata:**

*arjunasya sakhā kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇasya hi sakhārjunaḥ \|*  
*ubhayor antaraṁ nāsti pavanākāśayor iva \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa is the friend of Arjuna. Arjuna is the friend of Kṛṣṇa. There is no distance between them. They are like air and the sky.**

*athātma-nivedanaṁ, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Ātma-nivedana is described:**

*sarva-bhūteṣu yaḥ paśyed bhagavad-bhāvam ātmanaḥ \|*  
*bhūtāni bhagavaty ātmany eṣa bhāgavatottamaḥ \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The most advanced devotee sees Supreme Lord, one’s object of worship, within everything, and sees everything in the Supreme Lord. (SB** [**11.2.45**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.45)**)**

*iti jñātvā ātma-samarpaṇaṁ kurvanti santaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Knowing this, the devotees offer themselves:**

*ātmānam arpayet kṛṣṇe bhaved ātma-nivedanam \|*  
*ananya-bhāvam āśritya tavāhaṁ ca balir yathā \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Ātma-nivedana is offering the self to Kṛṣṇa. Taking shelter of exclusive devotion, one thinks “I am yours,” as in the case of Bali.‘**

*vikrītā gauḥ pradattā vā svayaṁ yat tan na vidyate \|*  
*tadā dehādikaṁ sarvaṁ dattaṁ kṛṣṇāya nātmanaḥ \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A cow sold or given does not think of itself. Giving everything to Kṛṣṇa, one does not think of oneself.**

*tathā kavir uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Kavi says:**

*kāyena vācā manasendriyair vā*  
*buddhyātmanā vānusṛta-svabhāvāt \|*  
*karoti yad yat sakalaṁ parasmai*  
*nārāyaṇāyeti samarpayet tat \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In accordance with one’s particular nature, whatever one does with body, words, mind, senses, intelligence or purified consciousness one should offer to the Lord, thinking, “This is for the pleasure of Nārāyaṇa.” (SB** [**11.2.36**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.36)**)**

*athaivaṁ jñāna-bhaktyā prema-lakṣaṇā bhaktir jāyate, yathā brahma saṁhitāyāṁ—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus in this way prema-lakṣaṇā bhakti arises by jñāna-bhakti. In Brahma-saṁhitā, it is said:**

*prabuddhe jñāna-bhaktibhyām ātmany ānanda-cin-mayī \|*  
*udety anuttamā bhaktir bhagavat-prema-lakṣaṇā \|\|41\|\| \[Brṣ 5.58\]*

**Перевод:**

**When the soul is awakened by knowledge and devotion, the highest form of bhakti, characterized by bliss and knowledge and endowed with prema for the Lord, will appear.**

*tad evādi-purāṇe arjunaṁ prati śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Ādi Purāṇa the Lord speaks to Arjuna:**

*na tapobhir na vedaiś ca nācārair na ca vidyayā \|*  
*vaśo’smi kevalaṁ premṇā pramāṇaṁ tatra gopikāḥ \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am not controlled by austerities, the Vedas, good conduct or knowledge but by prema. The gopīs are the proof of this.**

*tathātra prema yathā—jñānādy-anapekṣayā mamaivety ākāra-puraḥ-saraṁ sahaja-sphūrtiḥ prema \| tad eva—*

**Перевод:**

**Prema is defined. A natural prominent manifestation of possessiveness with disregard for jñāna is called prema.**

*yā dohane ’vahanane mathanopalepa-*  
*preṅkheṅkhanārbha-ruditokṣaṇa-mārjanādau \|*  
*gāyanti cainam anurakta-dhiyo ’śru-kaṇṭhyo*  
*dhanyā vraja-striya urukrama-citta-yānāḥ \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ladies of Vraja are the most fortunate of women because, with their minds fully attached to Kṛṣṇa and their throats always choked up with tears, they constantly sing about him while milking the cows, winnowing grain, churning butter, gathering cow dung for fuel, riding on swings, taking care of their crying babies, sprinkling the ground with water, and cleaning their houses. He was always in their hearts. (SB** [**10.44.15**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/44.15)**)**

*atha kīdṛk prema ? tad āha—*

**Перевод:**

**What are prema’s characteristics? That is described:**

*prāṇa-pratima-rūpeṇa darśanādarśanena ca \|*  
*jīvanaṁ maraṇaṁ syād yat tat premeti nigadyate \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will live by seeing he who is my very life and will die by not seeing him. That is called prema.**

*padma-purāṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**In Padma Purāṇa, it is said:**

*avyalīkena manasā preṣṭhasyārādhanaṁ prati \|*  
*ānandānubhavād bhaktir dhiyo vṛttir acañcalā \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti endowed with unwavering intelligence arises from the experience of bliss in worship of the beloved with a sincere mind.**

*atyanta-sukha-samprāptau vicchede duḥkha-santateḥ \|*  
*hetur eko’yam eveti saṁśrayo bhaktir ucyate \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the only cause of happiness is attaining the Lord and the only cause of suffering is separation from the Lord, that surrender is called bhakti.**

*dvābhyāṁ saṁvalitair bhāvaiḥ prema-bhaktir iti smṛtam \|\|47\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Prema bhakti is recognized by bhāvas mixing these two factors mentioned above.**

*athaivaṁ śrī-bhagavad-bhaktiḥ kenopāyena jāyate ? tad iti sat-saṅgād eva \| śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**By what method does bhakti to Lord arise? It arises from association with devotees. The Lord says:**

*satāṁ prasaṅgān mama vīrya-saṁvido*  
*bhavanti hṛt-karṇa-rasāyanāḥ kathāḥ \|*  
*taj-joṣaṇād āśv apavarga-vartmani*  
*śraddhā ratir bhaktir anukramiṣyati \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From association with the best devotees, topics of my glorious pastimes become directly realized, bringing the devotee to niṣṭhā.Then the topics become an elixir for the heart and ears at the stage of ruci. By taste for these topics, āsakti, bhāva and then prema for the Lord who is the destroyer of material life, quickly develop in sequence. (SB** [**3.25.25**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/25.25)**)**

*yathā prema-sudhā-sāraṇyām—*

**Перевод:**

**In Prema-sudhā-sāraṇī, it is said:**

*satata-prema-parāyaṇa-jana-mukha-galita-kṛṣṇa-kathā-mādhvī \|*  
*śravaṇa-puṭena nipītā vitarati kṛṣṇe’malaṁ prema \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sweet topics of the Lord flowing from the mouths of devotees, devoted constantly to prema, drunk through the ears, bestows pure prema for Kṛṣṇa.**

*tathā padma-purāṇe—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly in Padma Purāṇa, it is said:**

*na tapāṁsi na tīrthāni na śāstrāṇi jayanti naḥ \|*  
*saṁsāra-sāgarottāre vaiṣṇava-sevanaṁ vinā \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Without service to the devotees, austerities, tīrthas and scriptures do not enable us to cross the ocean of saṁsāra.**

*tad eva—*  
*no rodhayati māṁ yogo na sāṅkhyaṁ dharma eva ca \|*  
*na svādhyāyas tapas tyāgo neṣṭā-pūrtaṁ na dakṣiṇā \|\|51\|\|*  
*vratāni yajñaś chandāṁsi tīrthāni niyamā yamāḥ \|*  
*yathāvarundhe sat-saṅgaḥ sarva-saṅgāpaho hi mām \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! Only by associating with my pure devotees one can destroy material attachment and attain me. One cannot attain me by aṣṭāṅga-yoga, distinction of ātmā from body, practice of nonviolence, study of the Vedas, austerity, sannyāsa, sacrifices, charitable projects, donations, vows, worship of devatās, secret mantras, holy places, or observing prohibitions and rules. (SB** [**11.12.1-2**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/12.1)**)**

*tathā ata eva bhagavad-bhaktiḥ kṣipram utpadyate, yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**In this way, bhakti to the Lord quickly arises.**

*na hy am-mayāni tīrthāni na devā mṛc-chilā-mayāḥ \|*  
*te punanty uru-kālena darśanād eva sādhavaḥ \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**No one can deny that there are holy places with sacred rivers, or that the devatās appear in deity forms made of earth and stone.But these purify the soul only after a long time, whereas saintly persons purify just by being seen. (SB** [**10.48.31**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/48.31)**)**

*tathā nava-siddhān prati nimi-nṛpa uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**Nimi speaks to the Nava-yogendras:**

*ata ātyantikaṁ kṣemaṁ pṛcchāmo bhavato ’naghāḥ \|*  
*saṁsāre ’smin kṣaṇārdho ’pi sat-saṅgaḥ śevadhir nṛṇām \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O sinless ones! Therefore, I ask you to kindly tell me what is most beneficial. Even half a moment’s association with pure devotees within this world of birth and death is a treasure for any man.(SB** [**11.2.30**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.30)**)**

*atha santaḥ kīdṛśā ity ucyatām \| tad eva—*

**Перевод:**

**What are the characteristics of the devotees? That is described:**

*mahat-sevāṁ dvāram āhur vimuktes*  
*tamo-dvāraṁ yoṣitāṁ saṅgi-saṅgam \|*  
*mahāntas te sama-cittāḥ praśāntā*  
*vimanyavaḥ suhṛdaḥ sādhavo ye \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Service to saintly persons is the cause of two types of liberation, whereas association with men who associate with women is the cause of bondage. The impersonalist sees no differences and is peaceful whereas the devotee has a sincere heart and his intelligence is fixed in me. Both types of saintly persons are devoid of anger, are friendly to all and do not see faults in others.(SB** [**5.5.2**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-5/5.2)**)**

*tathā śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said:**

*kṛpālur akṛta-drohas titikṣuḥ sarva-dehinām \|*  
*satya-sāro ’navadyātmā samaḥ sarvopakārakaḥ \|\|56\|\|*

*kāmair ahata-dhīr dānto mṛduḥ śucir akiñcanaḥ \|*  
*anīho mita-bhuk śāntaḥ sthiro mac-charaṇo muniḥ \|\|57\|\|*

*apramatto gabhīrātmā dhṛti-māñ jita-ṣaḍ-guṇaḥ \|*  
*amānī māna-daḥ kalyo maitraḥ kāruṇikaḥ kaviḥ \|\|58\|\|*

*ājñāyaivaṁ guṇān doṣān mayādiṣṭān api svakān \|*  
*dharmān santyajya yaḥ sarvān māṁ bhajeta sa tu sattamaḥ \|\|59\|\|*

*jñātvājñātvātha ye vai māṁ yāvān yaś cāsmi yādṛśaḥ \|*  
*bhajanty ananya-bhāvena te me bhakta-tamā matāḥ \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava, a saintly person is merciful and never injures others. He is tolerant toward all living entities. He is most truthful.He is free from all envy and jealousy, and his mind is equal in material happiness and distress. He dedicates his time to work for the welfare of all others. His intelligence is never bewildered by material desires and he has controlled his senses. His behavior is always pleasing and exemplary and he is free from possessiveness. He never endeavors in worldly activities and he controls his eating. He has śānti-rati and performs his duties until completion. A saintly person is contemplative and accepts me as his only shelter. Such a person is alert and inscrutable and not subject to changes. He has conquered over the six material qualities—namely hunger, thirst, lamentation, illusion, old age and death. He is free from all desire for prestige and offers honor to others. He is expert in understanding others and is free from cheating. He is compassionate and understands bondage and liberation.**  
**A person who, understanding good and bad aspects of dharma as taught by me gives up all his duties and simply worships me is the best of all.**  
**Those who may or may not know the extent of my powers, my forms and my nature, but who worship me with great possessiveness, I consider to be the best of devotees. (SB** [**11.11.29-33**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/11.29)**)**

*evaṁ bhāgavatam ārādhya śrī-kṛṣṇe bhaktiḥ prajāyata ity asandehaḥ \| yathā śrī-bhagavan-nigamaḥ—*

**Перевод:**

**By worshipping the devotee, bhakti to Kṛṣṇa arises without doubt. The Lord says:**

*sat-saṅgena hi daiteyā yātudhānā mṛgāḥ khagāḥ \|*  
*gandharvāpsaraso nāgāḥ siddhāś cāraṇa-guhyakāḥ \|\|61\|\| ity ādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Due to the association of my devotees, living entities such as the Daityas, Rākṣasas, birds, beasts, Gandharvas, Apsarās, Nāgas, Siddhas, Cāraṇas, and Guhyakas (attained my abode). (SB** [**11.12.3**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/12.3)**)**

*ataḥ santam ārādhya śrī-harau bhaktiḥ karaṇīyā ananya-bhāvena \| yathā śrī-bhagavad-gītāyām arjunaṁ prati śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**After worshipping the devotee, one should perform bhakti to the Lord with pure feeling. The Lord says to Arjuna in Bhagavad-gītā:**

*sarva-dharmān parityajya mām ekaṁ śaraṇaṁ vraja \|*  
*ahaṁ tvā sarva-pāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyāmi mā śucaḥ \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up all dharmas, surrender to me alone. I will free you from all sins. Do not lament. (BG** [**18.66**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/18.66)**)**

*tathā ca brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ brahmāṇaṁ prati śrī-bhagavān uvāca—*

**Перевод:**

**In Brahma-saṁhitā (**[**5.61**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/5.61)**) the Lord speaks to Brahmā:**

*dharmān anyān parityajya mām ekaṁ bhaja viśvasan \|*  
*yādṛśī yādṛśī śraddhā siddhir bhavati tādṛśī \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up all other practices, just worship me alone with faith. According to one’s faith, one attains the goal.**

*tathā—*  
*tasmāt tvam uddhavotsṛjya codanāṁ praticodanām \|*  
*pravṛttiṁ ca nivṛttiṁ ca śrotavyaṁ śrutam eva ca \|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! Give up duties and forbidden acts, the path of enjoyment and the path of renunciation, the desire to hear more about dharma, and what you have already heard about dharma.(SB** [**11.12.14**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/12.14)**)**

*bhaktānāṁ dharma-karmādi-bādhena dūṣaṇam asti ? naivam \| tathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Are devotees at fault by rejecting dharma and karma? No. It is said:**

*devarṣi-bhūtāpta-nṝṇāṁ pitṝṇāṁ*  
*na kiṅkaro nāyam ṛṇī ca rājan \|*  
*sarvātmanā yaḥ śaraṇaṁ śaraṇyaṁ*  
*gato mukundaṁ parihṛtya kartam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O King! One who has given up all varṇāśrama duties and has taken full shelter of the lotus feet of Mukunda, who offers shelter to all, is not a debtor to or servant of the devatās, great sages, ordinary living beings, relatives or Pitṛs. (SB** [**11.5.41**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/5.41)**)**

*tathā bṛhan-nāradīye—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly, in Bṛhan-nāradīya Purāṇa, it is said:**

*vāsudeva-prasaṅgena kriyā-lopo bhaved yadi \|*  
*tasya karmāṇi kurvanti tisraḥ koṭyo maharṣayaḥ \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If one omits some karmas because of engagement in Vāsudeva, thirty million sages perform those actions.**

*tathaiva—*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly it is said:**

*ājñāyaivaṁ guṇān doṣān mayādiṣṭān api svakān \|*  
*dharmān santyajya yaḥ sarvān māṁ bhajeta sa tu sattamaḥ \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A person who, understanding good and bad aspects of dharma as taught by me gives up all his duties and simply worships me is the best of all. (SB** [**11.11.32**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/11.32)**)**

*tathā—*  
*yadā yasyānugṛhṇāti bhagavān ātma-bhāvitaḥ \|*  
*sa jahāti matiṁ loke vede ca pariniṣṭhitām \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the Lord shows mercy to the devotee, the devotee gives up thinking of karma-kāṇḍa and material affairs. (SB** [**4.29.46**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/29.46)**)**

*ity evaṁ śrī-bhagavad-bhaktānām anupapatitir bhayādayaḥ santi, naivam \|*  
*yathā śrīmad-bhāgavate—samāśritā ye pada-pallava-plavaṁ ity ādi \| tathā—*

**Перевод:**

**“The devotees will experience fear and other problems by nonperformance of karmas.” That is not so. In Bhāgavatam it is said:**

*samāśritā ye pada-pallava-plavaṁ*  
*mahat-padaṁ puṇya-yaśo murāreḥ*  
*bhavāmbudhir vatsa-padaṁ paraṁ padaṁ*  
*padaṁ padaṁ yad vipadāṁ na teṣām*

**Перевод:**

**For those who have accepted the boat of the lotus feet of the Lord, killer of Mura, the ocean of the material world is like the water contained in a calf’s hoof-print. Their goal is Vaikuṇṭha, the place of the eternal associates, not the material world. (SB** [**10.14.58**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.58)**)**

*tathā—*  
*na te mādhava tāvakāḥ kvacid*  
*bhraśyanti mārgāt tvayi baddha-sauhṛdāḥ \|\| ity ādi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Mādhava, Supreme Lord, Lord of the goddess of fortune, if devotees completely in love with you sometimes fall from the path of devotion, they do not fall like non-devotees, for you still protect them. (SB** [**10.2.33**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/2.33)**)**

*tathā śrī-bhagavad-gītāsu—*

**Перевод:**

**In Bhagavad-gītā, it is said:**

*ananyāś cintayanto māṁ ye janāḥ paryupāsate \|*  
*teṣāṁ nityābhiyuktānāṁ yoga-kṣemaṁ vahāmy aham \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I carry the burden of supply and maintenance of those who desire constant association with me, and who, thinking only of me, worship only me. (BG** [**9.22**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/9.22)**)**

*ata eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-candra-pādāravindāśrayaṇe na kiñcid durlabham \| janma-maraṇa-bhayātīta-padaṁ ca prāpyate \| nityānanda-padam api prāpyate ca \| yathā—*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, nothing is rare for the person who takes shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. He surpasses fear of birth and death. He attains the position of eternal bliss.**

*martyo mṛtyu-vyāla-bhītaḥ palāyan*  
*lokān sarvān nirbhayaṁ nādhyagacchat \|*  
*tvat pādābjaṁ prāpya yadṛcchayādya*  
*susthaḥ śete mṛtyur asmād apaiti \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**No one in this material world, fearful of the snake of time, has become free from fear, even by fleeing to various planets. But now that you have appeared, death is fleeing in fear of you, and the living entities, having obtained shelter at your lotus feet by your mercy, are fearless and tranquil. (SB** [**10.3.27**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/3.27)**)**

*tathā śrī-viṣṇu-sahasra-nāma-stotre—*

**Перевод:**

**In the Viṣṇu-sahasra-nāma-stotra, it is said:**

*na vāsudeva-bhaktānām aśubhaṁ vidyate kvacit \|*  
*janma-mṛtyu-jarā-vyādhi-bhayaṁ vāpy upajāyate \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is nothing inauspicious for the devotees of Vāsudeva. They have no fear of birth, death, old age and disease.**

*tathā—*  
*bhagavata uru-vikramāṅghri-śākhā-*  
*nakha-maṇi-candrikayā nirasta-tāpe \|*  
*hṛdi katham upasīdatāṁ punaḥ sa*  
*prabhavati candra ivodite ’rka-tāpaḥ \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**How can the heat of material existence again arise in the hearts of those who worship the Lord, since the heat of lust is destroyed by the Lord’s heroic lotus feet, his toes, his toenails, his toe jewels and the soothing light from his feet? When the moon rises, the heat of the sun is dissipated. (SB** [**11.2.54**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.54)**)**

*ity ādi śrī-bhagavad-bhakter mahattvam \| atraivāgre śrī-bhagavad-bhajanoddeśa-ratnādau katicid uktam \| tatraiva jñātavyam iti \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is the glory of bhakti to the Lord. Previously in this work (i.e. in Chapter 1), in Jewels of instruction on the worship of the Lord, some things were stated. Those statements should be understood here also.**

*ataḥ—*  
*nānā-deva-niṣevaṇaṁ parihara prāṇādi-saṁrodhanaṁ*  
*dharmaṁ karma ca dāna-tīrtha-niyama-brahmādikopāsanam \|*  
*sarveṣāṁ parameśvarasya paramānanda-pradasyātmanaḥ*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇasya sadāvyayasya caraṇa-dvandvāravindaṁ bhaja \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Give up serving various devatās, controlling the prāṇas, dharma, karma, worship of Brahman, charities, tīrthas and rules. Worship the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Lord of all beings, always indestructible, who awards the highest bliss.**

*tathā—*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇa-padāravinda-vigalat-prema-pravāhāmṛtaṁ*  
*pāyaṁ pāyam anārataṁ para-sukhī bhūtvā mahān unmadaḥ \|*  
*nānyatrāpi mano dadhāti na vadaty anyaṁ smaren netaraṁ*  
*tasyivāṅghri-yaśo vināpy ata imaṁ cakre kavī rāghavaḥ \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The poet Rāghava continuously drinking the nectar of rivers of prema flowing from the lotus feet of Radhā and Kṛṣṇa, becoming most joyful and intoxicated, does not concentrate on anything else, say anything else or remember anything else except the fame of his feet. Thus he has composed this work.**

*svar-vāpī-savidhe mahā-muni-varasyāmrātakasyāśrame*  
*nānā-śāstra-vidhijña-paṇḍita-yute sthāne’mbikādhiṣṭhite \|*  
*brahma-vyāsa-maheśa-gopita-dhanaṁ kṛṣṇa-prakāśābhidhaṁ*  
*ratnaṁ rāghava-nāma-dheya-kṛtinā vedyaṁ kṛtaṁ sarvataḥ \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This work named Kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśa, which should be known completely, a treasure hidden from Brahmā, Vyāsa and Maheśa, was written by Rāghava, near the Gaṅgā, at the āśramacalled Amrātaka of the great sage, where there is a temple of Ambikā and where many persons learned in various scriptures reside.**

*ye jānanti mahānta eva sudhiyas te modayanty uttamāḥ*  
*kṣīṇā ye na vidanti tattvam idam vādhyāpayantv āśu te \|*  
*etad ye tu vihāya cānya-viṣaye kurvanty aho mānase*  
*te kiṁ kṛṣṇa-padāravinda-surasaṁ samprāpnuvanty ajñakāḥ \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great intelligent, exalted devotees who know will relish this book. Those who are inferior, and do not know tattva, will quickly learn this book. How can the ignorant who reject the book and concentrate on other subjects obtain the sweet taste of Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet?**

*param—*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇāṅghri-saroja-yugma-vigalan-mādhvīka-dhārāmṛtaṁ*  
*pītaṁ yair na ca cāru-citta-caṣakais te vañcitā duḥkhitāḥ \|*  
*anyaṁ vānusaranty anitya-vibhavaṁ saukhyāśayā bāliśā*  
*yāsyanty udbhava-mṛtyu-tīvra-kadaneṣv ājanma-koṭiṣv api \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who do not drink through the cups of their hearts the sweet streams of honey flowing from Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet, being cheated, will suffer. These fools pursue other temporary fortunes with a desire for happiness and obtain intense misery arising from birth and death throughout millions of lives.**

*ataḥ sarvam anyaṁ vihāya sarvopari śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇāravindaṁ brahmādibhir bhajanīyaṁ bhajata \| tad eva kurutaitad durlabha-saṅgrahānusāreṇa \| tad evamimaṁ saṅgrahaṁ viruddha-matiṣu na prakāśayet \| tad iti—*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up everything else, worship the supreme lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa which are worshipped by Brahmā and others. Do this according to this rare compendium of instructions. One should not reveal these instructions to persons of contrary opinions.**

*dhūrtāyātyanta-mūrkhāya tathā paṇḍita-mānine \|*  
*pāṣaṇḍa-mataye caiva anyad evopasevine \|\|77\|\|*

*abhaktāya ca lolāya riktopāsāparāya ca \|*  
*nāstikāya tāmasāya tathāhaṅkāra-kāriṇe \|\|78\|\|*

*na prakāśyo na deyaś ca kadācin naiṣa saṅgrahaḥ \|\|79\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This work should not be revealed or given to those who think themselves learned, to the wicked and most foolish, to the pāṣaṇḍa, to those who serve devatās, to the non-devotee, the greedy, to the person who does not worship Kṛṣṇa, to the atheist, the person in tamas and the proud person.**

*deyo viśuddha-mataye kṛṣṇa-pādābja-sevine \|*  
*guru-bhaktāya śāntāya satya-sandhāya sarvadā \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should give this work to the person of pure mind, to the person who worships Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet, who has devotion to guru, who is peaceful, and always truthful.**

*tathā śrī-bhagavad-gītāsu—*

**Перевод:**

**In Bhagavad-gītā it is said:**

*idaṁ te nātapaskāya nābhaktāya kadācana \|*  
*na cāśuśrūṣave vācyaṁ na ca māṁ yo’bhyasūyati \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is not to be spoken to a person lacking control of his senses, to a person without true devotion, to a person who is unwilling to hear, or to a person who envies me, thinking that I am material. (BG** [**18.67**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/18.67)**)**

*ya idaṁ paramaṁ guhyaṁ mad-bhakteṣv abhidhāsyati \|*  
*bhaktiṁ mayi parāṁ kṛtvā mām evaiṣyaty asaṁśayaḥ \|\|82\|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**One who teaches this supreme secret to my devotees, attains the highest bhakti, and finally attains me without doubt. (BG** [**18.68**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/18.68)**)**

*īśvaraḥ sarva-bhūtānāṁ hṛd-deśe’rjuna tiṣṭhati \|*  
*bhrāmayan sarva-bhūtāni yantrārūḍhāni māyayā \|\|83\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Arjuna, the Lord is situated in the heart of all living entities. He engages in action all living entities, who are mounted on a machine made of prakṛti. (BG** [**18.61**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/18.61)**)**

*tam eva śaraṇaṁ gaccha sarva-bhāvena bhārata \|*  
*tat-prasādāt parāṁ śāntiṁ sthānaṁ prāpsyasi śāśvatam \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O descendent of Bharata, take refuge in the Paramātmā alone with all your faculties. By his mercy, you will attain the eternal spiritual abode. (BG** [**18.62**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/18.62)**)**

*ity evaṁ jñātvā śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇāravindam eva śaraṇaṁ kartavyam iti śeṣaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Knowing this, one should surrender to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa.**

*iti śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhakti-ratna-prakāśe bhakti-viracanaṁ nāma ṣaṣṭha-*  
*prakāśa-ratnaṁ samāptam \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sixth chapter called ‘Description of bhakti’ in Kṛṣṇa-bhaktiratna-prakāśa is completed.**

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhajanopāya-cintāmaṇir ayaṁ granthaḥ \|*  
*prema-bhakti-prado yat tat samāśrayata sattamāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May the devotees take shelter of this work, a cintāmaṇi of the method for worshipping Kṛṣṇa, which bestows prema-bhakti.**

*samāptaś cāyaṁ granthaḥ \|śrī-gurudevāya samarpaṇam astu \| śubham astu \| śakābdaḥ 1606 (lipi-kālo’yam)*

**Перевод:**

**The works is hereby completed. May it be offered to guru. May there be auspiciousness. This work was completed in 1606 śaka era.**

## About Rāghava Gosvāmī

In the Bhakti-ratnakara of Narahari Cakravarti it is said that Raghava Gosvami belonged to a Kulina brahmana family of South India, and he always very merciful to the poor. He wrote several books, including Krishna-bhakti-ratna-prakasa. Rāghava Gosvami was an authority in all sastras. He resided at Govardhana, and when Srinivasa Acarya and Narottama dasa Thakura came to Vrndavana, he guided them on a tour of all the holy places, beginning with Mathura.

In Gaura-gaṇoddeśa-dīpikā, it is said,

*srī-radha-prana-rupā yā srī-campakalata vraje \|*  
*sadya raghava-gosvamī govardhana-kṛta-sthitiḥ \|*

**Campakalāta, who was the life of Rādhā in Vraja, now became Rāghava Gosvāmī, living at Govardhana.**
